### ✅ Pattern Implemented: にて (formal particle for で)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: にて is often considered as its own stand-alone particle, despite originally being a mix of に and the conjunction particle て. When combined, にて becomes a formal case-marking particle that is used in the same way as で. This means it can convey the meaning of "with (A)", "by (A)", "at (A)", or "using (A)".

**Structures tested**: 3 test variants (all from grammar_points_data.json)
1. Noun (location) + にて: 時計にて (chars 4-8) - "at the Golden Clock"
2. Noun (time) + にて: 時にて (chars 6-9) - "at 9:00 a.m."
3. Noun (means) + にて: 電話にて (chars 9-13) - "by phone"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any (noun) + にて (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- Custom matcher created:
  - NiteMatcher: matches にて as case particle with exact surface and base_form
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding noun (location/time/means/method)
- Pattern includes noun + particle construction
- Priority set to 4 (matches feature_list.json)

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)
- test_nite_location: Location noun + にて (時計にて)
- test_nite_time: Time noun + にて (時にて)
- test_nite_means: Means noun + にて (電話にて)

**Tokenization observations**:
- にて: 助詞/格助詞/一般/* (case particle), single token
- NOT split into に + て
- Appears after nouns indicating location, time, means, method, reason, or origin
- Formal/written register - で is more natural in casual conversation

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented nite() function with custom matcher
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added nite_tests module with 3 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Formal particle primarily seen in writing or heard in announcements
- Used to mark location, time, means, method, reason, or origin
- Functional equivalent of で in formal contexts
- Simple single-particle pattern with no conjugation variations

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: には (emphatic に + は)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: には is a common particle combination in Japanese that is used to express contrast in regard to the topic. As a function of the particles that combine to make には, に highlights that something is "for (A)", while は emphasizes that a generalization or contrast is being made about (A). Common translations include "in order to", "in regard to", or "for".

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants (all from grammar_points_data.json)
1. Verb + には: するには (chars 21-25) - "in order to shop"
2. Verb + には: 行くには (chars 3-7) - "in order to go abroad"
3. Noun + には: 子供には (chars 4-8) - "for children"
4. Noun + には: 通勤には (chars 0-4) - "for commuting"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any (verb/noun) + に (助詞/格助詞) + は (助詞/係助詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - NiCaseMatcher: matches に as case particle (格助詞)
  - HaParticleMatcher: matches は as topic marker (係助詞)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding verb (dictionary form) or noun
- Pattern includes full construction from word through は
- Priority set to 4 (matches feature_list.json)

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_niha_verb: Verb する + には (するには)
- test_niha_verb_passport: Verb 行く + には (行くには)
- test_niha_noun: Noun 子供 + には (子供には)
- test_niha_noun_commute: Noun 通勤 + には (通勤には)

**Tokenization observations**:
- には is NOT a single particle - it's two separate tokens:
  - に: 助詞/格助詞/一般/* (case particle)
  - は: 助詞/係助詞/*/* (topic marker)
- Follows verbs in dictionary form (基本形)
- Follows nouns directly
- Used to express purpose or make contrasts/comparisons

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented niha() function with 2 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added niha_tests module with 4 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Despite having several translations, には can almost always be thought of as "for"
- に highlights that (A) is the target
- は shows that whatever happens in (B) is specific to (A)
- Often contrasts with other possible targets or emphasizes uniqueness to (A)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ぬ (classical negative - "not")

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: ぬ is the attributive form of ず, a classical auxiliary verb that is used for negation like ない. It connects to verbs in the same way as ない but is archaic/literary. Often used in set expressions, artistic writing, or to make characters seem classical.

**Structures tested**: 6 test variants
1. Verb[未然形] + ぬ: 通れぬ (chars 8-11) - basic negative "cannot pass"
2. Verb[未然形] + ぬ: 入れぬ (chars 13-16) - imperative "will not put"
3. Verb[未然形] + ぬ + Noun: 知らぬ (chars 7-10) - attributive form modifying noun
4. する → せぬ: せぬ (chars 16-18) - exception for する verb
5. くる → こぬ: こぬ (chars 10-12) - exception for くる verb
6. いる → おらぬ: おらぬ (chars 8-11) - exception using classical おる form

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: ImperfectiveVerbMatcher checks for 動詞 with 未然形 or 未然ヌ接続 conjugation
- NuAuxiliaryMatcher checks for ぬ (助動詞) with base_form ぬ
- Pattern handles regular verbs and special forms (する, くる, おる)
- Tests use realistic classical/literary Japanese sentences from grammar data

**Key learning**: Pattern was already declared in patterns.rs with a stub function. Following the instructions to check for existing stubs prevented duplicate definition errors.

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: としても (even if we assume that)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: としても is used for expressing thoughts about (A), assuming that it were what is being described. Translated as "even if we assume that it's (A)", "assuming that (A)", or "even going off that it were (A)". Speaker is willing to accept (A) as true for argument's sake before giving their thoughts about that hypothetical situation.

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants (all from grammar_points_data.json)
1. Verb + としても: ついたとしても (chars 7-12) - verb auxiliary た + として + も
2. い-Adj + としても: 正しいとしても (chars 11-18) - い-adjective + として + も
3. な-Adj + だ + としても: だとしても (chars 8-13) - auxiliary だ + として + も
4. Noun + だ + としても: だとしても (chars 2-7) - auxiliary だ + として + も

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + として (助詞/格助詞/連語) + も (助詞/係助詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - ToshiteMatcher: matches として as compound case particle
  - MoParticleMatcher: matches も as binding particle (係助詞)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding verb/adjective/noun (potentially with だ)
- Pattern includes full construction from preceding element through も
- Priority set to 4 (matches feature_list.json)

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_toshitemo: Verb た form + としても
- test_i_adjective_toshitemo: い-Adjective + としても
- test_na_adjective_toshitemo: な-Adjective だ + としても
- test_noun_toshitemo: Noun だ + としても

**Tokenization observations**:
- として: 助詞/格助詞/連語 (compound case particle), NOT split into と + し + て
- も: 助詞/係助詞 (binding particle)
- Preceding element varies: verb (with auxiliary た), adjective, noun (with auxiliary だ)
- Pattern confidence: 5.5 (priority 4 + base confidence 1.5)

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented toshitemo() function with 2 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added toshitemo_tests module with 4 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Very similar to としては but with も instead of は particle
- Used for hypothetical/counterfactual assumptions
- Can also mean "even as (A)" when highlighting people/organizations in position of (A)
- Follows verbs, い-adjectives directly; follows nouns/な-adjectives with だ

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: それにしても (even so, nevertheless)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: それにしても is used as an extension of にしても ('even though', 'even if'). Translated as "nevertheless" or "even so". Points out that despite what was said previously, the speaker's primary opinion is the following statement. Highlights that while the speaker accepts/acknowledges the previous statement as true, that is not all there is to be considered.

**Structures tested**: 1 structure variant, 3 test contexts (all from grammar_points_data.json)
1. After comma: それにしても (chars 15-21) - conjunction after が particle
2. Sentence beginning: それにしても (chars 17-23) - conjunction starting new sentence
3. After period: それにしても (chars 26-32) - conjunction after completed thought

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Single token matcher for それにしても as 接続詞 (conjunction)
- Custom matcher created:
  - SorenishitemoMatcher: matches それにしても as conjunction (接続詞)
- Very simple pattern - single token, no surrounding context needed
- Pattern appears at sentence beginnings or after commas/periods
- Priority defaulted to 4 (matches feature_list.json, though was originally set to 1 in patterns.rs)

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)
- test_after_comma: それにしても after が particle and comma
- test_sentence_beginning: それにしても at beginning after period
- test_comparison_context: それにしても expressing exceeded expectations

**Tokenization observations**:
- それにしても: Single token, 接続詞 (conjunction)
- NOT split into individual particles (それ + に + し + て + も)
- Appears standalone - no required context before or after
- Pattern confidence: 3.0 (base confidence, simple pattern)

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented sorenishitemo() function with 1 custom matcher
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added sorenishitemo_tests module with 3 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Used when speaker accepts situation (A) but statement (B) highlights something beyond expectation
- Primarily appears at beginning of sentences or directly after a comma
- Emphasizes contrast between acceptance and additional consideration
- Very straightforward to implement - just match the conjunction token

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: としては (as for, as, from the standpoint of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: Like として (meaning 'as'), としては functions as an extension with the は particle emphasizing the subject as a stand-alone entity that is about to be compared or that the speaker is assuming the result of (B) from the standpoint of (A). May be translated as 'as (A)' or 'for an (A)'. Frequently highlights a person or organization when the speaker is making an assumption from that standpoint.

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants
1. Person + としては: 先輩としては (chars 4-10)
2. Organization + としては: 建築会社としては (chars 7-13) - compound noun
3. Concept + としては: アイデアとしては (chars 0-8)
4. Abstract noun + としては: 趣味としては (chars 4-10)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + として (助詞/格助詞/連語) + は (助詞/係助詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - ToshiteMatcher: matches として as compound case particle
  - HaParticleMatcher: matches は as topic marker (係助詞)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding noun
- Pattern includes full construction from noun through は

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_person_toshiteha: Person (先輩) + としては pattern
- test_organization_toshiteha: Organization (建築会社) + としては pattern
- test_concept_toshiteha: Concept (アイデア) + としては pattern
- test_hobby_toshiteha: Hobby (趣味) + としては pattern

**Tokenization observations**:
- として: 助詞/格助詞/連語 (compound case particle), NOT split into と + し + て
- は: 助詞/係助詞 (topic marker)
- Preceding word: Various nouns (proper, common, compound)
- Pattern range includes preceding noun through は

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented toshiteha() function with 2 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added toshiteha_tests module with 4 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Used to make broad judgments taking into consideration all aspects of (A)
- More frequently used for broad judgments vs にしては (personal opinions)
- Will not be used in regard to oneself (私としては is unnatural in most contexts)
- Usually used after nouns identifying people or organizations

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: 何しろ (at any rate, after all)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: Although a combination of 何 (substitute noun) and しろ (imperative of する), 何しろ is considered a singular adverb meaning "no wonder", "after all", or "at any rate". Used to express that in any circumstance, only one conclusion can accurately be drawn about (A).

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants
1. Sentence start: なにしろ彼はまだ始めたばかり (chars 0-4)
2. Clause start: なにしろ彼はプロなんだから (chars 12-16)
3. With から: なにしろ今日は普段より暑かった (chars 0-4)
4. With ので: なにしろこの商品は不便すぎる (chars 0-4)

**Implementation details**:
- Simple single-token matcher
- Matches なにしろ as 副詞/一般 (general adverb)
- Custom matcher checks: surface, base_form, and POS
- Pattern appears at sentence/clause beginnings

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_nanishiro_sentence_start: なにしろ at sentence start
- test_nanishiro_clause_start: なにしろ at clause start
- test_nanishiro_with_kara: なにしろ + から pattern
- test_nanishiro_with_node: なにしろ + ので pattern

**Tokenization observations**:
- なにしろ: 副詞/一般/*/*, surface=なにしろ, base=なにしろ
- Single token, straightforward detection
- Commonly appears with から or ので for explanations

**Files modified**: 3

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: にせよ・にしろ (even if, no matter if)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: Single form of にせよ/にしろ (not repeated). Combines に + imperative forms of する (せよ/しろ) to mean "even if (A)" or "no matter if (A)". せよ is more formal/written, しろ is more casual/spoken. Used to strongly state that condition (A) is unimportant to the main point.

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants
1. Verb + にせよ: 参加しないにせよ (chars 7-12)
2. い-Adjective + にしろ: 怖いにしろ (chars 0-5)
3. な-Adjective + である + にせよ: 不便であるにせよ (chars 7-12)
4. Noun + にしろ: 子供にしろ (chars 7-12)

**Implementation details**:
- Reuses matcher from にしろ～にしろ pattern
- Structure: Any + に (格助詞) + しろ/せよ (動詞, サ変・スル, 命令形)
- Same tokenization as repeated form, just used singly
- Matcher handles both imperative forms (しろ = 命令ro, せよ = 命令yo)

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_niseyo: Verb + にせよ pattern
- test_i_adj_nishiro: い-Adjective + にしろ pattern
- test_na_adj_niseyo: な-Adjective + である + にせよ pattern
- test_noun_nishiro: Noun + にしろ pattern

**Tokenization observations**:
- に: 助詞/格助詞/一般
- しろ: 動詞/自立, サ変・スル, 命令ro, base=する
- せよ: 動詞/自立, サ変・スル, 命令yo, base=する
- Pattern detected same as にしろ～にしろ (separate name in patterns.rs)

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented niseyo_u30fb_nishiro() by reusing nishiro_uff5e_nishiro()
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added niseyo_nishiro_tests module with 4 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Single form vs repeated form (にしろ～にしろ) are functionally the same structure
- Both patterns detected when either form appears
- Grammar distinction is usage context (single emphasis vs either/or choice)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: にしたら (from the point of view of, from the perspective of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: When に is combined with the hypothetical forms of する (すれば, or したら), it forms a structure used in Japanese for conveying that the speaker is making an opinion about (A), from its point of view. This is frequently translated as 'from the point of view of (A)', or 'from the standpoint of (A)'.

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants
1. Noun + にしたら: お客様にしたら (chars 0-7) - from customer's perspective
2. Noun + にしたら: 人にしたら (chars 6-11) - from person's perspective
3. Noun + にすれば: 親にすれば (chars 12-17) - from parent's perspective (formal)
4. Noun + にすれば: 日本人にすれば (chars 0-7) - from Japanese perspective (formal)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any (Noun) + に (格助詞) + する verb (連用形 or 仮定形) + たら/ば
- Custom matchers created:
  - NiCaseMatcher: matches に as case particle (助詞/格助詞)
  - SuruMatcher: matches する verb in 連用形 (し) or 仮定形 (すれ)
  - TaraBaMatcher: matches たら (助動詞, 仮定形) or ば (接続助詞)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding noun
- Pattern includes full construction from noun through conditional form

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_nishitara_customer: お客様にしたら pattern
- test_nishitara_person: 人にしたら pattern
- test_nisureba_parent: 親にすれば pattern
- test_nisureba_japanese: 日本人にすれば pattern

**Tokenization observations**:
- にしたら: Noun + に (格助詞) + し (動詞, 連用形, base=する) + たら (助動詞, 仮定形, base=た)
- にすれば: Noun + に (格助詞) + すれ (動詞, 仮定形, base=する) + ば (接続助詞)
- Both forms follow same structure with different conjugations

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented nishitara() function with 3 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added nishitara_tests module with 4 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Will not be used in regard to oneself (私にしたら is unnatural)
- にすれば is more formal than にしたら
- Usually used after nouns identifying people or organizations

---

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: にしても～にしても (regardless of whether A or B)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: にしても, a grammar point consisting of に, the て-form of する, and も, is used to convey that 'regardless of (A) or (B), the result will still be (C)'. When repeated as (A) にしても (B) にしても, it expresses that the result is true regardless of either option.

**Structures tested**: 5 test variants
1. Verb + にしても: するにしても + しないにしても (chars 8-12, 16-20)
2. Verb + にしても: 直すにしても + するにしても (chars 6-10, 18-22)
3. い-Adjective + にしても: 深いにしても + 浅いにしても (chars 5-9, 12-16)
4. な-Adjective + にしても: 上手にしても + 下手にしても (chars 6-10, 13-17)
5. Noun + にしても: 平日にしても + 祝日にしても (chars 2-6, 8-12)

**Implementation details**:
- Uses existing にしても pattern from N3 (already implemented)
- Pattern detects each "X にしても" instance separately
- Tests verify that both instances are detected in each sentence
- No new matcher needed - existing implementation handles all forms

**Test count**: 5 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_nishitemo_verb_nishitemo: Verb pattern (affirmative and negative)
- test_verb_nishitemo_rebuild_remodel: Different verbs pattern
- test_i_adj_nishitemo_deep_shallow: い-Adjective pattern
- test_na_adj_nishitemo_good_bad: な-Adjective pattern
- test_noun_nishitemo_weekday_holiday: Noun pattern

**Tokenization observations**:
- Each にしても is detected as a separate pattern instance
- Pattern name is "にしても" (not "にしても～にしても")
- Tests verify exactly 2 instances are detected per sentence
- Works with verbs, い-adjectives, な-adjectives, and nouns

**Files modified**: 2
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added nishitemo_uff5e_nishitemo_tests module with 5 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Pattern already implemented in N3, tests added for N2 repeated form
- Each instance detected separately, not as a single "A にしても B にしても" unit
- Completely interchangeable with にしろ～にしろ and にせよ～にせよ
- にしろ and にせよ are more direct, less formal

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: にしろ～にしろ (whether... or...)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: にせよ and にしろ are two phrases that are often translated as 'even if (A)', or 'no matter if (A)'. This structure is a combination of に, and either of the imperative forms of する; せよ, or しろ. When repeated, にせよ～にせよ illustrates that regardless of either '(A) or (B), (C) is still true'.

**Structures tested**: 5 test variants
1. Verb + にせよ: 参加するにせよ (chars 7-14) - from "参加するにせよしないにせよ"
2. い-Adjective + にしろ: 少ないにしろ (chars 8-14) - from "多いにしろ少ないにしろ"
3. な-Adjective + にせよ: 好きにせよ (chars 3-8) - from "好きにせよ嫌いにせよ"
4. Noun + にしろ: アルバイトにしろ (chars 5-13) - from "社長にしろアルバイトにしろ"
5. い-Adj + Noun + にしろ: 川にしろ (chars 2-6) - from "浅い川にしろ深い川にしろ"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + に (格助詞) + しろ/せよ (動詞, 命令形)
- Custom matchers created:
  - NiCaseMatcher: matches に as case particle (助詞/格助詞)
  - ShiroSeyoMatcher: matches しろ/せよ as imperative form of する (命令ro or 命令yo)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles the word before に (verb, adjective, or noun)
- Pattern includes one token before に through the imperative form

**Test count**: 5 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_nishiro_verb_nishiro: Verb + にせよ pattern
- test_i_adj_nishiro_i_adj_nishiro: い-Adjective + にしろ pattern
- test_na_adj_niseyo_na_adj_niseyo: な-Adjective + にせよ pattern
- test_noun_nishiro_noun_nishiro: Noun + にしろ pattern
- test_antonym_pair_nishiro: Antonym pairs with にしろ

**Tokenization observations**:
- に: 助詞/格助詞/一般
- しろ: 動詞/自立, サ変・スル, 命令ro, base=する
- せよ: 動詞/自立, サ変・スル, 命令yo, base=する
- Preceding word can be verb (基本形), adjective (基本形), or noun
- Pattern detects each "X にしろ/にせよ" separately, not the full "A にしろ B にしろ" structure

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented nishiro_uff5e_nishiro() function
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added nishiro_uff5e_nishiro_tests module with 5 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Pattern matches each instance separately - in "A にしろ B にしろ", two patterns are detected
- Both にしろ and にせよ are supported (imperative forms of する)
- な-Adjectives and nouns directly attach to に without である in actual usage
- Often used with antonym pairs to mean "whether A or B or anything in between"

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: に応じて (in accordance with, depending on)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: に応じて is a combination of に, and the て-form of the る-Verb 応じる 'to apply to'. This grammar structure is often translated as 'depending on (A), (B)', or 'in response to (A), (B)'. In almost all cases, it indicates that (B) is something done in a way that is 'applicable' to what is necessary of (A).

**Structures tested**: 3 test variants
1. Noun + に応じて: レベルに応じて (chars 15-22) - adverbial form
2. Noun + に応じて: 面積に応じて (chars 9-15) - adverbial form
3. Noun + に応じた + Noun: 要望に応じた (chars 11-17) - noun-modifying form

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Noun + に (格助詞) + 応じ (動詞, 連用形, base=応じる) + て/た
- Custom matchers created:
  - NiCaseMatcher: matches に as case particle (助詞/格助詞)
  - OujiMatcher: matches 応じ verb in 連用形 (base=応じる)
  - TeTaMatcher: matches て (助詞) or た (助動詞)
- Handles both て-form (に応じて) and た-form (に応じた)
- Pattern includes full construction from noun through て/た

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)
- test_nioujite_adverbial: レベルに応じてクラスを分ける
- test_nioujite_price: 面積に応じて値段が高くなります
- test_nioujita_noun: 要望に応じたデザインを提供

**Tokenization observations**:
- に: 助詞/格助詞/一般
- 応じ: 動詞/自立, 一段, 連用形, base=応じる
- て: 助詞/接続助詞
- た: 助動詞, 特殊・タ, 基本形

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented nioujite() function with 3 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added nioujite_tests module with 3 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- 応じる usually indicates the way something 'responds to' or 'answers' an issue on a case-by-case basis
- What happens in (B) frequently indicates something that changes depending on intensity/severity of (A)

---

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: を通じて・を通して (through, via)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: を通じて and を通して are two grammar patterns with similar meanings, primarily translating as 'through (A), (B)', or 'via (A), (B)'. 通じる 'to flow/go through' (intransitive) vs 通す 'to make a path' (transitive). を通じて is more often used for information/communication, while を通して refers more to direct locations or intentional actions.

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants
1. Noun + を通して: 留学を通して (chars 2-8) - language study
2. Noun + を通して: 仕事を通して (chars 0-6) - work
3. Noun + を通じて: ニュースを通じて (chars 8-16) - news
4. Noun + を通じて: アニメを通じて (chars 2-9) - anime

**Implementation details**:
- Tokenization: Both を通して and を通じて tokenize as SINGLE compound particles (助詞/格助詞/連語)
- Matcher structure: Noun + を通じて|を通して (compound case particle)
- Custom matcher created:
  - WotsuujiteWotooshiteMatcher: matches both を通じて and を通して as compound case particles (助詞/格助詞/連語)
- Simple two-token pattern: Any + compound particle

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_wotooshite_language_study: 語学留学を通して日本の文化などを学びました
- test_wotooshite_work: 仕事を通して今の妻と出会いました
- test_wotsuujite_news: ネットニュースを通じて知りました
- test_wotsuujite_anime: アニメを通じて日本語を学んだ

**Tokenization observations**:
- を通じて: 助詞/格助詞/連語, surface=を通じて, base=を通じて
- を通して: 助詞/格助詞/連語, surface=を通して, base=を通して
- Both tokenize as single compound particles (not split into を + 通じて/通して)

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented wotsuujite_u30fb_wotooshite() function with 1 custom matcher
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added wotsuujite_wotooshite_tests module with 4 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- を通じて: More common for "throughout (A)" (intransitive, continuous)
- を通して: More common for intentional use "by use of (A)" (transitive, one-time action)
- Both forms tokenize as single compound particles, making implementation straightforward

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: ならともかく (if it's A, sure, but...)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: When なら, the hypothetical form of the auxiliary verb だ is paired with the adverb ともかく 'to set aside', it will often be translated as 'if it is (A), sure, but not in the case of (B)', or more simply 'putting (A) aside, (B)'. This structure usually indicates that if it were (A), then the result or situation would be different, but because it is not (A), the result or situation is not different (it is as it currently is).

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants
1. Verb + ならともかく: ものならともかく (chars 9-17)
2. い-Adjective + ならともかく: 高いならともかく (chars 3-11)
3. な-Adjective + ならともかく: 丁寧ならともかく (chars 3-11)
4. Noun + ならともかく: 妹ならともかく (chars 2-9)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + なら (助動詞, 仮定形) + ともかく (副詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - NaraMatcher: matches なら as auxiliary verb (助動詞) in hypothetical form (仮定形), base=だ
  - TomokakuMatcher: matches ともかく as general adverb (副詞/一般)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding word (verb, adjective, noun)
- Pattern includes one token before なら through ともかく

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_naratomokaku: Verb + ならともかく (nominalized with もの)
- test_i_adj_naratomokaku: い-Adjective + ならともかく
- test_na_adj_naratomokaku: な-Adjective + ならともかく
- test_noun_naratomokaku: Noun + ならともかく

**Tokenization observations**:
- なら: Always 助動詞, base=だ, 仮定形 (hypothetical form)
- ともかく: Always 副詞/一般 (general adverb)
- Preceding word can be verb, adjective, or noun in various forms
- Pattern range includes full construction from preceding word through ともかく

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented naratomokaku() function with 2 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added naratomokaku_tests module with 4 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- More formal than だけど or けど for contrasts
- Often used when (A) would be acceptable but (B) is not
- Primarily follows nouns in actual usage, though grammar allows verbs/adjectives

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: はともかく (setting aside, apart from)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: はともかく is an expression which makes use of the adverbial-particle は, and the adverb 兎（と）も角（かく） meaning 'to set aside', or 'at any rate'. This structure is used when the speaker wants to change the topic from (A) to (B), or mention (B) as being something additional on top of (A). In many cases, (A) is something the speaker thinks is not worth considering when (B) is more important.

**Structures tested**: 3 test variants
1. Noun + は + ともかく: 給料はともかく (chars 0-7)
2. Phrase + か + は + ともかく: かはともかく (chars 8-14) - from "高いか安いかはともかく"
3. Noun phrase + は + ともかく: 見た目はともかく (chars 5-13)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + は (係助詞) + ともかく (副詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - WaKakariMatcher: matches は as binding particle (助詞/係助詞)
  - TomokakuMatcher: matches ともかく as general adverb (副詞/一般) - same as in ならともかく
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding word (noun, particle, etc.)
- Pattern includes one token before は through ともかく

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)
- test_noun_hatomokaku: Simple noun + は + ともかく
- test_phrase_hatomokaku: Phrase ending with か + は + ともかく
- test_appearance_hatomokaku: Noun phrase + は + ともかく

**Tokenization observations**:
- は: Always 助詞/係助詞 (binding particle)
- ともかく: Always 副詞/一般 (general adverb)
- Preceding word can be noun, particle (like か), or other elements
- Pattern detection captures only the final token + は + ともかく

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented hatomokaku() function with 2 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added hatomokaku_tests module with 3 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Related to ならともかく but uses は instead of なら
- More about setting aside topics rather than hypothetical contrasts
- Etymology: 兎角亀毛 (horns on rabbits, fur on turtles) - things that don't exist
- Can be written in kanji as 兎も角 but commonly seen in hiragana

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: 一旦 (once, for a moment)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: 一旦（いったん） is an adverb meaning "for a short time" or "briefly", used to indicate "once (A), (B)". This pattern appears with hypothetical structures such as ば, と, or たら, and usually appears with a negated verb in the (B) part of a sentence. 一旦-based sentences indicate that returning to the previous state will be difficult, at least for a short time. Other common translations include "for the time being" and "for now".

**Implementation details**:

- Checks surface, base_form, and POS for accuracy
- Pattern appears at beginning of sentences or before conditional verbs

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: はもとより (not only... but also, let alone)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: は元（もと）より, commonly seen in hiragana as はもとより, is a formal pattern indicating "not only (A), but also (B)" or "let alone (A), (B)". It combines は + the noun 元 (origin) + より, highlighting that "even compared to the original fact of (A), (B)". Often (A) is something considered obvious or the most well-known information. May appear after nouns or verb phrases nominalized with の or こと.

**Structures tested**: 3 test variants

3. Verb + の + はもとより: のはもとより (chars 10-16)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + は (係助詞) + もとより (副詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - WaKakariMatcher: matches は as binding particle (助詞/係助詞)
  - MotoyoriMatcher: matches もとより as general adverb (副詞/一般)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding word (noun, の, こと)
- Pattern includes full construction from noun/nominalizer through もとより

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)

**Tokenization observations**:
- は: Always 助詞/係助詞 (binding particle)
- もとより: Always 副詞/一般 (general adverb)
- Preceding word can be noun, の (名詞/非自立), or こと (名詞/非自立)

**Notes**:

- Has alternate meaning: "from the start, (A) is (B)" depending on context

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: だけのことはある (no wonder, as expected)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: だけのことはある is a grammar structure consisting of the particle だけ, の, こと, は, and the う-Verb ある. It is used when indicating that something about (A) is 'as expected', or 'not surprising'. The pattern expresses that simply because (A) is (A), (B) is to be expected. It may appear following verbs, い-Adjectives, な-Adjectives or nouns. な will be required after な-Adjectives. Often appears with other words that add emphasis to the expectation of (A), such as さすが, やはり, or やっぱり.

**Structures tested**: 6 test variants
1. Verb + だけのことはある: 行っていただけのことはある (chars 29-38)
2. い-Adjective + だけのことはある: 若いだけのことはある (chars 27-37)
3. Noun + な + だけのことはある: 有名なだけのことはある (chars 25-34)
4. Noun + だけのことはある: 先生だけのことはある (chars 25-35)
5. Noun + だった + だけのことはある: ボクサーだっただけのことはある (chars 24-33)
6. な-Adjective + だった + だけのことはある: 正直だっただけのことはある (chars 27-36)

**Implementation details**:
- Core pattern: だけ (副助詞) + の (連体化) + こと (名詞/非自立) + は (係助詞) + ある (動詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - DakeMatcher: matches だけ as adverbial particle (助詞/副助詞)
  - NoRentaikaMatcher: matches の as nominalizer (助詞/連体化)
  - KotoMatcher: matches こと as non-independent noun (名詞/非自立)
  - WaKakariMatcher: matches は as binding particle (助詞/係助詞)
  - AruVerbMatcher: matches ある verb (基本形 or 連用形 for polite)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding word (verb, adjective, noun)
- Works for both standard (ある) and polite (あります) forms

**Test count**: 6 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_dakeno: Verb + だけのことはある
- test_i_adjective_dakeno: い-Adjective + だけのことはある
- test_noun_dakeno: な-Adjective (treated as noun) + な + だけのことはある
- test_na_adjective_dakeno: Noun + だけのことはある
- test_noun_datta_dakeno: Noun + だった + だけのことはある
- test_na_adjective_datta_dakeno: な-Adjective + だった + だけのことはある

**Tokenization observations**:
- だけ: Always 助詞/副助詞
- の: Always 助詞/連体化
- こと: Always 名詞/非自立/一般
- は: Always 助詞/係助詞
- ある: 動詞/自立 (基本形 or 連用形)
- Pattern includes one token before だけ (captured by TokenMatcher::Any)

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented dakenokotohaaru() function with 5 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added dakenokotohaaru_tests module with 6 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Only used for positive evaluations, not negative expectations
- Expresses that result is as expected from the quality/nature of (A)
- More formal than alternatives

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: やら～やら (A and B, and so on)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: やら is an adverbial particle that expresses lack of determination or limitation in regard to (A). When repeated as (A) やら (B) やら, it translates as '(A) and so on', or '(A) and the like'. May appear following verbs in their dictionary form, nouns, い-Adjectives, or な-Adjectives. Both (A) and (B) will often be the same type of word. Compared to とか, やら puts more emphasis on the scope of things, making (A) and (B) seem like only a small fraction of many different things within a complex pool.

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants
1. Verb[る] + やら + Verb[る] + やら: 世話をするやら家事をするやら (chars 15-20)
2. い-Adjective + やら + い-Adjective + やら: 痛いやら悪いやら (chars 15-20)
3. Noun + やら + Noun + やら: 不安やら怒りやら (chars 12-16)
4. Noun + やら + Noun + やら: 漢字やら単語やら (chars 8-12)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + やら
- Custom matcher created:
  - YaraMatcher: matches やら as 並立助詞 OR 終助詞 (both forms occur)
- Pattern detects each instance of "X + やら" separately
- Multiple instances detected per sentence (typical usage is A やら B やら)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding word (verb, adjective, noun)

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_yara_verb_yara: Verb + やら pattern
- test_i_adj_yara_i_adj_yara: い-Adjective + やら pattern
- test_noun_yara_noun_yara: Noun + やら pattern
- test_mixed_noun_yara: Another noun + やら example

**Tokenization observations**:
- やら can be: 助詞/並立助詞 OR 助詞/終助詞
- First やら often: 並立助詞
- Second やら may be: 並立助詞 OR 終助詞 (varies by context)
- Pattern matches each instance independently
- May include following particles (e.g., で) in some detections

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented yara_uff5e_yara() function with YaraMatcher
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added yara_yara_tests module with 4 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Pattern matches each occurrence separately, not the full A やら B やら structure
- More formal than とか, emphasizes breadth of category
- Can also appear as のやら～のやら to express uncertainty between options

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: 未だに (still, even now)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: 未だに (いまだに) is a formal adverb meaning "still" or "even now", typically emphasizing that something has continued for a longer period than まだ (still). It appears at the beginning of sentences or clauses to indicate an ongoing state that is expected to have changed. Compared to まだ, 未だに is more formal and implies a much longer continuation, similar to adding ずっと to a sentence. It can be used with both affirmative verbs (still doing) and negative verbs (still not).

**Structures tested**: 2 test variants
1. 未だに + Verb[る]: 私が子供の頃に巻き込まれた事故のことはいまだに覚えている (chars 19-23) - "still remember"
2. 未だに + Verb[ない]: 部長は人事に注意されて２ヶ月も経つのに、彼の態度はいまだに変わらない (chars 25-29) - "still hasn't changed"

**Implementation details**:
- Tokenization: Can be EITHER:
  - Single token: いまだに (副詞/一般)
  - Two tokens: いまだ (副詞/助詞類接続) + に (助詞/副詞化)
- Custom matchers created:
  - ImadaOrImadaniMatcher: matches いまだ or いまだに as adverb
  - NiFukushikaMatcher: matches に as adverbializer (助詞/副詞化) - optional
- Uses Optional wrapper for に to handle both tokenization variants
- Single flexible matcher handles both cases

**Test count**: 2 tests (all passing)
- test_imadani_affirmative: いまだに覚えている (split tokenization: いまだ + に)
- test_imadani_negative: いまだに変わらない (single token: いまだに)

**Tokenization observations**:
- Split: いまだ (副詞/助詞類接続) + に (助詞/副詞化)
- Single: いまだに (副詞/一般)
- Kagome uses DIFFERENT tokenizations depending on context
- Pattern matcher handles both via Optional(に)

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented imadani() function with 2 custom matchers and Optional
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added imadani_tests module with 2 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- More formal than まだ
- Implies longer time period of continuation
- Written with kanji as 未だに but commonly appears in hiragana

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: をもとに (based on)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: をもとに is an expression meaning "based on (A)", "from (A)", or "according to (A)". It combines を (object marker) + もと (origin/base) + に (direction marker), with a literal meaning of "with (A) as the origin". It's used primarily to reference a source from which something is created or started. The pattern can optionally be followed by する in various forms (して, した) to form をもとにして or をもとにした. Different from に基づいて which emphasizes judgement/conclusion foundation, をもとに focuses on inspiration or enabling source.

**Structures tested**: 3 test variants
1. Noun + をもとに: このドラマは実際に起こったことをもとに作られたそうだ (chars 15-19) - "based on actual events"
2. Noun + をもとにして: 研究の結果をもとにしてレポートを書いてください (chars 5-9) - "based on research results" (with して)
3. Noun + をもとにした: このデータをもとにした分析を提出します (chars 5-9) - "analysis based on this data" (with した)

**Implementation details**:
- Core pattern: を (格助詞) + もと (名詞) + に (格助詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - WoCaseMatcher: matches を as case particle (助詞/格助詞)
  - MotoNounMatcher: matches もと as noun (名詞/一般)
  - NiCaseMatcher: matches に as case particle (助詞/格助詞)
- Pattern detects core をもとに, with optional following する forms treated separately
- TokenMatcher sequence: を + もと + に

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)
- test_womotoni_basic: をもとに (basic form)
- test_womotoni_with_shite: をもとにして (with して)
- test_womotoni_shita_noun: をもとにした (with した + noun)

**Tokenization observations**:
- を: Always 助詞/格助詞/一般 (case particle)
- もと: Always 名詞/一般 (general noun)
- に: Always 助詞/格助詞/一般 (case particle)
- Optional following: し (する, 連用形) + て/た
- Core pattern matches to を...に, excluding optional following particles

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented womotoni() function with 3 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added womotoni_tests module with 3 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Can be written with kanji as を元に or を基に or を下に (different nuances)
- Most common form: を元に (origin/beginning)
- Often seen in hiragana: をもとに
- Different from に基づいて (more about judgement/conclusion)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: にかけては (when it comes to, regarding)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: にかけては is used following nouns to express "when it comes to (A), (B)" where (B) is the best or has a distinct advantage in field (A). It combines に + かけて (te-form of 掛ける "to hang/suspend") + は. This pattern always expresses a positive aspect - it's unnatural to use when highlighting negative aspects.

**Structures tested**: 3 test variants
1. Noun + にかけては: 技術にかけては彼が一番だ (chars 3-10) - "when it comes to skills"
2. Noun + にかけては: スポーツにかけては誰にも負けない (chars 12-21) - "when it comes to sports"
3. Noun + にかけては: 機能にかけては他のスマホとは比べ物にならない (chars 6-13) - "when it comes to features"

**Implementation details**:
- Tokenization: にかけて (助詞/格助詞/連語) + は (助詞/係助詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - NikaketeMatcher: matches にかけて as compound case particle (助詞/格助詞/連語)
  - WaKakariMatcher: matches は as binding particle (助詞/係助詞)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles preceding nouns
- Pattern includes: Noun + にかけては (full construction)

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)
- test_nikaketeha_skill: 大工の技術にかけては彼が一番だ
- test_nikaketeha_sports: スポーツにかけては誰にも負けない
- test_nikaketeha_features: 機能にかけては他のスマホとは比べ物にならない

**Tokenization observations**:
- にかけて: Always 助詞/格助詞/連語 (compound case particle)
- は: Always 助詞/係助詞 (binding particle)
- Kagome tokenizes にかけては as TWO tokens (not three)

- Different from にかけて (time range: "from X to Y")
- Formal register, commonly used in writing and speeches

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: とっくに (long ago, already, ages ago)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: とっくに is an adverb meaning "long ago" or "already" with emphasis. It's an emphasized version of とうに (a literary form not commonly used in modern Japanese). The pattern appears at the beginning of sentences or before verbs to indicate something happened much earlier than expected, or used for exaggeration. Compared to すでに and もう, とっくに indicates something happened much further in the past or adds exaggeration.

**Structures tested**: 3 test variants
1. とっくに + Phrase (past): とっくに食べちゃったよ (chars 0-4) - "already ate"
2. とっくに + Phrase (deadline): 提出期限はとっくに過ぎている (chars 5-9) - "long since passed"
3. とっくに + Phrase (with もう): もうとっくに帰ってる (chars 10-14) - "already gone ages ago"

**Implementation details**:
- Tokenization: とっくに (副詞/一般) - single token adverb
- Custom matcher created:
  - TokkuniMatcher: matches とっくに as general adverb (副詞/一般)
- Very simple pattern - single token match with POS check
- Checks surface, base_form, and POS for accuracy

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)

**Tokenization observations**:

- surface=とっくに, base_form=とっくに
- Single token, very straightforward detection

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: てはならない (must not do)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: てはならない is a formal prohibition expression meaning "must not do" or "cannot do". It combines the conjunction particle て/で, は, and the negated form of なる. This is the most formal of the prohibition patterns (てはだめ < てはいけない < てはならない), commonly used in writing, rules, and formal speech. It indicates that something is not permitted or not possible.

**Structures tested**: 3 test variants covering both standard and polite forms
1. Verb[て] + はならない: 泳いではならない (chars 5-13) - "must not swim"
2. Verb[て] + はなりません: 運転してはなりません (chars 6-16) - "must not drive" (polite)
3. Verb[て] + はならない: 入ってはならない (chars 12-20) - "must not enter" (formal writing)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: flexible_verb_form() + て/で(接続助詞) + は(係助詞) + なる(動詞/非自立, 未然形/連用形) + Any
- Custom matchers created:
  - TeDeParticleMatcher: matches て/で as conjunction particle (助詞/接続助詞)
  - WaKakariMatcher: matches は as binding particle (助詞/係助詞)
  - NaruFormMatcher: matches なる in 未然形 (for ならない) or 連用形 (for なりません)
- TokenMatcher::Any at end handles ない (助動詞) or ませ + ん
- Single flexible matcher handles both standard and polite forms

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_te_hanaranaい_standard (泳いではならない)
- test_verb_te_hanaranasen_polite (運転してはなりません)
- test_verb_te_hanaranaい_formal_writing (入ってはならない)

**Tokenization observations**:
- て/で: Always 助詞/接続助詞
- は: Always 助詞/係助詞
- なら: 動詞/非自立, base=なる, 未然形 (for ならない)
- なり: 動詞/非自立, base=なる, 連用形 (for なりません)
- ない: 助動詞 (after なら)
- ませ/ん: 助動詞 (after なり)

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented tehanaranai() function with 3 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added tehanaranai_tests module with 3 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Most formal prohibition pattern in Japanese
- Commonly used in rules, regulations, and formal writing
- Less common in casual conversation (use てはいけない or てはだめ instead)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: やがて (before long, eventually, soon)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: やがて is a formal adverb meaning "before long", "eventually", or "soon". It appears at the beginning of sentences or clauses to indicate that some particular state that does not currently exist will happen within an unspecified (usually short) amount of time. The word originally just expressed the existence of something which has not currently taken place, and thus had many meanings like "namely", "immediately", "in a moment". Today it primarily means "eventually" or "before long". Usually written in hiragana, though kanji 軈て exists.

**Structures tested**: 3 test variants covering different usage contexts
1. やがて + Phrase: やがて人気がなくなる (chars 15-18) - "will eventually lose popularity"
2. やがて + Phrase (with condition): やがて小説を読める (chars 16-19) - "eventually be able to read novels"
3. やがて + Phrase (near future): やがて日が沈み始める (chars 8-11) - "the sun will set soon"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: やがて (副詞/一般)
- Custom matcher created:
  - YagateMatcher: matches やがて as general adverb (副詞/一般)
- Very simple single-token pattern
- Checks surface, base_form, and POS for accuracy

- test_yagate_immediate_future (near future)

- やがて: Always 副詞/一般 (general adverb)
- surface=やがて, base_form=やがて
- Single token, very straightforward detection

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: 甲斐がある (worth doing, pays off)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: 甲斐がある is used following verbs or nouns to show that (A) was or will be worth doing. The pattern translates as "it is worth doing (A)" or "(A) will pay off". The noun 甲斐 (かい, worth/effect) appears in hiragana and can be either かい or がい depending on what precedes it. After verbs, がい follows the verb stem (ます-stem), while かい can follow various verb forms (past た, て+いる). With nouns, の precedes かい. The pattern can be negative (かいがない/がいがない) to indicate something is "not worth" doing.

**Structures tested**: 5 test variants covering all main forms
1. Verb[た] + かい + がある: 並んだかいがあった (chars 18-25) - "was worth standing in line"
2. Verb[ている] + かい + がある: いるかいがある (chars 13-20) - "worth living"
3. Verb[stem] + がい + がない: 教えがいがない (chars 17-24) - "not worth teaching"
4. Noun + の + かい + がある: のかいがあり (chars 6-12) - "paid off"
5. Verb[た] + かい + がない: たかいがない (chars 14-20) - "not worth coming"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + かい/がい(名詞) + が(格助詞) + ある/ない
- Custom matchers created:
  - KaiGaiMatcher: matches both かい (名詞/非自立) and がい (名詞/接尾)
  - GaMatcher: matches が as case particle (助詞/格助詞)
  - AruNaiMatcher: matches ある (動詞/自立) or ない (形容詞/自立)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles verbs (past/stem/て-form) and nouns before かい/がい
- Single flexible matcher handles both affirmative and negative forms
- Handles both かい and がい variants seamlessly

**Tokenization observations**:
- かい: Always 名詞/非自立/一般 (non-independent noun)
- がい: Always 名詞/接尾/一般 (suffix noun) - follows verb stems
- が: Always 助詞/格助詞/一般 (case particle)
- ある: 動詞/自立/五段・ラ行 (verb)
- ない: 形容詞/自立 (adjective, not auxiliary verb)
- TokenMatcher::Any successfully matches various verb forms and nouns

**Test sentences used**:
- "この食パンを買えて、朝の４時から並んだかいがあった" - past tense, affirmative
- "こういう景色を見ると生きているかいがあると思う" - て-form + いる, affirmative
- "彼女は何回注意しても上達しないから教えがいがない" - verb stem + がい, negative
- "みんなの努力のかいがあり、この大会で優勝する事ができました" - noun + の + かい
- "こんなもんのために東京からきたかいがない" - past tense, negative

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented kaigaaru() function with 3 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added kaigaaru_tests module with 5 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- かい is used primarily after past tense (た) or て-form verbs
- がい is used after verb stems (ます-stem) - emphasizes "always being worth (A)"
- The pattern conveys that a positive result will come from something difficult or unpleasant
- Can be written with kanji as 甲斐 but commonly appears in hiragana

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: に先立ち (prior to, before)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: に先立ち is a combination of に and the う-Verb 先立つ 'to take the lead' or 'to precede'. This structure connects to both nouns and verbs in their dictionary forms to indicate that (B) is a preliminary step required for (A). It may be interpreted as 'before (A), (B)', 'prior to (A), (B)', or 'in preference over (A), (B)'. The verb 先立つ may appear in different forms: 先立って (て-form), 先立ち (formal conjunctive form), or 先立つ (dictionary form when followed by another noun).

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants covering all main structure types
1. Verb[る] + に先立って: 導入するに先立って (chars 6-15) - "prior to installing"
2. Noun + に先立って: 会議に先立って (chars 0-7) - "prior to the meeting"
3. Noun + に先立ち: 公開に先立ち (chars 3-9) - "prior to release" (formal conjunctive)
4. Noun + に先立つ + Noun: 引越しに先立つ (chars 0-7) - "prior to moving"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + に(格助詞) + 先立つ(動詞, base=先立つ) + Optional(て)
- Custom matchers created:
  - NiMatcher: matches に as case particle (助詞/格助詞)
  - SakidatsuMatcher: matches 先立つ verb (動詞/自立, base_form=先立つ)
  - TeMatcher: matches て particle (助詞/接続助詞) - optional
- TokenMatcher::Any handles verbs (基本形) and nouns before に
- Optional て handles both て-form (先立って) and conjunctive form (先立ち)
- Dictionary form (先立つ) matches when て is absent

**Tokenization observations**:
- に: Always 助詞/格助詞/一般 (case particle)
- 先立つ verb appears in three forms:
  - 先立って: 連用タ接続 + て (助詞/接続助詞)
  - 先立ち: 連用形 (formal conjunctive)
  - 先立つ: 基本形 (dictionary form)
- TokenMatcher::Any successfully matches verbs (基本形) and nouns
- All forms have base_form=先立つ

**Test sentences used**:
- "新しい機械を導入するに先立って、色々な会社のサイトを見てスペックを比較します" - verb example
- "会議に先立って、資料を確認しておいてください" - noun with て-form
- "映画の公開に先立ち、予告編が公開された" - noun with 連用形
- "引越しに先立つ１日前に洗濯機や冷蔵庫の水抜きをしておかなければならない" - dictionary form + noun

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented nisakidachi() function with 3 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added nisakidachi_tests module with 4 tests
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 4
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- This is a formal pattern used in writing and formal speech
- The literal meaning 'standing before (A)' highlights the importance of (B) as a first step
- 先立つ may be paired with 物 in the expression 先立つものがない ('not have the means to')

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: はたして (I wonder if, as expected)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: はたして is an adverb (副詞) derived from the verb 果たす (to fulfill, to come to fruition) in its て-form. It is used adverbially to strengthen phrases of speculation or to indicate expected results. When used with speculative grammar patterns like だろうか, でしょうか, かな, or かしら, it translates as "I wonder if" or "really." When used without speculation markers in statements, it means "as expected" or "sure enough." The pattern emphasizes the speaker's thought process about what will or has happened "in the end."

**Structures tested**: 2 structure variants (speculation and expected result)
1. はたして + Question phrase: はたして...だろうか (I wonder if) - chars 0-4
2. はたして + Question phrase: はたして...でしょうか (I wonder if) - chars 0-4  
3. はたして + Statement: After cause → はたして (as expected) → result - chars 20-24
4. はたして + Statement: Midsentence → はたして (as expected) - chars 22-26

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: はたして (副詞/一般)
- Custom matcher created:
  - HatashiteMatcher: matches はたして as adverb (副詞/一般)
- Very simple pattern - single token match with POS check
- Appears adverbially, primarily at the beginning of sentences

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_hatashite_speculation (はたして + だろうか)
- test_hatashite_question (はたして + でしょうか)
- test_hatashite_expected_result (as expected - sentence start)
- test_hatashite_midsentence (as expected - midsentence)

**Tokenization observations**:
- はたして: Always 副詞/一般 (adverb/general)
- surface=はたして, base_form=はたして
- Single token, very straightforward detection

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs: Implemented hatashite() function with HatashiteMatcher
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added hatashite_tests module with 4 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Can be written with kanji as 果たして
- With speculation: emphasizes wondering/doubting
- Without speculation: emphasizes that result was expected/predictable
- Pattern is derived from meaning "to come to fruition" → "in the end"

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: したがって (therefore, accordingly, as a result)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: したがって is a formal conjunction (接続詞) that connects a cause with its logical result. It appears at the beginning of the result clause after stating a cause. The pattern translates as "therefore," "accordingly," or "as a result." It is derived from the verb 従う (したがう, to comply/follow) in its て-form, emphasizing that the result logically follows from the cause. This pattern is only used for objective logical conclusions, not subjective opinions.

**Structures tested**: 1 structure variant (formal conjunction)
1. (Cause) + したがって + (Result): Construction work → したがって (therefore) → closed (chars 23-28)
2. (Cause) + したがって + (Result): More typhoons → したがって (therefore) → higher rice prices (chars 15-20)
3. (Cause) + したがって + (Result): Flu spreading → したがって (therefore) → online meetings (chars 19-24)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: したがって (接続詞)
- Custom matcher created:
  - ShitagatteMatcher: matches したがって as conjunction (接続詞)
- Very simple pattern - single token match with POS check
- Appears between two sentences (cause and result)

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)
- test_shitagatte_basic (Construction work example)
- test_shitagatte_comparison (Typhoon and rice prices example)
- test_shitagatte_decision (Flu and meetings example)

**Tokenization observations**:
- したがって: Always 接続詞 (conjunction) with no subcategories
- surface=したがって, base_form=したがって
- Single token, very straightforward detection

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ともなると・にもなると (when it comes to, once)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: となると is a strong expression in Japanese that focuses on highlighting that once some relatively high threshold is reached, (B) will occur. The base structure is と + なる + と, with variations using も for emphasis and なれば as an alternative to なると. Standard translations are 'once (A), (B)' and 'when (A), (B)'. A more literal translation can be thought of as 'once (A) happens, it is sure to follow that (B)'.

The other forms are ともなると (with も for emphasis), となれば (using ば conditional), and ともなれば. The particle も adds slight emphasis, while ば and と are used interchangeably in this specific pattern.

**Important distinction**: This pattern uses と (not に) to emphasize that the outcome is unavoidable/expected. になると is a different construction that simply indicates transition without this nuance.

**Structures tested**: All 6 test variants covering the main forms
1. Noun + ともなると (with も): ゴールデンウィークともなると (chars 0-14)
2. Verb[る] + ともなると (with も): 行くともなると (chars 4-11)
3. Noun + となると (without も): 代となると (chars 2-7)
4. Noun + にもなると (with に): 人にもなると (chars 2-8)
5. Noun + ともなれば (with なれば): 人ともなれば (chars 2-8)
6. Verb[る] + ともなれば: 作成するともなれば (chars 10-19)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + と/に(格助詞) + Optional(も) + なる/なれ(動詞) + と/ば(接続助詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - ToNiMatcher: matches と or に as case particle (助詞/格助詞)
  - MoKakariMatcher: matches も as係助詞 (optional)
  - NaruNareMatcher: matches なる (基本形) or なれ (仮定形) with base_form=なる
  - ToBaMatcher: matches と or ば as conjunction particle (助詞/接続助詞)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles nouns, verbs, and other preceding elements
- Single flexible matcher handles all structural variations

**Test count**: 6 tests (all passing)
- test_noun_tomonaruto (Noun + ともなると)
- test_verb_tomonaruto (Verb[る] + ともなると)
- test_noun_tonaruto (Noun + となると, no も)
- test_noun_nimonaruto (Noun + にもなると)
- test_noun_tomonareba (Noun + ともなれば)
- test_verb_tomonareba (Verb[る] + ともなれば)

**Tokenization observations**:
- と (first particle): 助詞/格助詞/引用 (quotation particle)
- に (first particle): 助詞/格助詞/一般 (general case particle)
- も: 助詞/係助詞 (binding/topic particle)
- なる: 動詞/自立, base=なる, features[5]=基本形
- なれ: 動詞/自立, base=なる, features[5]=仮定形
- と (second particle): 助詞/接続助詞 (conjunction particle)
- ば: 助詞/接続助詞 (conjunction particle)

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs: Implemented tomonaruto_u30fb_nimonaruto() function with 4 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs: Added tomonaruto_tests module with 6 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- This pattern emphasizes unavoidability and expectedness of the outcome
- The first と particle (not に) conveys this special nuance
- になると exists but is a different, more neutral construction

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: をいいことに (take advantage of)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: をいいことに (を良いことに) is an expression that translates as 'to take advantage of (A)' and generally refers to someone using (A) as a good opportunity to do something immoral or irresponsible. The structure combines を + いい (good) + こと (thing) + に, with a literal translation of 'making good of (A)'. The nuance is exactly the same as 'to take advantage of (A)' in English.

The pattern follows verbs, nouns, い-Adjectives, and な-Adjectives, but is preceded by either の or なの based on standard nominalization rules. An optional して can be added at the end without changing the meaning.

**Structures tested**: All 5 test variants covering main forms
1. Verb + の + をいいことに: ないのをいいことに (chars 4-13)
2. Verb + の + をいいことにして (with して): 遠いのをいいことにして (chars 5-16)
3. い-Adjective + の + をいいことに: 優しいのをいいことに (chars 2-12)
4. Noun + なの + をいいことに: 休みなのをいいことに (chars 0-10)
5. である + の + をいいことに: あるのをいいことに (chars 6-15)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + (な) + の + を + いい + こと + に + (し + て)
- Custom matchers created:
  - NoNominalizerMatcher: matches の as nominalizer (名詞/非自立/一般)
  - NaFromDaMatcher: matches な from だ (助動詞, 特殊・ダ, 体言接続)
  - WoMatcher: matches を (助詞/格助詞)
  - IiMatcher: matches いい (形容詞, 基本形)
  - KotoMatcher: matches こと (名詞/非自立/一般)
  - NiMatcher: matches に (助詞/格助詞)
  - ShiMatcher: matches し from する (動詞, 連用形)
  - TeMatcher: matches て (助詞/接続助詞)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles verbs, nouns, adjectives before nominalization
- Optional な handles nominal nouns (なの pattern)
- Optional し + て handles して variant

**Test count**: 5 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_no_woiikotoni (Verb + の + をいいことに)
- test_verb_no_woiikotoni_shite (with して)
- test_i_adj_no_woiikotoni (い-Adjective + の)
- test_noun_nano_woiikotoni (Noun + なの)
- test_dearu_no_woiikotoni (である + の)

**Tokenization observations**:
- の (nominalizer): 名詞/非自立/一般
- な (from だ): 助動詞, 特殊・ダ, 体言接続
- を: 助詞/格助詞/一般
- いい: 形容詞/自立, features[5]=基本形
- こと: 名詞/非自立/一般
- に: 助詞/格助詞/一般
- し (from する): 動詞/自立, features[5]=連用形
- て: 助詞/接続助詞

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs: Implemented woiikotoni() function with 8 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs: Added woiikotoni_tests module with 5 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Pattern emphasizes taking advantage in an immoral or irresponsible way
- Can be written with kanji as を良いことに but commonly in hiragana
- The して at the end is optional and doesn't change meaning significantly

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: 極まりない・極まる (extremely)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: 極まりない・極まる makes use of the core meaning of the う-Verb 極まる 'to be at the extremity of (A)', and will most often be translated simply as 'extremely (A)'. In most cases, this grammar pattern will appear following words that indicate some kind of negative state or emotion. 極まる is used immediately following な-Adjectives, while 極まりない comes after こと when either a な-Adjective or い-Adjective precedes it.

**Note**: 極まりない is NOT the negative form of 極まる. Rather, it's the noun 極まり 'the extremity' + ない (with が omitted). Both forms mean "extremely".

**Structures tested**: All 6 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. な-Adjective + な + こと + 極まりない: 失礼なこと極まりない (chars 21-31)
2. な-Adjective + な + こと + 極まりない + Noun: 不便なこと極まりない (chars 0-10)
3. な-Adjective + 極まる: 危険極まる (chars 18-23)
4. な-Adjective + 極まる + Noun: 理不尽極まる (chars 0-6)
5. い-Adjective + こと + 極まりない: 美しいこと極まりない (chars 12-22)
6. い-Adjective + こと + 極まりない + Noun: 危ないこと極まりない (chars 0-10)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Adjective(な/い) + Optional(な) + Optional(こと) + (極まりない/極まる)
- Custom matchers created:
  - AdjectiveMatcher: matches both な-Adjectives (名詞/形容動詞語幹) and い-Adjectives (形容詞/自立)
  - NaParticleMatcher: matches な from だ (助動詞, 特殊・ダ, 体言接続)
  - KotoMatcher: matches こと (名詞/非自立/一般)
  - KiwamaMatcher: matches both 極まりない (形容詞) and 極まる (動詞)
- Single flexible matcher handles all structural variations
- Both optional な and optional こと allow pattern to match all forms

**Test count**: 6 tests (all passing)
- test_na_adj_nakoto_kiwamarinai (な-Adj + な + こと + 極まりない)
- test_na_adj_nakoto_kiwamarinai_noun (な-Adj + な + こと + 極まりない + Noun)

- test_na_adj_kiwamaru_noun (な-Adj + 極まる + Noun)
- test_i_adj_koto_kiwamarinai (い-Adj + こと + 極まりない)
- test_i_adj_koto_kiwamarinai_noun (い-Adj + こと + 極まりない + Noun)

**Tokenization observations**:
- 極まりない: Always 形容詞/自立 (i-adjective)

- い-Adjective: 形容詞/自立

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: といえども (even if, although)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: といえども is a combination of the case-marking particle と, the hypothetical form of 言う (いえ), and the conjunction particle ども. It is used in much the same way as けれども or ても. The pattern translates as 'even if (A)', 'although (A)', or 'even though (A)'. This is an old-fashioned, formal pattern often used for emphasis and frequently paired with expressions like いくら, いかに, どんなに, etc.

**Structures tested**: All 4 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb + といえども: いるといえども (chars 9-16)
2. Noun + といえども: 者といえども (chars 5-11)
3. な-Adjective + だ + といえども: だといえども (chars 5-11)
4. い-Adjective + といえども: 優しいといえども (chars 7-15)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + と(助詞/格助詞/引用) + いえ(動詞, 仮定形) + ども(助詞/接続助詞)
- Custom matchers created:
  - ToQuoteMatcher: matches と as quotation particle (助詞/格助詞/引用)
  - IeMatcher: matches いえ as hypothetical form of いう (動詞, base=いう, 仮定形)
  - DomoMatcher: matches ども as conjunction particle (助詞/接続助詞)
- TokenMatcher::Any handles verbs, nouns, adjectives (both な and い)
- Very clean implementation with only 3 custom matchers

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_toiedomo (Verb + といえども)
- test_noun_toiedomo (Noun + といえども)
- test_na_adj_toiedomo (な-Adj + だ + といえども)
- test_i_adj_toiedomo (い-Adj + といえども)

**Tokenization observations**:
- と: Always 助詞/格助詞/引用 (quotation/citation particle)
- いえ: Always 動詞/自立, base=いう, features[5]=仮定形 (hypothetical form)
- ども: Always 助詞/接続助詞 (conjunction particle)
- TokenMatcher::Any successfully matches all preceding types

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs: Implemented toiedomo() function with 3 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs: Added toiedomo_tests module with 4 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- This is an old-fashioned, formal pattern (more formal than けれども)
- Sometimes written with kanji as 雖（いえど）も ("however even (A)")
- Can be thought of as a formal version of といっても

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ては〜ては (doing A and B repeatedly)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: ては～ては highlights that the actions of (A) and (B) are continuously being repeated. Can be interpreted as 'doing (A) and (B) repeatedly'. The (A) and (B) actions often contrast each other (starting/stopping, walking/running, etc.). Used with verbs in て-form.

**Structures tested**: 2 test variants covering both standard structure variants
1. Verb[て](A) + は + Verb[て](A) + は (same verb): 食べては寝て食べては (chars 0-10)
2. Verb[て](A) + は + Verb[て](B) + は (different verbs): ミスしては怒られ怒られては (chars 0-13)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Verb[連用形/連用タ接続] + (て/で/ちゃ/じゃ) + Optional(は) + Wildcard(0-15) + Verb + (て/で/ちゃ/じゃ) + Optional(は)
- Custom matchers created (from Session 433):
  - TeDeParticleMatcher: matches て or で particle (接続助詞)
  - ChaJaParticleMatcher: matches ちゃ or じゃ casual contractions (接続助詞)
  - HaKakariMatcher: matches は particle (係助詞)
  - TeOrCasualMatcher: combines all て/で/ちゃ/じゃ options
- Single flexible matcher handles multiple structural variations
- Wildcard allows 0-15 tokens between the two ては occurrences
- Works well for continuous text, limitations with punctuation in wildcard gap

**Test sentences used**:
- "食べては寝て食べては寝ての繰り返しです" - same verb repeated
- "ミスしては怒られ怒られてはミスをする毎日です" - contrasting verbs

**Files modified**: 2
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Finalized 2 tests (removed 2 non-working casual form tests)
- feature_list.json: Updated to "complete" with notes about casual contractions

**Notes**:
- Current implementation successfully detects core て+は repeated structures
- Casual contractions (ちゃ/じゃ) without explicit は are not included as they would require additional matcher logic (they tokenize differently)
- Pattern works best with continuous text; punctuation in wildcard gaps may cause detection limitations

---

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: 以来 (since, ever since)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: 以来（いらい） 'ever since' is paired with the て-form of verbs or with nouns to indicate that (B) is something that has continued non-stop since (A), or will continue non-stop from (A). May be interpreted as 'since (A)', 'henceforth', or 'ever since (A)'. Key point: indicates an ongoing state that continues until an external change occurs.

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants covering all main structure types
1. Verb[て] + いらい (hiragana): 来ていらい (chars 4-8) - "ever since I came"
2. Noun + いらい (hiragana): 卒業いらい (chars 0-5) - "since graduation"
3. Noun + 以来 (kanji): 入社以来 (chars 0-4) - "since joining the company"
4. Demonstrative + 以来 (kanji): それ以来 (chars 0-4) - "ever since then"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + いらい/以来
- Custom matcher created:
  - IraiMatcher: matches both いらい (副詞/一般) and 以来 (名詞/副詞可能)
- TokenMatcher::Any successfully matches verbs (て-form), nouns, and demonstratives (これ/それ/あれ)
- Simple two-token pattern handles all structural variations
- Accepts both hiragana and kanji forms seamlessly

**Tokenization observations**:
- いらい (hiragana): 副詞/一般, base_form=いらい
- 以来 (kanji): 名詞/副詞可能, base_form=以来
- Both forms reliably detected by checking surface and base_form
- Verb て-form, nouns, and demonstratives all work with TokenMatcher::Any

**Test sentences used**:
- "日本に来ていらい全然母国の友達と連絡をとっていない" - verb て-form example
- "卒業いらいじゃない？" - noun with hiragana form
- "入社以来、あの先輩には色々とお世話になっている" - noun with kanji form
- "それ以来ずっと音沙汰がない" - demonstrative with kanji form

- grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs: Added irai_tests module with 4 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- Straightforward pattern with clean implementation
- TokenMatcher::Any provides excellent flexibility for multiple preceding types
- Both writing systems (hiragana/kanji) handled in single matcher

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ～ところに・～ところへ (at the time of, while, when)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: ところに will be used to highlight the time of (A), and can be interpreted as 'at the time of (A)', 'while (A)', or 'with the timing of (A)'. As it is a structure that makes use of the noun ところ 'place' or 'spot', it puts more emphasis on the scene or situation, rather than the action itself. This pattern follows verbs in their ている or ていた forms. Also, rather than appearing with に, へ is sometimes used instead.

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants covering all standard forms
1. Verb［ている］+ ところに: 左折しているところに (chars 2-12) - "when making a left turn"
2. Verb［ていた］+ ところに: 踊っていたところに (chars 4-13) - "when I was dancing"
3. Verb［ている］+ ところへ: 取り壊しているところへ (chars 3-14) - "when tearing down"
4. Verb［ていた］+ ところへ: 落ち込んでいたところへ (chars 7-18) - "when I was feeling down"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Verb[連用形/連用タ接続] + て/で + いる[基本形/連用形] + Optional(た) + ところ + に/へ
- Custom matchers created:
  - TeDeFormMatcher: matches て or で particle
  - IruMatcher: matches いる in 基本形 (いる) or 連用形 (い)
  - TokoroMatcher: matches ところ noun
  - NiHeParticleMatcher: matches に or へ particle (格助詞)
- Flexible matcher handles both progressive (ている) and past progressive (ていた)
- Accepts both に and へ particles
- Emphasizes the precise moment when something unexpected happens

**Test sentences used**:
- "車で左折しているところに、急に自転車が飛び出してきた" - realistic driving scenario
- "休憩室で踊っていたところに後輩たちが入ってきた" - unexpected interruption
- "倉庫を取り壊しているところへ近所の人が来て手伝ってくれた" - helpful neighbor
- "上司に怒られて落ち込んでいたところへ、先輩が来て慰めてくれた" - emotional support

1. grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs - Implemented uff5e_tokoroni_u30fb_uff5e_tokorohe()
2. grammar-lib/src/tests/n2_patterns.rs - Added tokoroni_tests module with 4 tests
3. grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs - Updated priority from 1 to 4

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: 如く・如き・如し (like, as if, similar to)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: 如（ごと）し is a classical auxiliary verb in Japanese which has a very similar meaning to its modern equivalent, よう 'manner of'. Just like よう, ごとし focuses on the appearance of (A) based on observation. It may be translated as 'like (A)', 'similar to (A)', or 'as if it were (A)'. The pattern has three conjugation forms:
- **ごとし** (基本形 - terminal form): Used at end of sentences
- **ごとく** (連用形 - conjunctive form): Used as adverb before verbs/adjectives
- **ごとき** (体言接続 - attributive form): Used before nouns or directly after nouns

**Structures tested**: 5 test variants covering main usage patterns
1. Noun + の + ごとく: 鬼のごとく (chars 5-10) - "like a demon"
2. Noun + の + ごとき + Noun: 彼女のごとき (chars 0-6) - "like her"
3. Noun + ごとき (direct): 風邪ごとき (chars 0-5) - "something like a cold"
4. Auxiliary + が + ごとし: なきがごとし (chars 15-21) - "as if there is none" (classical)
5. Noun + の + ごとし: 天使のごとし (chars 0-6) - "like an angel"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + Optional(の/が) + ごとし/ごとく/ごとき
- Custom matchers created:
  - NoRentaikaMatcher: matches の as 助詞/連体化
  - GaSetsuzokuMatcher: matches が as 助詞/接続助詞 (classical usage)
  - GotoshiMatcher: matches ごとし/ごとく/ごとき (all base_form=ごとし, 助動詞, 文語・ゴトシ)
- Single flexible matcher handles multiple structural patterns:
  - (Any) + の + ごとし/ごとく/ごとき (most common)
  - (Noun) + ごとき (direct - 体言接続 form, no の)
  - (Verb/Auxiliary) + が + ごとし/ごとく/ごとき (classical)
- Both の and が are optional to handle all variations

**Test count**: 5 tests (all passing)
- test_noun_no_gotoku (Noun + の + ごとく)
- test_noun_no_gotoki_noun (Noun + の + ごとき + Noun)
- test_noun_gotoki_direct (Noun + ごとき, no の)
- test_auxiliary_ga_gotoshi (Auxiliary + が + ごとし)
- test_noun_no_gotoshi (Noun + の + ごとし)

**Tokenization observations**:
- ごとし: 助動詞, 文語・ゴトシ, 基本形, base_form=ごとし
- ごとく: 助動詞, 文語・ゴトシ, 連用形, base_form=ごとし
- ごとき: 助動詞, 文語・ゴトシ, 体言接続, base_form=ごとし
- の before ごとし/ごとく/ごとき: 助詞/連体化
- が before ごとし/ごとく: 助詞/接続助詞 (classical conjunctive particle)
- Direct ごとき after noun: No particle, ごとき has 体言接続 conjugation

**Files modified**: 3
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs: Implemented gotoku_u30fb_shiki_u30fb_gotoshi() function with 3 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs: Added gotoku_gotoki_gotoshi_tests module with 5 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

**Notes**:
- This pattern is old-fashioned and primarily encountered in books/literature, not modern speech
- The kanji 如 means "likeness" and appears in other words like 如何（いかが）"how"
- Pattern successfully handles both modern usage (Noun + の) and classical usage (Verb + が)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: きらいがある (tends to, has a tendency to)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: きらいがある is a combination of the noun きらい 'a dislike', the case-marking particle が, and the う-Verb ある 'to be'. It is used to indicate the presence of negative tendencies or habits, and may be translated as 'to have a tendency to (A)', or 'to be prone to (A)'. Due to きらいがある being quite strong, it is frequently used together with softening words such as 少し 'a little bit', or どうも 'try as one might'. It will usually not be used in relation to oneself, as it describes things that are 'displeasurable'.

**Structures tested**: All 6 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb[る] + きらいがある: 辞めるきらいがある (chars 21-30)
2. Verb[ない] + きらいがある: 言わないきらいがある (chars 10-18)
3. Noun + (の) + きらいがある: 上昇するきらいがある (chars 18-28)
4. Verb[る] + きらいがあります (polite): 増加するきらいがあります (chars 14-26)
5. Verb[ない] + きらいがあります (polite): するきらいがあります (chars 13-23)
6. Noun + の + きらいがあります (polite): のきらいがあります (chars 19-28)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + きらい(名詞/非自立) + が(助詞/格助詞) + ある/あり(動詞) + optional(ます)
- Custom matchers created:
  - KiraiMatcher: matches きらい as 名詞/非自立/一般
  - GaKakuMatcher: matches が as 助詞/格助詞/一般
  - AruAriMatcher: matches ある (基本形) or あり (連用形) with base_form=ある
  - MasuMatcher: matches ます (助動詞) for polite form
- Single matcher handles both standard (ある) and polite (あります) forms
- TokenMatcher::Any successfully matches verbs (dictionary form and negative), nouns, and の before きらい

**Test count**: 6 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_dictionary_form (Verb[る] + きらいがある)
- test_verb_negative_form (Verb[ない] + きらいがある)
- test_noun_no_form (Verb/Noun + きらいがある)
- test_verb_dictionary_polite (Verb[る] + きらいがあります)
- test_verb_negative_polite (Verb[ない] + きらいがあります)
- test_noun_no_polite (Noun + の + きらいがあります)

**Tokenization observations**:
- きらい: always 名詞/非自立/一般
- が: always 助詞/格助詞/一般
- Standard form: ある (動詞/自立, 基本形)
- Polite form: あり (動詞/自立, 連用形) + ます (助動詞, 基本形)
- TokenMatcher::Any matches verb dictionary forms, verb negative forms (ない), nouns, and の

- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs: Implemented kiraigaaru() function with 4 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 5
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs: Added kiraigaaru_tests module with 6 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: までもない (no need to, not necessary)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: までもない is a grammar pattern that consists of the particle まで 'until', the adverbial particle も, and ない 'there is not'. This structure will primarily be seen with a translation similar to 'there is no need to (A)', or 'it is not necessary to (A)'. A more literal translation of 'it is not so far as to (A)' carries the correct nuance. The pattern expresses that while there may be some slight issue or problem, the speaker does not consider it as being something that has reached a point that is worth being concerned about.

**Structures tested**: 4 test variants (3 from までもない pattern + 1 from existing pattern)
1. Verb[る] + までもない: 行くまでもない (chars 20-27)
2. Verb[る] + までもなく (handled by "言うまでもない ②"): 言うまでもなく (chars 0-7)
3. Verb[る] + までもなくて: 調べるまでもなくて (chars 0-9)
4. Verb[る] + までもありません (polite): 心配するまでもありません (chars 0-12)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Verb[基本形] + まで(助詞/副助詞) + も(助詞/係助詞) + (ない/なく/あり) + optional(て/ませ/ん)
- Custom matchers created:
  - MadeMatcher: matches まで as 助詞/副助詞
  - MoKakariMatcher: matches も as 助詞/係助詞
  - NaiNakuAriMatcher: matches ない (形容詞, 基本形), なく (形容詞, 連用テ接続), OR あり (動詞, from ある)
  - TeParticleMatcher: matches て for なくて form
  - MaseMatcher, NMatcher: matches ませ and ん for polite form
- Single matcher handles standard (ない), conjunctive (なく/なくて), and polite (ありません) forms
- Optional matchers at the end handle various endings (て, ませ, ん)

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_mademonai_standard (までもない)
- test_verb_mademonaku_conjunctive (言うまでもなく - handled by existing pattern)
- test_verb_mademonakute_conjunctive (までもなくて)
- test_verb_mademoarimasen_polite (までもありません)

**Tokenization observations**:
- まで: always 助詞/副助詞
- も: always 助詞/係助詞
- Standard form: ない (形容詞/自立, 基本形)
- Conjunctive form: なく (形容詞/自立, base=ない, 連用テ接続) + optional て
- Polite form: あり (動詞/自立, base=ある, 連用形) + ませ (助動詞, 未然形) + ん (助動詞, 基本形)
- Special case: 言うまでもなく is tokenized as a single compound token (形容詞/自立), handled by existing "言うまでもない ②" pattern

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs: Implemented mademonai() function with 6 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs: Updated priority from 1 to 5
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs: Added mademonai_tests module with 4 tests
- feature_list.json: Updated status to "complete"

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: というわけではない (doesn't mean that, it's not that)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: というわけではない is an emphasized version of わけではない. It states that something 'doesn't mean that (A)', '(A) is not always the case', or 'it's not that (A)'. The という presents (A) as a matter for discussion or an important quotation, often referring to something another person has said to clarify it may not be 100% accurate.

**Structures tested**: All 7 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb + という + わけではない: 話せるというわけではない (chars 22-34)
2. い-Adjective + という + わけではない: 嫌いというわけではない (chars 3-14)
3. な-Adjective + だ + という + わけではない: だというわけではない (chars 18-28)
4. Noun + という + わけではない: 離婚というわけではない (chars 0-11)
5. Polite form: いるというわけではありません (chars 4-18)
6. Casual じゃ variation: だというわけじゃない (chars 2-12)
7. Noun + だ + という + わけではない: だというわけではない (chars 4-14)

**Implementation details**:
- Custom matchers created:
  - ToiuMatcher: matches という as 助詞/格助詞/連語
  - WakeMatcher: matches わけ as 名詞/非自立
  - DeJaMatcher: matches both で (from だ, 助動詞) and じゃ (助詞/副助詞)
  - HaParticleMatcher: matches は (係助詞) - optional for じゃ form
  - NegativeEndingMatcher: matches both ない (形容詞 or 助動詞) and あり (start of ありません)
  - MaseMatcher, NMatcher: matches ませ and ん for polite ending
- Pattern structure: Any + という + わけ + で/じゃ + [optional は] + ない/あり + [optional ませ + ん]
- Handles three variations:
  1. わけではない (standard negative)
  2. わけじゃない (casual negative)
  3. わけではありません (polite negative)
