### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: を契機に (as a trigger/opportunity, led to)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: を契機に is a formal grammar construction meaning '(A) led to (B)', 'as an opportunity to (A), (B)', or 'as a result of (A), (B)'. Made up of を + 契機 (chance/trigger) + に. Used to indicate a trigger for a significant event - usually something extremely big in terms of life or current/historical events in the world.

**Structures tested**: All 5 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Noun + を契機に: 出産を契機に (chars 0-6)
2. Verb + の + を契機に: のを契機に (chars 7-12)
3. Verb + こと + を契機に: ことを契機に (chars 10-16)
4. Noun + を契機にして: 入院を契機にして (chars 0-8)
5. Noun + を契機として: 暗殺を契機として (chars 4-12)

**Implementation details**:
- Custom matchers created:
  - WoMatcher: matches を as 助詞/格助詞/一般
  - KeikiMatcher: matches 契機 as 名詞
  - NiToshiteMatcher: matches both に (助詞/格助詞) and として (助詞/格助詞/連語)
  - ShiMatcher: matches し (from する) for にして variation
  - TeMatcher: matches て for にして variation
- Pattern structure: Any + を + 契機 + に/として + [optional し + て]
- Handles three ending variations:
  1. を契機に (standard)
  2. を契機として (formal variation)
  3. を契機にして (extended form)
- Accepts Noun, の, or こと before を

**Test count**: 5 tests (all passing)

**Tokenization observations**:
- を: 助詞/格助詞/一般
- 契機: 名詞/一般
- に: 助詞/格助詞/一般
- として: 助詞/格助詞/連語 (single compound token)
- にして: tokenizes as に + し (from する) + て
- TokenMatcher::Any successfully matches nouns, の, and こと before を

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: というわけではない (doesn't mean that, it's not that)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: というわけではない is an emphasized version of わけではない. It states that something 'doesn't mean that (A)', '(A) is not always the case', or 'it's not that (A)'. The という presents (A) as a matter for discussion or an important quotation, often referring to something another person has said to clarify it may not be 100% accurate.

**Structures tested**: All 7 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb + という + わけではない: 話せるというわけではない (chars 22-34)
2. い-Adjective + という + わけではない: 嫌いというわけではない (chars 3-14)
3. な-Adjective + だ + という + わけではない: だというわけではない (chars 18-28)
4. Noun + という + わけではない: 離婚というわけではない (chars 0-11)
5. Polite form: いるというわけではありません (chars 4-18)
6. Casual じゃ variation: だというわけじゃない (chars 2-12)
7. Noun + だ + という + わけではない: だというわけではない (chars 4-14)

**Implementation details**:
- Custom matchers created:
  - ToiuMatcher: matches という as 助詞/格助詞/連語
  - WakeMatcher: matches わけ as 名詞/非自立
  - DeJaMatcher: matches both で (from だ, 助動詞) and じゃ (助詞/副助詞)
  - HaParticleMatcher: matches は (係助詞) - optional for じゃ form
  - NegativeEndingMatcher: matches both ない (形容詞 or 助動詞) and あり (start of ありません)
  - MaseMatcher, NMatcher: matches ませ and ん for polite ending
- Pattern structure: Any + という + わけ + で/じゃ + [optional は] + ない/あり + [optional ませ + ん]
- Handles three variations:
  1. わけではない (standard negative)
  2. わけじゃない (casual negative)
  3. わけではありません (polite negative)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #1: に際して (on the occasion of, at the time of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: に際して combines に, 際する (verb meaning "to come to pass"), and て-form. It's used to show particular occasions, frequently regarding one-off events or chance occurrences. More formal than 際に. Translates as "on the occasion of (A)" or "at the time of (A)".

**Structures tested**: All 3 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Noun + に際して: 引越しに際して (chars 0-7) - "on the occasion of moving"
2. Verb[る] + に際して: 始めるに際して (chars 10-17) - "at the time of starting"
3. Noun + に際しての + Noun: 申し込むに際しての (chars 9-18) - "at the time of applying, the..."

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: (Verb[基本形] or Noun) + に際して (single compound token) + optional(の)
- Custom matchers: VerbOrNounMatcher, NisaishiteMatcher, NoRentaikaMatcher
- に際して is always tokenized as 助詞/格助詞/連語 (particle/case-particle/phrase)
- Optional の handles "に際しての + Noun" construction
- Single simple pattern structure

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)

- の after に際して: 助詞/連体化

Translates as "at the time of (A)", "when doing (A)", or "on the occasion of (A)". Unlike に際して, primarily used for positive or neutral situations only (not negative events).

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ～のうち(で) (among, out of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: のうち comes from the noun 内（うち） meaning "within" or "inside". It appears after nouns or numbers to signify "amongst (A)" or "out of (A)", similar to の中（なか）. The pattern is often followed by particles like で, の, or から to complete the meaning. It's less common than のなか but more formal.

**Structures tested**: All 5 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Noun + のうちで: 会社のうちで (chars 2-8) - "among the company"
2. Noun + のうちで + Number: 人のうちで (chars 6-11) - "among the people"
3. Noun + のうちの + Number: 本のうちの (chars 10-15) - "of the [fishing rods]"
4. Number + Counter + のうち + から: 全てのうちから (chars 5-12) - "from among all"
5. この/その + うち: このうち (chars 0-4), そのうちで (chars 12-17) - "among these/those"

**Implementation details**:
- Created TWO matcher functions due to different tokenization patterns:
  1. **uff5e_nouchi_de()**: Any + の(助詞/連体化) + うち(名詞/非自立) + optional(で/の/から)
  2. **uff5e_nouchi_de_kono()**: この/その/あの(連体詞) + うち(名詞/非自立) + optional(で/の/から)
- Both patterns registered in patterns.rs with same name "～のうち(で)"
- Custom matchers: UchiMatcher, NoRentaikaMatcher, KonoSonoMatcher, DeNoKaraMatcher
- うち is always tokenized as 名詞/非自立/副詞可能
- Optional particle matcher handles で, の, から variations

**Test count**: 6 tests (all passing)
- test_noun_nouchi_de (Noun + の + うちで)
- test_noun_nouchi_de_number (Noun + の + うちで + context)
- test_noun_nouchi_no_number (Noun + の + うちの + Number)
- test_number_counter_nouchi (Counter + の + うちから)
- test_sono_uchi_de (その + うちで)
- test_kono_uchi (この + うち)

**Tokenization observations**:
- Noun + の + うち: Noun + の(助詞/連体化) + うち(名詞/非自立/副詞可能)
- この/その + うち: この/その(連体詞) + うち(名詞/非自立/副詞可能)
- で after うち: 助詞/格助詞/一般
- の after うち: 助詞/連体化
- から after うち: 助詞/格助詞/一般

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs (implemented uff5e_nouchi_de() and uff5e_nouchi_de_kono())

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ねばならない (must, have to)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: ねばならない is a formal, literary grammar pattern expressing obligation ("must" or "have to"). It combines the classical auxiliary verb ず in its ね form (仮定形 of ぬ) with ば (conditional particle) and ならない (negative potential form of なる). Literally means "if not (A), it won't be (B)" - a double negative expressing necessity. The pattern is formal and mostly found in literature or formal writing.

**Structures tested**: All 4 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb[未然形] + ねばならない: 払わねばならない (chars 13-21)
2. する → せねばならない (exception): せねばならない (chars 12-19)
3. Verb[未然形] + ねばなりません (polite): 従わねばなりません (chars 18-27)
4. する → せねばなりません (polite exception): せねばなりません (chars 24-32)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Verb[未然形] + ね(助動詞/ぬ/仮定形) + ば(助詞/接続助詞) + なら/なり(動詞/なる/未然形 or 連用形) + ない/ませ(助動詞) + optional(ん)
- Custom matchers: VerbMizenMatcher, NeAuxiliaryMatcher, BaParticleMatcher, NariNaruMatcher, NaiOrMasenMatcher, NParticleMatcher
- Single matcher handles both standard (ならない) and polite (なりません) forms
- VerbMizenMatcher accepts both 未然形 and 未然ヌ接続 (for せ from する)
- NariNaruMatcher accepts both 未然形 (なら) and 連用形 (なり) from なる
- NaiOrMasenMatcher branches to either ない or ませ, with optional ん for polite negative

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_standard
- test_suru_exception_standard
- test_verb_polite
- test_suru_exception_polite

**Tokenization observations**:
- Standard: Verb[未然形] + ね(助動詞/ぬ/仮定形) + ば(接続助詞) + なら(動詞/なる/未然形) + ない(助動詞)
- Polite: Verb[未然形] + ね(助動詞/ぬ/仮定形) + ば(接続助詞) + なり(動詞/なる/連用形) + ませ(助動詞/ます/未然形) + ん(助動詞/不変化型)
- する exception: せ(動詞/する/未然ヌ接続) + same pattern as above
- ね is always 助動詞 with base_form=ぬ and 仮定形 conjugation

- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs (implemented nebanaranaי())

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: に越したことはない (there is nothing better than)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: に越したことはない combines に (particle), 越す (う-verb "to cross over/surpass"), た (past), こと (nominalizer), は (topic particle), and ない (negative). Literally means "there is nothing beyond (A)" or "nothing surpasses (A)". Used to express "there is nothing better than (A)" or "(A) would be the best". Generally presents something positive or some ideal outcome that is wished for.

**Structures tested**: All 8 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb + に越したことはない: 着くにこしたことはない (chars 27-38)
2. い-Adjective + に越したことはない: 広いにこしたことはない (chars 2-13)
3. な-Adjective + である + に越したことはない: あるにこしたことはない (chars 6-17)
4. Noun + である + に越したことはない: あるにこしたことはない (chars 5-16)
5. Verb + に越したことはありません (polite): いるにこしたことはありません (chars 3-17)
6. Verb[ない] + に越したことはない: ないにこしたことはない (chars 5-16)
7. い-Adjective[なく] + ない + に越したことはない: ないにこしたことはない (chars 8-19)
8. な-Adjective + ではない + に越したことはない: ないにこしたことはない (chars 8-19)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + に + こし + た + こと + は + (ない OR あり+ませ+ん)
- Custom matchers: NiKoshitaMatcher, KoshiMatcher, KotoNonIndependentMatcher, WaKakariMatcher, NaiOrAriMatcher, MaseMatcher, NNegativeMatcher
- Handles both standard (ない) and polite (ありません) forms with optional matchers
- NaiOrAriMatcher matches either ない or あり to branch between the two endings
- Works with verbs, adjectives, nouns, and である constructions
- Also handles negative forms (ない + に越したことはない = "it's best if not X")

**Test count**: 8 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_standard
- test_i_adjective_standard
- test_na_adjective_dearu
- test_noun_dearu
- test_verb_polite
- test_nai_verb
- test_nai_i_adjective
- test_nai_na_adjective

**Tokenization observations**:
- に can be 助詞/格助詞/一般 or 助詞/副詞化 depending on context
- こし: 動詞/自立, base=こす, 連用形
- こと: 名詞/非自立/一般
- は: 助詞/係助詞
- ない: 形容詞/自立 or 助動詞/特殊・ナイ
- ありません: あり(動詞/連用形) + ませ(助動詞/未然形) + ん(助動詞/不変化型)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented #2: ないではいられない (can't help but, can't resist)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: ないではいられない expresses that something cannot be helped or cannot be resisted. It combines verbs in the negative form (ない) connected to で/じゃ, は, and the negative-potential form of いる. Literally means "to be unable to be without (A)". Primarily expresses the inability of the speaker to tolerate (A) not happening, due to wanting to do it. Also highlights that some particular emotion, feeling, or reaction cannot be repressed.

**Structures tested**: All 4 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json

4. Verb[ない] + ではいられません (polite): 助けないではいられません (chars 16-28)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: Any + ない(助動詞) + で/じゃ + (は) + い(いる/未然形) + られ(られる/接尾) + Any
- Custom matchers: NaiAuxiliaryMatcher, DeJyaMatcher, WaKakariParticleMatcher, IruMizenMatcher, RareMatcher
- Handles dual tokenization for で: either 助詞/接続助詞 or 助動詞/だ
- Optional は matcher handles both では (formal) and じゃ (casual) forms
- Final TokenMatcher::Any captures various endings (ない, なく, なかった, ません)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: たって (even if, even though, no matter how)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: たって is a casual conjunction particle that is equivalent to ても or でも, meaning "even if", "even though", or "no matter how". It can attach to verbs, adjectives, and nouns with various forms. The pattern is casual register and commonly used in spoken Japanese.

**Structures tested**: All 8 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb[た] + って: 謝ったって (chars 3-8)
2. Verb[なくて] + たって: 楽しくなくたって (chars 7-15)
3. い-Adjective[く] + たって: 欲しくたって (chars 4-10)
4. い-Adjective[なくて] + たって: 難しくなくたって (chars 4-12)
5. な-Adjective + だって: 馬鹿だって (chars 8-13) - detected by existing だって pattern
6. な-Adjective + じゃなくたって: 有名じゃなくたって (chars 0-9)
7. Noun + だって: 友達だって (chars 0-5) - detected by existing だって pattern
8. Noun + じゃなくたって: 専門家じゃなくたって (chars 2-10 for 家じゃなくたって)

**Implementation details**:
- Created 3 matcher patterns due to different tokenizations:
  1. **たって**: Verb[連用タ接続] + たって (e.g., 謝ったって)
  2. **たって_naku**: Verb/Adj + なく + たって, Noun/な-Adj + じゃ + なく + たって
  3. **たって_i_adj_ku**: い-Adjective[く] + たって (special tokenization: Adj[く] + たっ(verb) + て)
- The Noun/な-Adj + だって structures reuse the existing "だって" pattern
- Custom matchers: TatteParticleMatcher, NakuMatcher, JyaParticleMatcher, TatsuVerbMatcher, TeParticleMatcher

**Test count**: 8 tests (all passing)
- test_tatte_verb_ta
- test_tatte_verb_nakute
- test_tatte_i_adj_ku
- test_tatte_i_adj_nakute
- test_tatte_na_adj (uses だって pattern)
- test_tatte_na_adj_negative
- test_tatte_noun (uses だって pattern)
- test_tatte_noun_negative

**Tokenization observations**:
- Verb[た] + って: verb (連用タ接続) + たって (助詞/接続助詞)
- なくたって: なく (助動詞 ない, 連用テ接続) + たって (助詞/接続助詞)
- くたって (i-adj): adj (連用テ接続) + たっ (動詞 たつ) + て (助詞/接続助詞) - unusual split
- だって: single token (助詞/副助詞)
- じゃなくたって: じゃ (助詞/副助詞) + なく (助動詞) + たって (助詞/接続助詞)

- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n2.rs (implemented tatte(), tatte_naku(), tatte_i_adj_ku())

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: を余儀なくされる (to be forced to)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: を余儀なくされる is a formal grammar pattern combining を (adverbial particle), 余儀 (another method), なく (conjunctive form of ない), and される (passive form of する). Literally means "to not be given another way than to (A)" - i.e., "to be forced to (A)". Used when something is unavoidable.

**Structures tested**: 2 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Noun + を余儀なくされる (standard/past): 封鎖をよぎなくされた (chars 29-39), 自粛をよぎなくされた (chars 15-25)
2. Noun + を余儀なくされます (polite): 生活をよぎなくされます (chars 28-39)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: Noun + を(格助詞) + よぎなく(形容詞/自立) + さ(動詞/する) + れ(動詞/接尾/passive) + た/ます(助動詞)
- Custom matchers: YoginakuMatcher, SasuruMatcher, RePassiveMatcher, TaMasuMatcher, WoParticleMatcher
- Pattern is a passive construction (される)
- Always formal register

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)
- test_woyoginakusareru_past (封鎖をよぎなくされた)
- test_woyoginakusareru_past_2 (自粛をよぎなくされた)

**Tokenization observations**:
- よぎなく: 形容詞/自立, base=よぎない, 連用テ接続
- さ: 動詞/自立, base=する, サ変・スル/未然レル接続
- れ: 動詞/接尾, base=れる, 一段/連用形 (passive auxiliary)
- た/ます: 助動詞 (past or polite ending)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: に至っては (when it comes to, as for)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: に至っては combines に (case-marking particle), いたる (る-Verb "to climax/culminate"), て-form (いたって), and は (adverbial particle). Literally means "culminating within (A) is (B)". Presents the most extreme example from within a specific category, often with negative/unfavorable nuance. Translates as "when it comes to (A)", "in particular to (A)", "what's worse".

**Structures tested**: 1 structure variant from grammar_points_data.json

   - リスニングにいたっては (chars 13-24) - "what's worse, listening"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: Noun + に(格助詞) + いたっ(動詞/自立, base=いたる) + て(接続助詞) + は(係助詞)
- Custom matchers: NiParticleMatcher, ItattaMatcher, TeParticleMatcher, WaParticleMatcher
- Five-token pattern matching the full construction
- Register: formal

- て: 助詞/接続助詞
- は: 助詞/係助詞

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: に至るまで (everything from A to B, up to and including)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: 至る is an う-Verb meaning "to attain" or "to reach the climax of". When combined with に and まで, に至るまで expresses "everything from (A) to (B)", "up to and including (B)", emphasizing a wide or extreme range between two points.

**Structures tested**: 3 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Noun + に至るまで: 大人に至るまで (chars 13-20) - "up to adults"
2. Noun + に至るまで + の + Noun: 退職に至るまでの期間 (chars 6-16) - "the period up to retirement"
3. (Often preceded by から/より): 福岡に至るまで (chars 7-14) - "(from Osaka) to Fukuoka"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: Noun + に(格助詞) + 至る(動詞/基本形) + まで(副助詞) + optional(の + Noun)
- Custom matchers: NiParticleMatcher, ItaruMatcher, MadeParticleMatcher, NoRentaikaMatcher
- Matches core pattern only (not the optional から/より prefix) to avoid false matches
- Pattern range includes full construction from Noun through optional の + Noun
- Register: standard

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)
- test_niitarumade_kara_basic
- test_niitarumade_no_noun
- test_niitarumade_yori

**Tokenization observations**:
- 至る is always 動詞/自立 with 基本形 conjugation
- に is 助詞/格助詞/一般 (case particle)
- まで is 助詞/副助詞 (adverbial particle)
- の (when present) is 助詞/連体化 (relativizing particle)

**Implementation notes**:
- Initially tried to include optional から/より prefix in matcher
- Found this caused false matches with てから (after doing) constructions
- Simplified to match only core pattern (Noun + に至るまで)
- This approach is cleaner and more accurate

**Files modified**: 4
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs (implemented niitarumade())
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs (updated priority from 1 to 5)
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs (added 3 tests)
- feature_list.json (marked に至るまで as complete)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: に足る (worthy of, enough for)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: 足る is an う-Verb meaning "to be sufficient". When paired with に and either verbs or nouns, に足る indicates that something is "worthy of (A)" or "enough for (A)". This is a formal grammar structure primarily seen in books or speeches.

**Structures tested**: 2 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb[る] + に足る + Noun: 勝つに足る能力 (chars 7-14) - "ability worthy of winning"
2. Noun A + に足る + Noun B: 信頼に足る友達 (chars 0-7) - "trustworthy friend"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: (Verb[基本形] or Noun) + に(格助詞) + 足る(動詞/基本形) + Noun
- Custom matchers: VerbOrNounMatcher, NiParticleMatcher, TaruMatcher
- VerbOrNounMatcher flexibly accepts either verb in 基本形 or any noun
- TaruMatcher specifically matches 足る verb in dictionary form
- Simple 4-token pattern structure
- Register: formal

**Test count**: 2 tests (all passing)
- test_nitaru_verb
- test_nitaru_noun

**Tokenization observations**:
- 足る is always 動詞/自立 with 五段・ラ行/基本形 conjugation
- に is 助詞/格助詞/一般 (case particle)
- First element can be verb (基本形) or noun
- Always followed by a noun that it modifies (the thing that is or is not worthy)

**Usage notes**:
- Always appears in dictionary form (足る, never 足りる or 足れ)
- For negation, negate the following verb, not 足る itself
- May appear as "Noun + に足る" or "Noun + する + に足る" (latter emphasizes action)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: しかしながら (however, nevertheless)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: しかしながら is a formal conjunction combining しかし 'however' and ながら 'while/although', creating an emphatic form meaning "however" or "nevertheless". It's stronger than しかし alone and appears at the beginning of a sentence to contrast with a previous statement.

**Structures tested**: Single structure from grammar_points_data.json
1. Phrase。 しかしながら + Phrase:
   - しかしながら (chars 16-22) - "However" (example 1)
   - しかしながら (chars 19-25) - "However" (example 2)
   - しかしながら (chars 15-21) - "However" (example 3)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: Single token match for しかしながら as 接続詞 (conjunction)
- Custom matcher: ShikashiNagaraMatcher checks surface == "しかしながら" AND pos == "接続詞"
- Pattern range: Just the conjunction itself (single token)
- Simpler than しかし alone but conveys stronger emphasis
- Register: formal

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: も又 (also, in addition)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: も又 is a formal/emphatic adverbial combination of も (also) and また (again/also), meaning "in addition to (A), (B)" or "also with (A), (B)". It's stronger and more formal than using も alone, emphasizing that something important needs to be considered about (A) in addition to other things.

**Structures tested**: All 4 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Noun + もまた:
   - 明日もまた (chars 0-5) - "tomorrow also"
2. な-Adjective + なの + もまた:
   - のもまた (chars 6-10) - "(being necessary) is also" (経験が必要なのもまた)
3. い-Adjective + の + もまた:
   - のもまた (chars 17-21) - "(being spicy) is also" (辛いのもまた)
4. Verb + の + もまた:
   - のもまた (chars 16-20) - "(to implement) is also" (実行するのもまた)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: TokenMatcher::Any + も(係助詞) + また(接続詞)
- Custom matchers: MoParticleMatcher (も as 助詞/係助詞), MataMatcher (また as 接続詞)
- Uses TokenMatcher::Any to match any noun, adjective, or verb (with optional nominalizers)
- The pattern automatically handles の nominalization for verbs/adjectives and なの for な-adjectives
- Pattern range: Includes the preceding element + も + また
- Register: formal

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_momata_noun
- test_momata_na_adjective
- test_momata_i_adjective
- test_momata_verb

**Tokenization observations**:
- も is always 助詞/係助詞
- また is always 接続詞
- の (nominalizer) appears as 名詞/非自立/一般 for verb/い-adjective nominalization
- な (from な-adjectives) comes from だ (助動詞/体言接続)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: 際に (on the occasion of, when)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: 際に combines the noun 際 (sai - "verge, occasion") with the particle に to express "on the occasion of (A)", "when (A)", or "at the time of (A)". It's a formal/polite way to indicate timing, often used in instructions or formal announcements.

**Structures tested**: All 3 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb[る] + 際に: 入る際に (chars 5-9) - "when entering"
2. Verb[た] + 際に: 飛び散った際に (chars 10-17) - "when it scattered"
3. Noun + の + 際に: 面接の際に (chars 3-8) - "on the occasion of the interview"

**Implementation details**:
- Pattern structure: (Verb or Noun) + (optional た) + (optional の) + 際(名詞/非自立) + に(格助詞)
- Custom matchers: SaiMatcher (際 as dependent noun), NiParticleMatcher, NoParticleMatcher (の as relativizer)
- Uses TokenMatcher::Any to flexibly match verb or noun
- Two optional matchers handle た (past auxiliary) and の (nominalizer)
- This single pattern matches all three structure variants
- Register: polite

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)

**Tokenization observations**:
- 際 is always 名詞/非自立/副詞可能 (dependent noun)
- の when present is 助詞/連体化 (relativizing particle)
- Pattern successfully captures full construction from verb/noun through に

---

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ないことには～ない (unless, without)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: ないことには～ない is a 2-part structure expressing "unless (A), (B) will not happen". The first part uses ない (negative) + こと (nominalizer) + には (particle combination), and the second part (not explicitly matched) typically ends with ない or negative form. Literally means "within the situation of (A) not being a thing, neither will (B)".

**Structures tested**: All 4 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb[ない] + ことには: 被らないことには (chars 8-14) - "unless you wear"
2. い-Adjective[ない] + ことには: ないことには (chars 15-21) - "unless it is (thrilling)"
3. な-Adjective + でない + ことには: でないことには (chars 7-14) - "unless (it is earnest)"
4. Noun + でない + ことには: ないことには (chars 4-10) - "unless (there is a permit)"

**Implementation details**:
- Pattern structure: (optional で) + ない + こと(名詞/非自立) + に(格助詞) + は(係助詞)
- Custom matchers: DeAuxiliaryMatcher (で from だ), NaiMatcher (ない as auxiliary or adjective), KotoMatcher, NiParticleMatcher, WaParticleMatcher
- Optional で handles な-adjectives/nouns (でない) vs verbs/い-adjectives (ない)
- NaiMatcher accepts both 助動詞 (auxiliary) and 形容詞 (adjective) POS tags
- Register: standard

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)

**Tokenization observations**:
- Verb + ない: ない is 助動詞/特殊・ナイ (negative auxiliary)
- い-Adj (standalone ない): ない is 形容詞/自立 (independent adjective)
- な-Adj/Noun + でない: で(助動詞/だ) + ない(助動詞)
- こと is always 名詞/非自立/一般 (dependent noun)
- Pattern successfully matches all four structural variants

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: を機に (taking advantage of, on the occasion of)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: を機に uses 機（き） 'opportunity' with particles を and に to indicate "taking advantage of (A), (B)" or "on the occasion of (A), (B)". It's slightly formal and emphasizes actively making use of an opportunity to do something.

**Structures tested**: Both structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb[た] + の + を機に: 別れたのを機に (chars 5-10) - "taking advantage of the breakup"
2. Noun + を機に:
   - 妊娠を機に (chars 2-7) - "taking advantage of pregnancy"
   - 車検を機に (chars 0-5) - "on the occasion of car inspection"

**Implementation details**:
- Pattern structure: (Verb[た] or Noun) + (optional の nominalizer) + を(格助詞) + 機(名詞/一般) + に(格助詞)
- Custom matchers: WoParticleMatcher, KiNounMatcher, NiParticleMatcher, NoNominalizerMatcher
- Uses TokenMatcher::Any to flexibly match noun or verb+past
- Optional の nominalizer handles verb nominalization
- Register: standard

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: すら (even - extreme example)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 5

**Pattern**: すら is a formal adverbial particle meaning "even", more formal than さえ. It presents the most extreme example from a large group, implying that if this extreme case applies, all less extreme cases also apply. Usually used with negative nuance.

**Structures tested**: Single structure with multiple variations from grammar_points_data.json
1. Noun + すら: 鉛筆すら (chars 0-4) - "even a pencil"
2. Noun + で + すら: 子供ですら (chars 0-5) - "even a child"
3. Noun + に + すら: 彼にすら (chars 0-4) - "even to him"
4. Noun + すら + も: ガラケーすらも (chars 0-7) - "even a flip phone" (emphatic)

**Implementation details**:
- Pattern structure: Noun + (optional particle) + すら(係助詞) + (optional も)
- Custom matchers: SuraParticleMatcher, MoParticleMatcher, OptionalParticleMatcher
- OptionalParticleMatcher handles both に(格助詞) and で(助動詞 from だ)
- Uses TokenMatcher::Any to match any noun
- Two optional matchers for particle before すら and も after すら
- Register: formal

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)

**Key difference from さえ**: すら is more formal and emphasizes the most extreme example from a large group, while さえ places more emphasis on specific individuals.

### Pattern Implemented: を以て (by means of, with)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Category**: Construction

**Pattern**: をもって is a formal grammar pattern meaning "by means of", "with", or "via". Used with abstract concepts rather than concrete items (e.g., "with sincerity" not "with a hammer").

**Structures tested**: Single structure from grammar_points_data.json, but two tokenization variants:
1. **Compound particle** (を以て): をもって as single token (助詞/格助詞/連語)
   - 電話をもって (chars 6-12) - "by telephone"
2. **Split tokenization** (を以て_split): を + もつ(verb) + て
   - アプリをもって (chars 14-21) - "via the app"
   - 身をもって (chars 20-25) - "firsthand"

**Implementation details**:
- Two pattern variants registered to handle both tokenizations
- Compound: Noun + をもって (single token)
- Split: Noun + を(格助詞) + もつ(動詞) + て(接続助詞)
- Different tokenizations depend on context (compound particle vs verb "to hold")

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)

---

### Pattern Implemented: たところで (even if, even though)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Category**: Construction

**Pattern**: たところで combines Verb[た] + ところ + で to express "even if (A), not (B)" or "even if (A), it won't be (B)". Always followed by negative or unfavorable outcome in second clause.

**Structures tested**: Single structure from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb[た] + ところで:
   - 始めたところで (chars 5-12) - "even if start"
   - 急いだところで (chars 16-23) - "even if hurry"
   - 走ったところで (chars 2-9) - "even if run"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: flexible_verb_form() + past_auxiliary() + ところ(名詞/非自立) + で(格助詞)
- Past auxiliary can be た or だ depending on verb conjugation
- Reused existing helper functions (flexible_verb_form, past_auxiliary)
- Always indicates frustration about inevitable negative result

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)

---

### Pattern Implemented: ならまだしも (if A, that's fine, but B)

**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Category**: Construction

**Pattern**: ならまだしも is a fixed phrase combining なら (conditional) + まだ (still) + しも (classical adverbial particle). Separates acceptable scenario (A) from unacceptable scenario (B). Translates as "(A) is fine, but not (B)".

**Structures tested**: Single structure from grammar_points_data.json
1. Phrase + ならまだしも:
   - ならまだしも (chars 4-10) - all three test sentences
   - Pattern detects just the fixed phrase ならまだしも

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: なら(助動詞/仮定形) + まだしも(副詞)
- なら: base_form="だ", features[5]="仮定形" (not features[4]!)
- まだしも: tokenized as single adverb token
- Classical particle しも strengthens まだ
- **Important learning**: features[5] is conjugation form, not features[4]

**Test count**: 3 tests (all passing)

**Technical note**: Initially tried starting with TokenMatcher::Any but it didn't work. Pattern matching engine needs concrete matchers to anchor the search. Fixed by matching just the fixed phrase ならまだしも.

**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 1

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: Noun + という (助詞/格助詞/連語) + Noun
- Custom matcher checks surface == "という" AND pos == "助詞/格助詞"
- Uses shared noun_matcher() helper from mod.rs
- Pattern range includes full construction: Noun (A) + という + Noun (B)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: てからでないと (unless you do, until you do)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 1 (low priority - detected but not prominently highlighted)

**Pattern**: てからでないと combines the て-form sequence (てから "after doing"), で (copula auxiliary だ in 連用形), ない (negative auxiliary), and と/ば (conditional particles). Translates as "unless (A), (B) will not happen" or "until (A), you cannot (B)". Emphasizes that (B) must come after (A) is completed.

**Structures tested**: Both structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb[て] + からでないと: 揃ってからでないと (chars 6-15), してからでないと (chars 12-20)
2. Verb[て] + からでなければ: とってからでなければ (chars 3-13), 相談してからでなければ (chars 11-22)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 2619-2687), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 5597-5652)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: Verb (連用形/連用タ接続) + て/で(接続助詞) + から(格助詞) + で(助動詞/だ) + ない/なけれ(助動詞) + と/ば(接続助詞)
- Custom matchers: TeDeParticleMatcher, KaraAfterMatcher, DeAuxiliaryMatcher, NaiNakerebaMatcher, ToOrBaMatcher
- Handles both conditional forms: でないと (with と) and でなければ (with ば)
- Extension of base てから pattern with negative conditional
- Register: standard

**Tokenization observations**:
- **でないと form**: て/で(助詞/接続助詞) + から(助詞/格助詞) + で(助動詞/特殊・ダ) + ない(助動詞/基本形) + と(助詞/接続助詞)
- **でなければ form**: て/で(助詞/接続助詞) + から(助詞/格助詞) + で(助動詞/特殊・ダ) + なけれ(助動詞/仮定形) + ば(助詞/接続助詞)

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from verb through conditional particle
- All 4 test cases verified with correct character ranges
- Pattern successfully matches both と and ば conditional forms

**Related patterns**:
- てから (N5): Base pattern "after doing"
- てからというもの (N2): "ever since" (emphatic variant)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: 次第だ・次第で (depending on, depends on)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: 次第だ・次第で uses しだい (noun suffix meaning "directly following" or "depending on") attached to a noun, followed by だ/です (copula) or で (particle). Translates as "depending on (A), (B)" or "(B) depends on (A)". The noun (A) represents a person or situation that controls what happens next.

**Structures tested**: All 4 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Noun + しだい + だ: 努力しだいだ (chars 15-21) - "depends on effort"
2. Noun + しだい + です: 結果しだいです (chars 17-24) - "depends on results" (polite)
3. Noun + しだい + で: 日しだいで (chars 7-12), 値段しだいで (chars 0-6) - "depending on (date/price)"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 2471-2513), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 5654-5711)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: Noun + しだい(名詞/接尾/一般) + だ/です(助動詞) OR で(助詞/格助詞)
- Custom matchers: ShidaiMatcher (しだい as noun suffix), DaDesuDeMatcher (handles だ, です, or で)
- Simple three-token pattern with flexible ending (だ/です/で)
- The noun (A) indicates what the outcome depends on
- Register: standard

**Tokenization observations**:
- **だ form**: Noun + しだい(名詞/接尾/一般) + だ(助動詞/特殊・ダ/基本形)
- **です form**: Noun + しだい(名詞/接尾/一般) + です(助動詞/特殊・デス/基本形)
- **で form**: Noun + しだい(名詞/接尾/一般) + で(助詞/格助詞)

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from noun through ending particle/auxiliary
- All 4 test cases verified with correct character ranges
- Pattern successfully matches all three ending variants (だ, です, で)

**Related patterns**:
- 次第に (N2): "Gradually, bit by bit" (adverb usage)
- 次第です (N2): "Due to that, as a result of that"

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: よりしかたがない (there is no choice but, cannot be helped)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: よりしかたがない is a set phrase combining より (comparison particle), 仕方 (しかた) 'way/method', が (subject marker, optional), and ない (negative). Translates as "there is no choice but (A)", "cannot be helped", or "(A) cannot be avoided". Expresses that given the situation, there is no alternative to doing (A).

**Structures tested**: Both structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb + より + 仕方がない: 立て直すよりしかたがない (chars 36-48) - "no choice but to rebuild"
2. Verb + より + 仕方ない: 働くよりしかたない (chars 15-24) - "no choice but to work" (が dropped)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 2534-2597), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 5566-5595)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: Verb(基本形) + より(格助詞) + しかた(名詞/ナイ形容詞語幹) + (が)(optional) + ない(形容詞 or 助動詞)
- Custom matchers: YoriParticleMatcher, ShikataMatcher, GaParticleMatcher (optional), NaiMatcher
- Handles both forms: with が (しかたがない) and without (しかたない)
- NaiMatcher accepts both 形容詞 and 助動詞 POS tags for ない
- Related pattern: よりほかにない (similar meaning using 他 instead of 仕方)
- Register: standard

**Tokenization observations**:
- **With が**: 立て直す(動詞/基本形) + より(助詞/格助詞) + しかた(名詞/ナイ形容詞語幹) + が(助詞/格助詞) + ない(形容詞)
- **Without が**: 働く(動詞/基本形) + より(助詞/格助詞) + しかた(名詞/ナイ形容詞語幹) + ない(助動詞/特殊・ナイ)

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from verb through ない
- Both test cases verified with correct character ranges
- Optional が handled via TokenMatcher::Optional

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: にもかかわらず (despite, in spite of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: にもかかわらず is a formal grammar structure combining に, も, the verb かかわる 'to be affected', and the literary auxiliary verb ず (negative, equivalent to ない). Translates as "in spite of (A)", "despite (A)", conveying that (B) happens without consideration for (A).

**Structures tested**: All 4 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb + の + にもかかわらず: たのにもかかわらず (chars 15-24) - "despite working"
2. い-Adjective + の + にもかかわらず: 悪いのにもかかわらず (chars 2-12) - "despite being bad"
3. な-Adjective + である + にもかかわらず: 親切であるにもかかわらず (chars 9-21) - "despite being kind"
4. Noun + にもかかわらず: 深夜にもかかわらず (chars 0-9) - "despite late at night"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 2312-2417), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 5507-5565)

**Key notes**:
- Core structure: に(格助詞) + も(係助詞) + かかわら(動詞/未然形, base=かかわる) + ず(助動詞/特殊・ヌ, base=ぬ)
- Custom matchers: NoNominalizerMatcher (の/名詞/非自立), DeAruSequenceMatcher (で from だ), AruAuxiliaryMatcher, NiParticleMatcher, MoParticleMatcher, KakawaruMatcher, ZuAuxiliaryMatcher
- Uses TokenMatcher::Any for flexible verb/adjective/noun matching
- Optional の nominalizer for verbs and adjectives
- Optional である for na-adjectives
- Register: formal

**Tokenization observations**:
- **Verb**: た(助動詞) + の(名詞/非自立) + に(助詞/格助詞) + も(助詞/係助詞) + かかわら(動詞/未然形) + ず(助動詞/特殊・ヌ)
- **i-Adjective**: 悪い(形容詞) + の(名詞/非自立) + に(助詞/格助詞) + も(助詞/係助詞) + かかわら(動詞/未然形) + ず(助動詞)
- **na-Adjective**: 親切(名詞/形容動詞語幹) + で(助動詞/特殊・ダ) + ある(助動詞) + に(助詞/格助詞) + も(助詞/係助詞) + かかわら(動詞/未然形) + ず(助動詞)
- **Noun**: 深夜(名詞) + に(助詞/格助詞) + も(助詞/係助詞) + かかわら(動詞/未然形) + ず(助動詞)

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction including preceding element through ず
- All 4 test cases verified with correct character ranges
- Pattern successfully matches all structure variants

**Difference from に関わらず**:
- にもかかわらず: "despite, in spite of" - emphasizes contradiction
- に関わらず (not yet implemented): "regardless of, no matter if" - emphasizes independence

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ところだった ② (was just about to, was in the middle of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 1 (low priority - detected but not highlighted prominently)

**Pattern**: When combined with the dictionary form of verbs, ところだった indicates that something was about to happen, and will regularly be translated as 'was just about to do (A)', or 'was in the middle of (A)'. In many cases, ところだった ② indicates either a positive or negative result that came about from 'not doing (A)'.

**Structures tested**: Both structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Standard: Verb[る] + ところだった - ひくところだった (chars 13-21), 遅刻するところだった (chars 13-23), 出るところだった (chars 5-13)
2. Polite: Verb[る] + ところでした - 乗り遅れるところでした (chars 15-26)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 2833-2869), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 5367-5429)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: Verb (基本形) + ところ (名詞/非自立) + だった/でした (助動詞)
- Custom matchers: TokoroMatcher (checks surface="ところ", pos=名詞/非自立), DaDattaMatcher (checks だっ/でし + past auxiliary)
- Uses TokenMatcher::Any to match any verb in 基本形
- Identical structure to ところだった ① (N3), but N2 version emphasizes the result from not doing (A)
- Register: standard

**Tokenization observations**:
- **Standard**: ひく(動詞/基本形) + ところ(名詞/非自立) + だっ(助動詞/特殊・ダ/連用タ接続) + た(助動詞/特殊・タ/基本形)
- **Polite**: 乗り遅れる(動詞/基本形) + ところ(名詞/非自立) + でし(助動詞/特殊・デス/連用形) + た(助動詞/特殊・タ/基本形)

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from verb through だった/でした
- All 4 test cases verified with correct character ranges
- Pattern detected in both standard and polite forms

**Difference from ところだった ①**:
- Structurally identical pattern definition
- ところだった ① (N3): emphasizes that something almost happened but didn't
- ところだった ② (N2): emphasizes the result/consequence from not doing the action
- Both patterns will match the same grammatical constructions

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: どころではない (far from, out of the question)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: どころではない is a set phrase that highlights that something is 'far from (A)', or that '(A) is out of the question'. These translations appear when the actual situation is considered to be even more remarkable than the phrase that どころではない is attached to. May also be translated as 'it is not the time for (A)' when there are far more important matters.

**Structures tested**: All 4 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Standard: Phrase + どころ + ではない - 痛いどころではない (chars 3-15), 仕事どころではない (chars 24-33)
2. Casual: Phrase + どころ + じゃない - 取るどころじゃない (chars 17-26), 結婚どころじゃない (chars 20-29)
3. Polite: Phrase + どころ + ではありません - 休暇どころではありません (chars 7-19)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 2871-2936), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 5431-5505)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: Phrase + どころ (名詞/非自立) + (で/じゃ) + (は) + (ない/あり+ません)
- Custom matchers: DokoroMatcher (どころ as 名詞/非自立), DeJaMatcher (で from だ or じゃ casual), HaParticleMatcher (は particle), NegativeEndingMatcher (ない or あり for polite)
- Uses TokenMatcher::Any to match any preceding phrase (verb, adjective, or noun)
- Optional は particle (present in ではない, absent in じゃない)
- For polite form (ではありません), matcher catches up to あり, then continues through ません
- Register: standard

**Tokenization observations**:
- **Standard**: どころ(名詞/非自立) + で(助動詞/特殊・ダ/連用形) + は(助詞/係助詞) + ない(形容詞/基本形)
- **Casual**: どころ(名詞/非自立) + じゃ(助詞/副助詞) + ない(助動詞/特殊・ナイ/基本形)
- **Polite**: どころ(名詞/非自立) + で(助動詞/連用形) + は(助詞/係助詞) + あり(動詞/連用形) + ませ(助動詞/未然形) + ん(助動詞/基本形)

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from phrase through negative ending
- All 5 test cases verified with correct character ranges
- Pattern detected in standard, casual, and polite forms
- Note: Pattern may extend to include following auxiliary verbs (e.g., らしい) in some cases due to pattern aggregation

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: つつある (to be -ing, in the process of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: つつある combines the particle つつ 'while doing' with the verb ある 'to be' to indicate ongoing gradual change or process. Translates as "to be -ing" with emphasis on slow, continuous change (increase, decrease, expansion, contraction). More formal than ている and typically not used for physical actions with obvious endpoints.

**Structures tested**: Both structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Standard: Verb[stem] + つつある - 減りつつある (chars 10-16), 上がりつつある (chars 7-14)
2. Polite: Verb[stem] + つつあります - 減りつつあります (chars 32-40)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 2622-2641), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 5271-5318)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: Verb (連用形) + つつ (助詞/接続助詞) + ある (動詞)
- Custom matcher: TsutsuParticleMatcher (checks surface="つつ", base="つつ", pos=助詞/接続助詞)
- Uses TokenMatcher::specific_verb("ある") for the ある verb
- Works with both 基本形 (ある) and 連用形 (あり) conjugations
- Emphasis on gradual change processes, not physical actions
- Register: formal

**Tokenization observations**:
- **Standard**: 減り(動詞/連用形) + つつ(助詞/接続助詞) + ある(動詞/基本形)
- **Polite**: 減り(動詞/連用形) + つつ(助詞/接続助詞) + あり(動詞/連用形) + ます(助動詞)

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from verb stem through ある/あります
- All 3 test cases verified with correct character ranges

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ていては (if you keep doing, if one continues with)

**JLPT Level**: N2

**Pattern**: ていては expresses "if one continues with (A), (B)" where (B) is typically a negative outcome. Combines Verb[て] + いる (in 連用形) + て + は. The final ては is a conjunctive structure expressing hypothetical situations or repetitive actions.

**Structures tested**: 1 structure variant from grammar_points_data.json
1. Standard: Verb[て] + いては - 食べていては (chars 9-15), 遊んでいては (chars 2-8), していては (chars 10-15)

- Custom matchers: TeDeParticleMatcher (て/で after verb), IRuyoukeiMatcher (い in 連用形), TeParticleMatcher (て after いる), HaParticleMatcher (は topic marker)
- Uses flexible_verb_form() to handle both 連用形 and 連用タ接続
- Works with both て and で variants (depending on verb type)
- Indicates continuing action leading to negative consequence

- **で variant**: 遊ん(動詞/連用タ接続) + で(助詞/接続助詞) + い(動詞/非自立/連用形) + て(助詞/接続助詞) + は(助詞/係助詞)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: も構わず (without worrying about, without minding)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: も構（かま）わず is an expression that combines も, the う-Verb 構（かま）う 'to mind' or 'to care', and the literary auxiliary verb ず (negative form, equivalent to ない). Common translations include "without worrying about (A), (B)", "without minding (A), (B)", "regardless of (A), (B)". Used to express that (A) is something being ignored or that the speaker is not worried about.

**Structures tested**: All 5 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb + の + も + かまわず: いるのもかまわず (chars 14-22) - "without worrying that (verb)"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 2104-2173), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 5192-5269)

**Key notes**:
- Core pattern: かまわ (動詞/自立/未然形, base=かまう) + ず (助動詞/特殊・ヌ, base=ぬ)
- Custom matchers: NoNominalizerMatcher (の as 名詞/非自立), NiParticleMatcher, MoParticleMatcher, KamawaMatcher, ZuAuxiliaryMatcher
- TokenMatcher::Any handles verb/adjective/noun flexibility

- Optional に for emphatic にも variant (both にも and も are supported)
- Register: standard/formal

### Technical Notes

**Flexible pattern matching with TokenMatcher::Any**:
- Used TokenMatcher::Any to match any verb, adjective, or noun
- Optional の (名詞/非自立) for nominalization of verbs/adjectives
- Optional に (助詞/格助詞) for emphatic にも variant
- Required も (助詞/係助詞) - the key particle
- かまわ (動詞/未然形) + ず (助動詞/特殊・ヌ) - core negative construction

**Literary auxiliary verb ず**:
- ず is the classical/literary negative form (equivalent to ない)
- Base form is ぬ (助動詞/特殊・ヌ)
- Attaches to verbs in 未然形 (imperfective form)
- Used in formal/written contexts

**Pattern matching strategy**:
- Start with TokenMatcher::Any for maximum flexibility
- Use Optional matchers for の and に to handle variants
- Specific matchers for も, かまわ, ず ensure correct detection
- All 5 structure variants work with single matcher definition

### Implementation Approach
- Followed test-first workflow strictly
- Wrote all 5 tests with print_debug first
- Analyzed actual tokenization from Kagome for each variant
- Built matcher based on observed token structure (not assumptions)
- Removed print_debug and added assertions after verification
- Verified no regressions before committing

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: に気をつける (be careful of, watch out for, pay attention to)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: に気をつける is a collocation based on 気（き） 'attention/focus', combining the noun 気, を particle, and the verb 付ける 'to attach/adhere'. Used to mark something as requiring attention - "to pay attention to (A)", "to be careful of (A)", "to watch out for (A)".

**Structures tested**: 2 structure variants (2 of 4 from grammar_points_data.json)
1. Noun + にきをつける: 人にきをつけ (chars 21-27) - "watch out for people"
2. Noun + にきをつけます: 者にきをつけましょう (chars 13-23) - "be careful of pedestrians"

**Undetectable structures** (documented in test file with TODO):
- Verb[ない] + ように気をつける: Pattern matcher's wildcard mechanism doesn't reliably match the intervening tokens (ない + よう) between verb and に. Would require more sophisticated pattern matcher or separate pattern definition.

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 2034-2102), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 4907-4972)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: Noun + に(格助詞) + き(動詞, くる) + を(格助詞) + つける(動詞)
- Custom matchers: NounOrVerbMatcher (excludes よう), NiParticleMatcher, KiVerbMatcher, WoParticleMatcher, TsukeruVerbMatcher
- Kagome tokenizes 気 (attention) as き with base_form=くる (verb "to come")
- Pattern successfully matches basic noun form
- Register: standard

**Tokenization observations**:
- Noun: 人(名詞) + に(助詞/格助詞) + き(動詞, base=くる, 連用形) + を(助詞/格助詞) + つけ(動詞, base=つける)
- Verb form has intervening tokens that complicate matching: Verb + ない + よう + に + き + を + つける

**Pattern ranges**: From noun through つける verb (conjugation suffix may or may not be included)
- 人にきをつけ: chars 21-27 (without て)
- 者にきをつけましょう: chars 13-23 (includes ましょう)

**Limitations**:
- Simple matcher handles Noun + にきをつける well
- Verb[ない] + ように form requires wildcard between verb and に, which doesn't work reliably with current pattern matcher
- Future enhancement: Could split into two separate patterns or improve wildcard matching

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: に向かって・に向けて (towards, facing, aimed at)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: に向かって (using intransitive verb 向かう "to face") indicates an action done "towards (A)" or "facing (A)", where (A) is usually a stationary object or destination. に向けて (using transitive verb 向ける "to direct") implies intentionally "aiming" or "facing" something towards (A), especially to make it "suitable" for (A).

**Structures tested**: 3 structure variants with 4 comprehensive tests

3. Noun + に向けて + の + Noun: (covered by structure 2 tests)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 1923-1966), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 4851-4905)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: Noun + に(格助詞) + (むかう OR むける)(動詞) + て(接続助詞)
- Custom matchers: NiParticleMatcher, MukauMukeruVerbMatcher, TeParticleMatcher
- むかう (intransitive): facing/going towards a direction
- むける (transitive): intentionally directing/aiming at something
- Both verbs work with same grammar pattern
- Register: standard

**Tokenization observations**:
- **に向かって**: に (助詞/格助詞) + むかっ (動詞, base='むかう', 連用タ接続) + て (助詞/接続助詞)
- **に向けて**: に (助詞/格助詞) + むけ (動詞, base='むける', 連用形) + て (助詞/接続助詞)

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from noun through て particle
- All 4 test cases verified with correct character ranges
- Covers both intransitive (向かう) and transitive (向ける) forms

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: つつ (while doing, in the course of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 1 (low priority - still detected but not highlighted prominently)

**Pattern**: つつ is a formal particle that appears after the ます-stem (連用形) of verbs to indicate that (B) is happening at the same time as (A). Translates as "while (A), (B)". More formal than ながら and typically used with ongoing states rather than physical actions.

**Structures tested**: 1 structure with 3 comprehensive tests
1. while knowing: 知りつつ (chars 6-10)
2. while looking: 見つつ (chars 12-15)
3. while thinking: 考えつつ (chars 10-14)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 2280-2300), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 4719-4761)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: Verb (連用形) + つつ (助詞/接続助詞)
- Custom TsutsuParticleMatcher checks surface="つつ", base="つつ", pos=助詞/接続助詞
- More formal than ながら, used primarily in writing
- Best used with non-physical actions (knowing, looking, thinking) rather than physical movements
- Register: formal

**Tokenization observations**:
- Verb: 連用形 (stem form)
- つつ: surface="つつ", base="つつ", pos=助詞/接続助詞

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from verb stem through つつ particle
- All test cases verified with correct character ranges

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: つつ(も) (even while doing, although doing)

**JLPT Level**: N2

**Pattern**: Extension of つつ with the addition of も particle, adding the nuance of "even" or "although". Translates as "even while doing (A), (B)" or "although doing (A), (B)". (B) is almost always contradictory to (A).

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: に伴って・に伴い (along with, in conjunction with, due to)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: に伴って・に伴い comes from the verb 伴う (to accompany) and is used to show that something happens in conjunction with or as a result of something else. Translates to "along with", "in conjunction with", "due to", or "accompanying". Can be used with both verbs and nouns, with optional の nominalizer after verbs.

**Structures tested**: 4 main structure variants with 8 comprehensive tests:
1. Verb[る] + に伴って: 減少するに伴って (chars 7-15)
2. Verb[る] + の + に伴って: するのに伴って (chars 9-16)
3. Verb[る] + に伴い: 取るに伴って (chars 2-8)
4. Verb[る] + の + に伴う + Noun: するのに伴う手順 (chars 9-15)
5. Noun + に伴って: 普及に伴って (chars 8-14)
6. Noun + に伴い: 現象に伴い (chars 2-7), 地震に伴い (chars 3-8)
7. Noun + に伴う + Noun: 工事に伴う車線規制 (chars 17-22)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 1699-1780), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 4609-4718)

**Key notes**:
- Matches Verb(基本形) or Noun + (optional の) + に + 伴う verb + (optional て)
- Custom matchers for: NounOrVerbMatcher, NiParticleMatcher, TomonauVerbMatcher, TeParticleMatcher, NoNominalizerMatcher
- The verb 伴う appears in three forms: 伴って (連用タ接続 + て), 伴い (連用形), 伴う (基本形)
- Optional の nominalizer allows "Verb + の" nominalization before に
- Register: standard/formal

**Tokenization observations**:
- Noun + に伴って: 普及(名詞) + に(助詞/格助詞) + 伴っ(動詞/連用タ接続) + て(助詞/接続助詞)
- Verb + に伴い: 取る(動詞/基本形) + に(助詞/格助詞) + 伴い(動詞/連用形)
- Verb + の + に伴う: する(動詞/基本形) + の(名詞/非自立) + に(助詞/格助詞) + 伴う(動詞/基本形)

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from Noun/Verb to end of 伴って/伴い/伴う
- All 8 test cases verified with correct character ranges
- Covers progressive change contexts (歳を取るに伴って) and momentary events (地震に伴い)

**Key notes**:
- Verb stem + たまえ (imperative form)
- Kagome inconsistently tokenizes たまえ
- Can only detect when correctly tokenized as: たまえ(動詞/命令ｅ/base=たまう)
- Custom matcher checks surface="たまえ", base_form="たまう", pos=動詞, conjugation=命令ｅ
- Register: formal/polite (but hierarchical)
- Gender: primarily men only

**Tokenization observations**:
- Correct: surface='たまえ', base='たまう', pos=動詞/自立, features[5]='命令ｅ'

- KagirazuVerbMatcher checks for 未然形 (for ず-form)
- Both check base_form == "限る" to ensure same verb root

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: た末・の末 (after, as a result of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: Appearing with verbs in their past form, or grouped with nouns after の is 末（すえ）, a word meaning 'end tip'. に will follow 末（すえ）, marking the 'tip' as the point from which something will result. This pattern expresses that various things happened along the way, and then finally reached the 'point' or 'conclusion', often after long-lasting effort or struggle. Usually translated as 'after (A), (B)' or 'as a result of (A), (B)'.

**Structures tested**: 2 comprehensive tests covering all variants
1. Verb[た] + すえ (に): 考えたすえに (3-9), 迷ったすえ (3-8) - "after much thought/hesitation"
2. Noun + の + すえ (に): 争いのすえ (7-12), 議論のすえに (5-11) - "after the conflict/discussion"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 1579-1643), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 3962-4017)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern uses TokenMatcher::Any to match either Verb (連用形/連用タ接続) or Noun
- Custom matcher VerbTaOrNounNoMatcher checks for EITHER た (助動詞) OR の (助詞/連体化)
- すえ always tokenizes as 名詞/非自立/一般
- Optional に particle at the end (格助詞)
- Pattern includes full construction: (Verb+た or Noun+の) + すえ + optional に

**Tokenization observations**:
- **Verb pattern**: 考え (動詞/連用形) + た (助動詞) + すえ (名詞/非自立) + に (optional)
- **Noun pattern**: 議論 (名詞) + の (助詞/連体化) + すえ (名詞/非自立) + に (optional)
- すえ always has POS tags: 名詞/非自立/一般

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from verb/noun through optional に
- 考えたすえに: includes verb stem + た + すえ + に
- 迷ったすえ: includes verb stem + た + すえ (without に)
- 争いのすえ: includes noun + の + すえ
- 議論のすえに: includes noun + の + すえ + に

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: にわたって (across, throughout, over the period of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: Partnered with the case marking particle に, the う-Verb 渡（わた）る 'to cross' in its て-form highlights a span of something in regard to breadth, size, or time. This structure is often translated as either 'across (A)', 'throughout (A)', 'extending through (A)', or 'over the period of (A)'.

**Structures tested**: 4 comprehensive tests covering all variants
1. Noun + にわたって: 長年にわたって (5-12) - "over many years"
2. Noun + にわたる + Noun: 年にわたる (2-7) - "lasting 15 years"
3. Noun + にわたり: 日間にわたり (4-10) - "continuing for 3 days"
4. Noun + にわたった: 年間にわたった (1-8) - "lasted for 10 years"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 1476-1526), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 3872-3918)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern handles TWO tokenization variants:
  * Single-token compound particles: にわたって, にわたる, にわたり (助詞/格助詞/連語)
  * Multi-token form: に + わたる verb (e.g., にわたった = に + わたっ + た)
- Combined matcher checks both cases in one custom matcher
- Optional わたる verb matcher for multi-token form
- Pattern includes full construction from noun through particle/verb

**Tokenization observations**:
- **Single tokens**: にわたって/にわたる/にわたり tokenize as 助詞/格助詞/連語
- **Multi-token**: に (助詞/格助詞) + わたっ (動詞/自立, base='わたる') + た (助動詞)
- All forms express the same meaning with different conjugations

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from noun through particle/verb form
- にわたって: 長年にわたって (includes noun + compound particle)
- にわたる: 年にわたる (includes noun + compound particle)
- にわたり: 日間にわたり (includes noun + compound particle)
- にわたった: 年間にわたった (includes noun + に + わたっ + た)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: に沿って (along, in accordance with, in line with)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: When used with the case marking particle に, the う-Verb 沿（そ）う 'to run alongside' in its て-form expresses that something is going 'along with (A)', 'in accordance with (A)', or 'in line with (A)'. This is primarily in relation to laws, conventions, traditions, needs, and other things which are not usually deviated away from.

**Structures tested**: 2 of 3 structures detectable
1. Noun + にそって: ルールにそって (3-10) - "in accordance with the rules"
2. Noun + にそった + Noun: 希望にそった (10-16) - "in accordance with wishes"
3. **UNDETECTABLE**: Noun + にそう (dictionary form) - tokenization ambiguity

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 1528-1577), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 3920-3961)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern matches: Noun + に + そう verb + Optional(て/た)
- Handles にそって (て-form) and にそった (た-form) reliably
- Dictionary form にそう is UNDETECTABLE due to tokenization issue
- The verb そう (to follow along) tokenizes correctly in conjugated forms
- In dictionary form, Kagome misidentifies そう as an adverb instead of the verb

**Tokenization observations**:
- **にそって**: に (助詞/格助詞) + そっ (動詞/自立, base='そう') + て (助詞/接続助詞)
- **にそった**: に (助詞/格助詞) + そっ (動詞/自立, base='そう') + た (助動詞)
- **にそう** (problematic): に (助詞/格助詞) + そう (副詞/助詞類接続) - NOT detected as verb!

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from noun through て/た
- にそって: ルールにそって (includes noun + に + そっ + て)
- にそった: 希望にそった (includes noun + に + そっ + た)

**Undetectable case explanation**:
The dictionary form "にそう" creates tokenization ambiguity. In the sentence "希望にそう方向で", Kagome tokenizes "そう" as an adverb (副詞) rather than the verb 沿う. This is because "そう" can be either:
- The verb 沿う (to follow along) - what we want to detect
- The auxiliary そう (seems like, appears) - more common usage

Without conjugation markers (て/た), Kagome defaults to interpreting it as the auxiliary, making the pattern undetectable in dictionary form. Only the conjugated forms にそって and にそった can be reliably detected.

**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: One of the more indirect ways that 'to be concerning (A)' is conveyed in Japanese is through the pairing of a noun with を and the verb 巡（めぐ）る 'to go around'. This pattern can be translated as 'concerning (A)', 'in regard to (A)', or 'about (A)'. Following the natural translation: 'to go around the topic of (A)', or 'encompassing (A)'. This pattern keeps the 'around the topic of (A)' nuance and takes into consideration many different viewpoints. Frequently used when there is a disagreement or dispute about (A).

**Structures tested**: 3 comprehensive tests covering all variants
1. Noun + をめぐって: 境界線をめぐって (8-14) - "concerning the property line"
2. Noun + をめぐる + Noun: 島をめぐる争い (2-7) - "conflict concerning the island"
3. Noun + をめぐって + の + Noun: ルールをめぐってのトラブル (4-12) - "trouble concerning rules"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 1432-1474), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 3835-3870)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: Noun + (をめぐって | を + めぐる)
- Handles two tokenization variants:
  * Single token: をめぐって (助詞/格助詞/連語)
  * Two tokens: を (助詞/格助詞) + めぐる (動詞/自立)
- Flexible matching using Optional(めぐる) to handle both cases

**Tokenization observations**:
- **Single token**: surface="をめぐって", base="をめぐって", pos=助詞/格助詞/連語
- **Two tokens**: を (助詞/格助詞/一般) + めぐる (動詞/自立/五段・ラ行)
- めぐる verb appears in various forms: めぐる, めぐって, めぐり

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from noun through をめぐって/をめぐる
- をめぐって: 境界線をめぐって, ルールをめぐって
- をめぐる: 島をめぐる (includes noun + を + めぐる)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: その上 (besides, in addition to, furthermore)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: その上（うえ） is a formal expression used in Japanese to indicate that something is 'besides', 'in addition to', or 'furthermore' in relation to the previous statement. It primarily comes at the beginning of sentences before reflecting back on and adding new information in regard to what has already been said. The literal meaning can be thought of as 'on top of that (B)'.

**Structures tested**: 3 comprehensive tests covering usage contexts
1. その上 at sentence beginning with comma: その上、 (13-16) - "Furthermore, ..."
2. その上 mid-conversation: その上 (17-20) - "In addition, ..."
3. その上 after negative statement: その上、 (14-17) - "Besides that, ..."

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 1236-1269), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 3669-3713)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern structure: その (連体詞) + 上 (名詞/非自立/副詞可能)
- Simple two-token conjunction pattern
- Despite being formal, also used in daily speech due to simplicity
- Always tokenizes as two separate tokens: その + 上

**Tokenization observations**:
- **その**: surface="その", base="その", pos=連体詞 (demonstrative)
- **上**: surface="上", base="上", pos=名詞/非自立/副詞可能 (non-independent noun, adverbial)
- Pattern is always 3 characters long (その上)

**Pattern ranges**: Always just その上 (3 characters)
- Standard: その上 (regardless of following punctuation)

**Edge case note**: Without proper punctuation (e.g., "その上家" without comma), Kagome may merge "上家" as a single noun token (うわや), preventing detection. All tests use proper punctuation with commas for clarity.

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: 以上② (since, now that, as long as)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 1 (low priority - still detected and functional, just not highlighted prominently)

**Pattern**: When combined with the adverbial particle は, 以上（いじょう） is frequently translated as 'since (A), (B)', 'now that (A), (B)', or 'as long as (A), (B)'. This is a formal structure rarely found in daily speech. The literal meaning is 'above and beyond (A), (B)' - highlighting that anything past, beyond, or above (A) is what needs to be considered.

**Structures tested**: 6 comprehensive tests covering all variants
1. Verb + 以上は: 決めた以上は (7-11) - "now that I've decided"
2. Verb + 以上 (without は): 選ばれた以上 (9-12) - "since I was chosen"
3. い-Adjective + 以上は: 悪い以上は (3-8) - "as long as you're sick"
4. な-Adjective + である + 以上は: ある以上は (3-8 from 有名である以上は) - "as long as you're famous"
5. Noun + である + 以上は: ある以上は (8-13 from 住民である以上は) - "as long as you're a resident"
6. Noun + である + 以上 (without は): ある以上 (16-20 from 人間である以上) - "as long as you're human"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 972-1026), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 3391-3461)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern matches: (Verb|い-Adj|である's ある) + 以上 + Optional(は)
- 以上 tokenizes as 名詞/非自立/副詞可能
- は (when present) tokenizes as 助詞/係助詞
- である structure: で (助動詞/特殊・ダ/連用形) + ある (助動詞/五段・ラ行アル/基本形)
- Pattern includes full grammatical construction from verb/adjective through は (if present)

**Tokenization observations**:
- **以上**: surface="以上", base="以上", pos=名詞/非自立/副詞可能
- **Verb form**: Any verb (決めた, 選ばれた) + 以上
- **い-Adj form**: 形容詞/自立/基本形 (悪い) + 以上
- **である form**: で (助動詞/連用形) + ある (助動詞/基本形) + 以上
- **Optional は**: surface="は", pos=助詞/係助詞

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from preceding element through 以上 and optional は
- Verb+は: た以上は (includes past auxiliary + 以上 + は)
- Verb only: た以上 (includes past auxiliary + 以上)
- Adj+は: 悪い以上は (adjective + 以上 + は)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: まい (negative volition auxiliary verb)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: The auxiliary verb まい expresses negative volition - the opposite of う/よう. It means "won't (A)", "intend not to (A)", or "probably not (A)". It's a condensed way of expressing ～ないだろう. Considered slightly formal.

**Structures tested**: 6 comprehensive tests covering all variants
1. Verb[基本形] + まい: 行くまい (17-21) - "probably won't go"
2. する → し + まい: しまい (23-26) - stem form variant
3. する → す + まい: すまい (8-11) - classical stem form variant
4. 来る[基本形] + まい: 来るまい (27-31) - standard form
5. 来る → こ + まい: こまい (27-30) - stem form variant
6. Verb[連用形] + ます + まい: 住みますまい (20-26) - polite form

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 853-891), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 3168-3238)

**Key notes**:
- Three token patterns: Verb + Optional(ます) + まい
- まい always tokenizes as 助動詞, base_form='まい', 不変化型
- Handles all verb forms: 基本形 (dictionary), 未然形 (stem), 連用形+ます (polite)
- Special handling for する (し/す variants) and 来る (こ variant)
- Pattern includes full grammatical construction (verb through まい)

**Tokenization observations**:
- Basic: Verb[基本形] + まい(助動詞) - 行くまい, 来るまい
- Stem: Verb[未然形] + まい(助動詞) - しまい, すまい, こまい
  * し: base='する', 未然形
  * す: base='する', 文語基本形
  * こ: base='くる', 未然形
- Polite: Verb[連用形] + ます(助動詞) + まい(助動詞) - 住みますまい

**Pattern ranges**: Full construction from verb through まい
- Standard: 行くまい (verb + auxiliary), 来るまい
- Stem forms: しまい, すまい, こまい
- Polite: 住みますまい (verb + ます + まい)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: それなら (if that's the case, then)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: それなら is a conjunction used when replying to a statement to express "if that's the case, then (B)". It strengthens conditional responses and often precedes suggestions, considerations, or guesses based on what the speaker has just heard.

**Structures tested**: 3 tests covering all usage contexts
1. それなら + Phrase (mid-sentence): それなら私を駅まで送ってくれない？ (16-20)
2. それなら at sentence start: それなら彼でもできるようだ (0-4)
3. それなら with suggestion: それなら先生に聞いたほうがいいと思います (0-4)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 672-686), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 3028-3070)

**Key notes**:
- Tokenizes as single conjunction token: surface="それなら", base="それなら", pos=接続詞
- Simple single-token matcher checking surface, base_form, and POS
- Pattern range: just それなら (4 characters)
- **Important**: The variants だったら and それだったら mentioned in grammar_points_data.json are already detected by the existing たら conditional pattern, as they have different structure (copula だ + conditional auxiliary たら)

**Tokenization observations**:
- それなら: surface="それなら", base="それなら", pos=接続詞
- だったら: だっ (base='だ', 助動詞, 連用タ接続) + たら (base='た', 助動詞, 仮定形)
- それだったら: それ (名詞/代名詞) + だっ + たら
- Only それなら is detected by this pattern; the だったら variants are structurally different conditionals

**Pattern ranges**: Full conjunction token
- Standard: それなら (4 characters)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ものなら① (if one could / if it were possible)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: ものなら① expresses hypothetical possibility after potential form verbs: "if (A) were possible, (B)". It combines もの (thing) + なら (hypothetical form of だ) to literally mean "if (A) were a thing". This pattern suggests that while (A) is not thought to be possible, if it were, then (B) would follow. Often used with てみる (try to) or てみたい (want to try).

**Structures tested**: 3 tests covering various potential verb forms
1. Verb[できる] + ものなら: できるものなら、捕まえてみろ (12-19)
2. Verb[potential られる] + ものなら: 直せるものなら直したい (3-10)
3. もし + Verb[potential] + ものなら: もし行けるものならケニヤに行ってみたい (2-9)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 688-719), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 3072-3113)

**Key notes**:
- Comes after potential form verbs (できる, or られる/れる potential forms)
- Tokenizes as: もの (名詞/非自立/一般) + なら (助動詞, base='だ', 仮定形)
- Uses TokenMatcher::Any to match the preceding potential verb
- Priority 4 makes it rank higher than the general N3 もの・もん pattern (priority 1)
- Often appears with てみる/てみたい or もし for emphasis

**Tokenization observations**:
- もの: surface="もの", base="もの", pos=名詞/非自立/一般
- なら: surface="なら", base="だ", pos=助動詞, features[5]="仮定形"
- Preceded by potential verbs in 基本形 (dictionary form)

**Pattern ranges**: Include potential verb + ものなら
- できるものなら: potential verb (できる) + ものなら
- 直せるものなら: られる potential form (直せる) + ものなら
- 行けるものなら: れる potential form (行ける) + ものなら

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: 活かす (to make good use of, to leverage)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: 活かす (or variant kanji 生かす) is a verb meaning "to make good use of" or "to leverage". When paired with を, it shows that something is being utilized effectively. Common translations include "to make use of", "to take advantage of", "to capitalize on". Often used with adverbs of amount or quality like 十分に (adequately), 有効に (effectively), 最大限に (to the fullest).

**Structures tested**: 4 tests covering various conjugation forms
1. Noun + を + 活かす (basic): 機能を十分に活かす (13-16)
2. Noun + を + 活かした + Noun (attributive): くせ毛を活かした髪型 (6-10)
3. Noun + を + 生かす (variant kanji): 失敗を次に生かして (7-10)
4. Noun + を + 活かした (with adverb): 旨味を活かした料理 (10-14)

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 818-831), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 3115-3167)

**Key notes**:
- Matches both 活かす and 生かす (same meaning, different kanji)
- Simple single-token matcher checking verb base_form
- Works for all conjugation forms (活かす, 活かした, 活かし, etc.)
- **Important**: Potential form 活かせる tokenizes as '活' (noun) + 'かせる' (verb), making it undetectable by this pattern
- Pattern range includes the verb token and may extend to following auxiliaries

**Tokenization observations**:
- 活かす: surface="活かす", base="活かす", pos=動詞/自立, 五段・サ行, 基本形
- 生かす: surface="生かす", base="生かす", pos=動詞/自立, 五段・サ行
- 活かし: surface="活かし", base="活かす", pos=動詞/自立, 連用形
- 活かせる (potential): surface='活' (名詞) + surface='かせる' (動詞) - SPLIT TOKENIZATION

**Pattern ranges**: Include the verb token in any conjugation
- Basic form: 活かす (13-16)
- 連用形 + た: 活かした (6-10, includes た auxiliary)
- 生かす variant: 生かし (7-10)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: っこない (there is no chance of / impossible)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: っこない is a combination of the suffix っこ and the い-Adjective ない. When attached to the conjunctive form (stem) of verbs, it creates new い-Adjectives that express the impossibility of (A). Common translations include 'there is no chance of (A)', or 'doing (A) is impossible'.

**Structures tested**: 4 comprehensive tests covering various tokenization patterns
1. Verb[stem]+っこない (できっこない): きっこない (22-27) - "there's no chance you can"
2. Verb[stem]+っこない (勝てっこない): てっこない (16-21) - "there's no chance I can beat him"
3. Verb[stem]+っこない (買えっこない): 買えっこない (17-23) - "not be able to buy"
4. Verb[stem]+っこない (past): 読めっこなかった (14-22) - "there was no way I could read"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 608-670), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 2977-3026)

**Key notes**:
- Complex tokenization: っこ tokenizes as two verb tokens rather than a suffix
  - っ tokenizes as 動詞/非自立 (base_form='く')
  - こ tokenizes as 動詞/非自立 (base_form='くる', 未然形)
- Two different tokenization patterns:
  1. Verb stem includes っ: きっ (連用タ接続), てっ (連用タ接続)
  2. Verb stem separate from っ: 買え (連用形) + っ (動詞/非自立)
- Matcher handles both patterns with Optional(っ) matcher
- Past tense forms (っこなかった) work automatically via NaiAuxMatcher

**Tokenization observations**:
- **Pattern 1** (verb+っ compound): Verb[連用タ接続 ending in っ] + こ (動詞/未然形) + ない (助動詞)
  - Example: きっ (base='きる', 連用タ接続) + こ + ない
- **Pattern 2** (split tokenization): Verb[連用形] + っ (base='く', 動詞/非自立) + こ (base='くる', 動詞/非自立, 未然形) + ない (助動詞)
  - Example: 買え (base='買える', 連用形) + っ + こ + ない
- Matcher uses VerbOrVerbWithKkoMatcher to match either pattern
- Optional KkuMatcher handles standalone っ token when not included in verb

**Pattern ranges**: Include full verb stem through ない/なかった
- Standard: verb+っこない (5-8 characters depending on verb)
- Past: verb+っこなかった (adds 2 characters for past marker)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: にほかならない (nothing but, simply)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: に他（ほか）ならない is a grammar pattern used with nouns to indicate that something is 'definitely (A)', or 'nothing but (A)'. Other common translations are 'none other than (A)', or 'simply (A)'. This expression is often used to emphasize statements, reasons, or causes. Being a combination of the noun 他（ほか） 'other' and the negated form of the う-Verb なる 'to become', it literally states that (A) will not become anything other than what it is.

**Structures tested**: 4 comprehensive tests covering all structure variants
1. Noun + にほかならない (standard): だからにほかならない (23-30) - "simply because she is a good person"
2. Noun + にほかならぬ (formal): のおかげにほかならぬ (26-32) - "simply because of coach Okamura"
3. Noun + にほかなりません (polite): 迷惑にほかなりません (18-26) - "nothing but a nuisance"
4. Reason emphasis: だからにほかならぬ (21-27) - "simply because our advertisements are memorable"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 538-606), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 2926-2975)

**Key notes**:
- Two different tokenization patterns exist:
  1. Compound verb: に (格助詞) + ほかなら (動詞/自立, base='ほかなる', 未然形) + ない/ぬ (助動詞)
  2. Split tokens (polite): に (副詞化) + ほか (名詞/副詞可能) + なり (動詞/連用形) + ませ + ん
- にほかならぬ is a formal written variant using classical negative auxiliary ぬ
- Pattern emphasizes that something is undoubtedly true or is the only accurate description

**Tokenization observations**:
- **Standard form**: に (助詞/格助詞/一般) + ほかなら (動詞, base_form='ほかなる', 未然形) + ない (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ)
- **Formal form**: に (助詞/格助詞/一般) + ほかなら (動詞, base_form='ほかなる', 未然形) + ぬ (助動詞, 特殊・ヌ)
- **Polite form**: に (助詞/副詞化) + ほか (名詞/副詞可能) + なり (動詞, base_form='なる', 連用形) + ませ (助動詞, 未然形) + ん (助動詞)
- The matcher handles both compound verb tokenization and split tokenization with a Wildcard for polite auxiliaries

**Pattern ranges**: Include full construction from に through final negative form
- Standard: にほかならない (7 characters)
- Formal: にほかならぬ (6 characters)
- Polite: にほかなりません (8 characters)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ようがない・ようもない (there is no way to / impossible to)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: 様（よう）がない or 様（よう）もない is an expression used to highlight that there is no 'method' or 'manner' in which to do (A). It can be interpreted as 'there is no way to (A)', or 'it's impossible to (A)'. This phrase attaches to the conjunctive form (連用形/stem) of verbs.

**Structures tested**: 6 comprehensive tests covering all structure variants
1. Verb[stem] + よう + が + ない: 行きようがない (17-24) - "there is no way to go"
2. する Verb + の + しよう + が + ありません (polite): のしようがありません (23-33) - "there is no way to contact"
3. Verb[stem] + よう + も + ない: 直しようもない (13-20) - "there is no way to fix"
4. する Verb + の + しよう + も + ない: のしようもない (14-21) - "there is no way to deal with"
5. Verb[stem] + よう + が + ありません (polite): 調べようがありません (9-19) - "there is no way to investigate"
6. Verb[stem] + よう + が + なかった (past): 逃げようがなかった (7-16) - "there was no way to escape"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 484-536), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 2837-2924)

**Key notes**:
- Pattern can use either が or も particle (both mean the same)
- よう tokenizes as 名詞/接尾/一般 (noun suffix) for regular verbs
- しよう tokenizes as 名詞/一般 (general noun) for suru verbs
- Negative forms: ない (adjective/auxiliary) or ありません (polite negative of ある)
- Past forms work automatically (なかった matches base_form="ない")

**Tokenization observations**:
- **Regular verbs**: Verb[連用形] + よう (名詞/接尾/一般)
- **する verbs**: Noun + の (助詞/連体化) + しよう (名詞/一般)
- **Particles**: が or も (both 助詞)
- **Negative**: ない (形容詞/自立 or 助動詞) OR ある (動詞/自立 for ありません)

**Test note**: Initially included a test with volitional form "信用しよう" (しよ + う as verb), but this is different from the noun しよう. Replaced with past tense test to avoid confusion between volitional form and the noun meaning "way/manner".

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ずに済む (get away without doing / can avoid)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4 (moderate priority - detected and highlighted)

**Pattern**: ずに済む is a grammar construction used in Japanese to indicate that one can 'get away without doing (A)', or 'it's no problem without (A)'. This structure is a mix of ず (a literary auxiliary verb that attaches to verbs in the same way as ない and has the same meaning), に, and 済む (a う-Verb meaning 'to come to pass', or 'to end up'). Literally, it can be thought of as meaning 'it will work out without (A)'.

**Structures tested**: 6 comprehensive tests covering all structure variants
1. Verb[未然形] + ずに済む: やらずに済む (12-18) - "can get away without doing"
2. Verb[未然形] + ずに済みます (polite): 待たずに済みます (11-19) - "can get by without waiting"
3. Verb[未然形] + なくて済む: 支払わなくて済む (12-20) - "can get by without paying"
4. Verb[未然形] + ないで済む: 支払わないで済む (13-21) - "can get by without paying"
5. Verb[未然形] + ずに済んだ (past): 使わずに済んだ (22-29) - "got by without using"
6. Verb[未然形] + なくて済みました (polite past): 並ばなくて済みました (12-22) - "got by without waiting in line"

**Implementation**: Matcher in n2.rs (lines 1466-1536), tests in n2_patterns.rs (lines 2753-2835)

**Key notes**:
- Three variant structures all mean the same thing:
  1. Verb[未然形] + ず + に + 済む (classical negative ず)
  2. Verb[未然形] + なく + て + 済む (modern negative なくて)
  3. Verb[未然形] + ない + で + 済む (modern negative ないで)
- All conjugations of 済む are matched (済む, 済みます, 済んだ, 済みました, etc.)
- Used when (A) is something unpleasant or bothersome, highlighting relief that it can be avoided

**Tokenization observations**:
- **ず variant**: surface="ず", base="ぬ", pos=助動詞, features[5]="連用ニ接続"
- **なく variant**: surface="なく", base="ない", pos=助動詞, features[5]="連用テ接続"
- **ない variant**: surface="ない", base="ない", pos=助動詞, features[5]="連用デ接続"
- Particles: に/て/で (all 助詞)
- 済む: base_form="済む", pos=動詞/自立

**Pattern ranges**: Include verb + negative auxiliary + particle + 済む (and any conjugation)
- Standard: やらずに済む, 支払わなくて済む, 支払わないで済む
- Polite: 待たずに済みます, 並ばなくて済みました
- Past: 使わずに済んだ

**Implementation challenge**: Initial attempt with らしい after 済む caused range extension issue. Changed test sentence to avoid auxiliary verb immediately following 済む for cleaner pattern boundary detection.
