### ✅ Pattern Completed: わけではない (it's not the case that / doesn't mean that)

**JLPT Level**: N3
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 3

**Pattern**: わけではない is the negative form of わけだ. It combines わけ (noun) with ではない (the conjugated form of the auxiliary verb だ + adverbial particle は + い-adjective ない). The pattern means "it doesn't mean that (A)", "it's not the case that (A)", or more literally "it is not necessarily so that it's (A)" / "so it's not (A)". わけ can be followed by the word before わけ in attributive form.

**Structures tested**: 6 comprehensive tests covering all major variants
1. Verb + わけではない: 日本語を話せるわけではないです (It's not the case that I can speak Japanese) - chars 7-13
2. い-Adjective + わけではない: 私の家は広いわけではない (It's not necessarily that my house is big) - chars 6-12
3. な-Adjective + な + わけではない: 仕事が大変なわけではない (It's not that my job is difficult) - chars 6-12
4. Noun + の + わけではない: 熱のわけではない (It's not necessarily that I have a fever) - chars 2-8
5. わけじゃない (casual): 映画を監督して有名になったわけじゃない (Didn't become famous because he directed) - chars 24-30
6. わけではありません (polite): 嫌いというわけではありません (It's not that I dislike you) - chars 5-14

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: grammar-lib/src/matchers/n3.rs (wakedehanai, lines 3301-3364)
- Tests: grammar-lib/src/tests/n3_patterns.rs (wakedehanai_tests module, lines 11810-11896)
- Pattern: grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs (Wakedehanai, declared at line 3232)

**Tokenization patterns**:
```
Four-token matcher: わけ + (で|じゃ) + は? + (ない|あり)

わけ (required):
- わけ (名詞/非自立/一般) - dependent noun

で or じゃ (required):
- で (助動詞, base=だ, 連用形) - auxiliary verb connective form
- じゃ (助詞) - particle (casual form)

は (optional):
- は (助詞/係助詞) - topic/contrast particle
- Note: じゃ already contains じゃ = で + は, so は is optional after じゃ

ない or あり (required):
- ない (助動詞 or 形容詞) - negative auxiliary or adjective
- あり (動詞, base=ある) - start of ありません (polite negative)
```

**Key implementation points**:
1. Four-token matcher with optional は: わけ + (で|じゃ) + Optional(は) + (ない|あり)
2. WakeMatcher checks surface == "わけ", POS == 名詞/非自立
3. DeJyaMatcher matches EITHER で (auxiliary) OR じゃ (particle)
4. WaMatcher (optional) matches は as topic particle
5. NaiAriMatcher matches EITHER ない (plain negative) OR あり (start of ありません)
6. The pattern matcher automatically extends あり to include the full ありません
7. Handles both ではない and じゃない forms
8. Priority set to 3 (moderate) as per patterns.rs

**Pattern ranges**: Include full negative construction
- Verb: "わけではない" (chars 7-13)
- い-Adj: "わけではない" (chars 6-12)
- な-Adj: "わけではない" (chars 6-12)
- Noun: "わけではない" (chars 2-8)
- Casual: "わけじゃない" (chars 24-30)
- Polite: "わけではありません" (chars 5-14) - auto-extends to full ありません

**Test Quality**:
- All 6 tests include assert_has_pattern and assert_pattern_range
- No print_debug in final committed tests
- Realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples
- Cover all major structural variants: verb, i-adj, na-adj, noun, casual (じゃ), polite (ありません)
- Correct character ranges verified for all variants

**Linguistic Note**: The grammar data explains that わけではない matches the explanatory meaning of "so" in English that often replaces "because" and "since". However, わけ also matches the emphatic nuance of "so", as in "it's SO not (A)". The nuance "it is not necessarily so that it's (A)" is closer to the actual meaning than simple "it's not that (A)".

**Related patterns**:
- わけだ (N3) - "so / no wonder / that's why" (affirmative form)
- わけがない (N3) - "there's no way that" (impossibility - stronger negation)
- じゃない (N5) - generic negative construction

### ✅ Pattern Completed: ような気がする (have a feeling that / kinda feel like)

**JLPT Level**: N3
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 1 (low priority, not highlighted in overlay)

**Pattern**: ような気がする adds uncertainty to 気がする through the auxiliary verb よう. It expresses "to have a funny feeling that (A)" or "to kinda have a feeling that (A)". The pattern specifically requires ような to be present - plain 気がする without ような is handled by the separate "がする" pattern.

**Structures tested**: 5 comprehensive tests covering major variants
1. Verb + ような + 気がする: 出来るような気がする (3-13)
2. い-Adj + ような + 気がする: 危ないような気がする (7-17)
3. な-Adj + な + ような + 気がする: 元気なような気がする (11-21)
4. Noun + の + ような + 気がする: 警察のような気がする (6-16)
5. Polite form - Verb + ような + 気がします: できるような気がします (7-18)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: grammar-lib/src/matchers/n3.rs (younakigasuru, lines 4689-4758)
- Tests: grammar-lib/src/tests/n3_patterns.rs (youna_ki_ga_suru_tests module, lines 11646-11731)
- Pattern: grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs (Younakigasuru)

**Tokenization patterns**:
```
ような:
- よう (名詞/非自立/助動詞語幹)
- な (助動詞, base=だ, 体言接続)

気:
- 気 (名詞/非自立/一般) - dependent noun (in context)
- 気 (名詞/一般) - regular noun (when alone)

が + する:
- が (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- する (動詞, base=する, 基本形) - plain form
- し + ます (動詞, 連用形 + 助動詞) - polite form
```

**Key implementation points**:
1. Pattern requires ような to be present (not optional) - 気がする alone is a separate pattern
2. Wildcard stops before よう or 気 (dependent noun) to prevent overconsumption
3. Six-token matcher: Wildcard + よう + な + 気 + が + する
4. Matches both plain (する) and polite (します) forms
5. Pattern priority: 1 (detected but not highlighted in overlay)
6. Custom matchers for よう, な, 気, and が ensure correct tokenization

**Pattern ranges**: Include content word + ような + 気がする
- Verb: 出来る + ような気がする
- い-Adj: 危ない + ような気がする
- な-Adj: 元気 + な + ような気がする (includes the な before ような)
- Noun: 警察 + の + ような気がする (includes の)

**Test Quality**:
- All 5 tests include assert_has_pattern and custom range verification
- No print_debug in committed tests
- Realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples
- Cover all major structural variants: verb, i-adj, na-adj, noun, polite
- Custom range checking using .any() to handle multiple wildcard matches

**Implementation challenge solved**:
Initially tried Optional matchers for ような, but discovered that match_remaining_pattern_with_pos doesn't handle Optional correctly after Wildcard. Solved by making ような required, which is linguistically appropriate since 気がする alone is a different pattern with different nuance.

**Related patterns**:
- がする (N3) - handles 気がする without ような
- ように・ような (N4) - the auxiliary verb ような itself
- ようだ (N4) - another use of よう

## Session 350 - 2025-12-14

### ✅ Session Summary
**Status**: COMPLETE - Successfully completed 1 N3 pattern
**Patterns completed**: 1 (もの・もん)
**Tests added**: 8 (all passing)
**Total tests**: 2229 passing, 1 ignored (was 2222 passing, 1 ignored - net +7 tests)

### ✅ Pattern Completed: もの・もん (because / 'cause - sentence-ending particle)

**JLPT Level**: N3
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 3

**Pattern**: もの is a common noun meaning "thing" that can also function as a sentence-ending particle. When used as a particle, it adds emotion to a statement and presents it as a reason, similar to "because" or "'cause" in English. The literal meaning is "it's an (A) thing!". もの is quite casual and should be avoided in formal situations. It can be shortened to もん for a more casual/childish tone.

**Structures tested**: 8 comprehensive tests covering major variants
1. Verb + もの: だって、あなたはいつも嘘をつくもの → つくもの (13-17)
2. な-Adj + だもの: これはあなたにあげられない。大切だもの → 大切だもの (14-19)
3. Noun + だもの: すぐ泣くに決まってるじゃん、まだこどもだもの → こどもだもの (16-22)
4. Verb + んだもの (emphasized): だって、暑くて寝れないんだもの → ないんだもの (9-15)
5. Noun + なんだもの (emphasized): この子は失敗するに決まってるでしょう、まだ新人なんだもの → なんだもの (23-28)
6. Noun + だもん (casual): もうお腹いっぱいだもん → いっぱいだもん (4-11)
7. Verb + もん (casual): いいよ、お前とはもう遊ばないもん！ → ないもん (12-16)
8. Verb negative + んだもの: 暑くて寝れないんだもの → ないんだもの (5-11)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher: grammar-lib/src/matchers/n3.rs (mono_u30fb_mon, lines 1651-1762)
- Tests: grammar-lib/src/tests/n3_patterns.rs (mono_mon_tests module, lines 11471-11572)
- Pattern: grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs (MonoU30fbMon)

**Tokenization patterns**:
```
もの (noun):
- もの (名詞/非自立/一般) - dependent noun

もん (particle OR noun):
- もん (助詞/終助詞) - sentence-ending particle (after verbs/adjectives)
- もん (名詞/非自立/一般) - dependent noun (after だ)

ん (explanatory particle):
- ん (名詞/非自立/一般) - dependent noun (explanatory)

だ (copula):
- だ (助動詞, base=だ, 基本形)
- な (助動詞, base=だ, 体言接続) - for noun + な + ん patterns
```

**Key implementation points**:
1. Four-token matcher: ContentWord + Optional(ん) + Optional(だ) + MonoOrMon
2. ContentWordMatcher matches verbs (動詞), adjectives (形容詞), nouns (名詞), and auxiliary verbs (助動詞)
3. NParticleMatcher matches ん as explanatory particle (名詞/非自立/一般)
4. DaAuxMatcher matches だ or な as auxiliary (助動詞, base=だ)
5. MonoOrMonMatcher matches:
   - もの as dependent noun (名詞/非自立/一般)
   - もん as particle (助詞/終助詞)
   - もん as dependent noun (名詞/非自立/一般)
6. Pattern priority: 3 (moderate priority)

**Pattern ranges**: Include the content word + optional particles + もの/もん
- Verb + もの: `つくもの` (chars 13-17) - verb + もの
- Noun + だもの: `こどもだもの` (chars 16-22) - noun + だ + もの
- Verb + んだもの: `ないんだもの` (chars 5-11) - auxiliary + ん + だ + もの
- Noun + なんだもの: `なんだもの` (chars 23-28) - な + ん + だ + もの
- Noun + だもん: `いっぱいだもん` (chars 4-11) - noun + だ + もん
- Verb + もん: `ないもん` (chars 12-16) - auxiliary + もん

**Test Quality**:
- All 8 tests include assert_has_pattern and assert_pattern_range
- No print_debug in final committed tests
- Realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples
- Cover all major structural variants: もの/もん, with/without だ, with/without ん
- Correct character ranges verified for all variants

**Tokenization Notes**:
1. **もん has two tokenizations**: As a particle (助詞/終助詞) when directly after verbs/adjectives, or as a noun (名詞/非自立/一般) when after だ
2. **Pattern matches from auxiliary verbs**: In negative verb forms like `遊ばないもん`, the pattern matches from the auxiliary `ない` (助動詞) rather than from the verb stem `遊ば` (動詞). This is a tokenization limitation - ideally we'd capture the full verb phrase, but detecting where verb phrases start is complex.
3. **Content word scope**: The matcher includes auxiliary verbs (助動詞) in ContentWordMatcher to handle patterns like `ないんだもの` and `ないもん`, where ない is an auxiliary that can precede もの/もん.

**Related patterns**:
- ～んです・のです (N4) - explanatory ん pattern
- ものだ (N3) - different meaning ("should" / "naturally is")

### Session 368 - Completed 言うまでもない ② Pattern (N3)

**Date**: Current session

**Pattern**: 言うまでもない ② (sentence-initial form of "it goes without saying")

**Priority**: 3

**Pattern**: Sentence-initial expression meaning "it goes without saying" or "needless to say". Unlike は言うまでもない which comes after phrases, this tends to come at the beginning of sentences.

**Structures tested**: 5 tests covering all major variants
1. いうまでもないことだが + Phrase (full form with こと + だ + が): いうまでもないことだが (0-11)
2. いうまでもないが (mid-sentence): いうまでもないが (12-20)
3. いうまでもないけど (variant with けど): いうまでもないけど (0-9)
4. 言うまでもなく (single-token adverbial form): 言うまでもなく (2-9)
5. いうまでもないけれども (variant with けれども): いうまでもないけれども (0-11)

**Implementation**: Custom matcher handling two tokenization forms:
- Split form: いう (verb) + まで (particle) + も (particle) + ない (adjective) + optional(こと + だ) + optional(が/けど/けれども)
- Single token: 言うまでもなく (形容詞 with base form 言うまでもない)

The matcher uses a single initial custom matcher that accepts either form, followed by optional matchers for the remaining tokens of the split form.

**Test Quality**:
- All 5 tests include assert_has_pattern and assert_pattern_range
- No print_debug in final committed tests
- Realistic sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples
- Covers both hiragana (いう) and kanji (言う) forms
- Tests all major conjunction variants (が, けど, けれども)

**Tokenization Notes**:
- Hiragana form (いうまでもない): Splits into 4 tokens (いう + まで + も + ない)
- Kanji form with く ending (言うまでもなく): Single token (形容詞/自立) with base form 言うまでもない
- Optional elements (こと, だ, が/けど/etc) are properly handled with Optional matchers

**Technical Challenge**: Initially attempted to use TokenMatcher::Or (which doesn't exist), then realized I could use a single custom matcher that checks for both forms in its matches() method, with the remaining matchers as Optional.

## Session 369 - 2025-12-14

**Status**: ✅ Complete - Implemented 又〜も pattern (N3)

**Pattern**: 又〜も (moreover/additionally/furthermore)

**Priority**: 3

**Pattern Description**: Combination of the adverb また, a noun/phrase, and the particle も (or emphasis variants でも/ても). Expresses "moreover", "additionally", or "furthermore" when adding new information to a previous statement.

**Structures tested**: 4 tests covering all major variants
1. また、Noun phrase + も (with comma): また、ギターの音も (18-27)
2. また + Noun phrase + も (without comma): またトースターとしても (19-30)
3. また + Noun + でも (emphasis variant): また、地理の先生でも (14-24)
4. また + Adjective + ても (emphasis variant): また、古くても (11-18)

**Implementation**: Pattern matcher with wildcard for flexible noun/adjective phrases:
```rust
vec![
    Custom(MataMatcher),              // また as 接続詞
    Optional(CommaMatcher),            // Optional comma
    Wildcard { min: 0, max: 10 },     // Any tokens (noun phrases, adjectives, particles)
    Custom(MoParticleMatcher),         // も as 係助詞
]
```

The matcher captures the full range from また through the final も, handling both:
- Basic form: また + Noun + も
- Emphasis variants: また + Noun + でも / また + Adjective + ても

**Test Quality**:
- All 4 tests include assert_has_pattern and assert_pattern_range
- No print_debug in final committed tests
- Realistic sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples
- Covers both comma and no-comma variants
- Tests both basic も and emphasis variants (でも, ても)

**Tokenization Notes**:
- また tokenizes as 接続詞 (conjunction)
- Comma (、) is optional and tokenizes as 記号/読点
- も tokenizes as 助詞/係助詞
- でも = で (助詞/格助詞) + も (助詞/係助詞)
- ても = て (助詞/接続助詞) + も (助詞/係助詞)

The wildcard with max: 10 allows for complex noun phrases like "地理の先生" or "トースターとして" between また and も.

**Technical Notes**: The でも and ても variants are naturally captured because they end with the も particle (係助詞). The wildcard allows any intervening tokens, making the pattern flexible enough to handle various sentence structures while maintaining accuracy.

## Session 369 (continued) - 2025-12-14

**Status**: ✅ Complete - Implemented 第一 pattern (N3)

**Pattern**: 第一 (first of all/foremost/most important)

**Priority**: 3

**Pattern Description**: Expression that can be used as an adverb meaning "first of all" or as a noun meaning "foremost"/"most important". When used adverbially, it's often paired with に. As a noun, it's followed by の (connecting nouns) or copula だ/です (end of sentence).

**Structures tested**: 5 tests covering all major variants
1. だいいち + Phrase (adverbial, sentence-initial): だいいち (0-4)
2. だいいち + の + Noun (noun, "number one"): だいいち (6-10)
3. Phrase + だいいち + だ (noun, "most important"): だいいち (13-17)
4. まず + だいいち + に (emphatic adverbial): だいいち (2-6)
5. Phrase + 第一 + です (polite, kanji form): 第一 (7-9)

**Implementation**: Pattern matcher handling both hiragana and kanji tokenization:
```rust
vec![
    Custom(DaiichiMatcher),       // Matches だいいち (副詞) OR 第 (接頭詞)
    Optional(IchiMatcher),         // Matches 一 (名詞/数) - only for kanji form
]
```

**Tokenization Notes**:
- **Hiragana** (だいいち): Single token, 副詞/一般 (adverb)
- **Kanji** (第一): Two tokens - 第 (接頭詞/数接続, prefix) + 一 (名詞/数, number)
- The matcher checks for both forms: single token だいいち OR two-token sequence 第+一
- The 一 matcher is Optional because it's only present in the kanji form

**Test Quality**:
- All 5 tests include assert_has_pattern and assert_pattern_range
- No print_debug in final committed tests
- Realistic sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples
- Covers both hiragana (だいいち) and kanji (第一) forms
- Tests adverbial, noun, and copula usages

**Technical Challenge**: The pattern has two different tokenizations depending on whether it's written in hiragana or kanji. The solution uses a custom matcher that accepts either the single hiragana token OR the first kanji token (第), followed by an Optional matcher for the second kanji token (一).

## Session 370: Implemented 考えられない (kangaerarenai) - N3 Pattern

**Date**: Continuation of Japanese Grammar Pattern Library development

### Pattern Completed: 考えられない (kangaerarenai)

**JLPT Level**: N3  
**Pattern**: 考えられない (unthinkable/unimaginable)  
**Meaning**: Expresses that something is unthinkable, unimaginable, or inconceivable

**Structure Variants Tested**:
1. Noun + など + 考えられない (standard negative): 妻と離婚することなど考えられない
2. Noun + なんて + 考えられない (emphatic): 人に手を出すなんて私には考えられない
3. Phrase + とは + 考えられない (quotative): あんなに小さな人が相撲をやろうと思ったとは考えられない
4. Phrase + ことは + 考えられない (nominalized): 人違いだったことは考えられないか？
5. Noun + など + 考えられません (polite): 今の私にそんなことなど考えられません
6. Phrase + ことは + 考えられませんでした (polite past): 携帯で映画を見ることは考えられませんでした
7. 考えられなかった (past): 考えられなかったでしょう
8. Noun + は + 考えられない (without など): インターネットなしの生活は考えられない

**Implementation Details**:

The pattern matches the negative potential form of 考える (to consider):
- 考え (base_form=考える, 動詞, 未然形)
- られ (base_form=られる, 動詞/接尾, 未然形) - potential form suffix
- ない/ません (助動詞) - negative auxiliary
- Optional: た/でした (助動詞) - past auxiliary

**Matcher Structure**:
```rust
vec![
    Custom(KangaeMatcher),      // 考え (未然形 of 考える)
    Custom(RareMatcher),         // られ (potential suffix)
    Custom(NegAuxMatcher),       // ない or ます
    Optional(PastAuxMatcher),    // た or です (past forms)
]
```

**Key Implementation Challenges**:
1. **Multiple conjugations**: Had to handle ない, ません, なかった, ませんでした, なかったでしょう
2. **Precise range matching**: Initial implementation used `TokenMatcher::Any` which incorrectly matched trailing particles like か
3. **Solution**: Created specific matchers for negative auxiliary (ない/ます) and past auxiliary (た/です) to ensure precise boundaries

**Test Coverage**:
- 8 tests covering all standard and polite forms
- Tests include both assert_has_pattern and assert_pattern_range
- No print_debug in final committed tests
- Realistic subtitle-quality sentences
- All tests passing

**Pattern Ranges**:
The pattern range includes the full verb construction: 考えられない/ません/なかった/ませんでした
- Does NOT include preceding particles/context (など, なんて, ことは, とは)
- Does include optional past markers (た, でした, でしょう)

**Technical Notes**:
- This is a specific verb pattern, not a general conjugation pattern
- The pattern specifically detects 考える + potential + negative, not other verbs
- Could potentially be generalized to other verbs, but grammar data treats this as a specific construction

**Tokenization Observations**:
- 考えられない: 3 tokens (考え + られ + ない)
- 考えられません: 3 tokens (考え + られ + ません)
- 考えられなかった: 4 tokens (考え + られ + なかっ + た)
- 考えられませんでした: 4 tokens (考え + られ + ません + でした)

## Session 376 Update: 連用形 Pattern Implementation

**Pattern completed**: 連用形 (renyoukei)
- **Meaning**: Conjunctive Form - Formal Clause Connector (formal writing pattern)
- **Structure**: Verb stem + comma + Phrase OR i-Adjective ku-form + comma + Phrase
- **JLPT Level**: N3
- **Priority**: 1 (low - common construction pattern, not highlighted in browser extension)
- **Tests added**: 6

### Implementation Details

**Matcher type**: Custom matchers for formal conjunctive form + comma requirement
- RenyoukeiMatcher: Matches verb in 連用形 (stem form) OR i-Adjective in 連用テ接続 (ku-form)
- CommaMatcher: Ensures pattern is followed by Japanese comma (、)
- Key distinction: Requires comma to differentiate from regular te-form connections

**Pattern structure**:
- Verb in 連用形 (stem form) + comma (、)
- i-Adjective in 連用テ接続 (ku-form) + comma (、)

**Test coverage**:
1. test_verb_stem_suru: 爆発をし、海に墜落した (exploded and crashed)
2. test_verb_stem_taberu: 朝食を食べ、出かける準備を (eat breakfast and get ready)
3. test_verb_stem_kuru: 友達が来、一緒に観光した (friend came and toured together)
4. test_i_adjective_ku_form: 北海道は寒く、景色が綺麗 (Hokkaido is cold and scenery is beautiful)
5. test_i_adjective_ku_form_tanoshii: 学校は楽しく、色々学べる (school is fun and can learn various things)
6. test_i_adjective_ku_form_negative: 部屋は広く、明るくない (room is spacious and not bright)

**Tokenization observations**:
- Verbs in 連用形 have features[5] = "連用形" (e.g., し from する, 食べ from 食べる, 来 from 来る)
- i-Adjectives in ku-form have features[5] = "連用テ接続" (e.g., 寒く, 楽しく, 広く)
- Japanese comma tokenizes as 記号/読点 (symbol/reading point)
- Pattern ranges include both the conjunctive form and the comma

## Session 376 Update (continued): 前者は・後者は Pattern Implementation

**Pattern completed**: 前者は・後者は (zenshaha kousha wa)
- **Meaning**: The Former / The Latter (used in comparisons like English)
- **Structure**: 前者は + (Comment) OR 後者は + (Comment)
- **JLPT Level**: N3
- **Priority**: 1 (low - common construction pattern)
- **Tests added**: 4

### Implementation Details

**Matcher type**: Custom matchers for zensha/kousha nouns + wa particle
- ZenshaKoushaMatcher: Matches 前者 (the former) OR 後者 (the latter) as nouns
- WaParticleMatcher: Ensures pattern is followed by は particle (topic marker)
- Pattern specifically targets contrastive usage with は particle

**Pattern structure**:
- 前者は (zensha wa) + comment about the former
- 後者は (kousha wa) + comment about the latter
- Usually appear in pairs to contrast two previously mentioned items

**Test coverage**:
1. test_zensha_wa: 前者は家に近いが学費が高い (the former is close to home but expensive)
2. test_kousha_wa: 前者は地下をゆっくりと走り、後者は速いスピードで (former slow, latter fast)
3. test_zensha_kousha_together: 前者は安くて古くて、後者は高くて新しい (both in same sentence)
4. test_kousha_wa_alone: 後者はより良い選択だと思います (the latter is a better choice)

**Tokenization observations**:
- 前者 tokenizes as 名詞/一般 with base_form "前者"
- 後者 tokenizes as 名詞/一般 with base_form "後者"
- は particle tokenizes as 助詞/係助詞 (topic marker)
- Pattern ranges include both noun and particle (e.g., 前者は, 後者は)

## Session 377 (continued): てでも Pattern Implementation

**Pattern completed**: てでも (tedemo)
- **Meaning**: Even if I have to (strong determination/willingness)
- **Structure**: Verb[て] + でも
- **JLPT Level**: N2
- **Priority**: 4
- **Tests added**: 3

### Implementation Details

**Matcher type**: Custom matchers for て/で + でも
- TeDeConjunctionMatcher: Matches て or で as 助詞/接続助詞
- DemoParticleMatcher: Matches でも as either 助詞/副助詞 OR 接続詞
- Pattern handles both て and で conjugation forms

**Pattern structure**:
- Verb[て] + でも (e.g., 払ってでも, してでも)
- Verb[で] + でも (e.g., 並んででも - で+でも variant)
- Expresses strong determination regardless of difficulty/consequences

**Test coverage**:
1. test_tedemo_strong_will: 高いお金を払ってでも手に入れたい (willingness to pay high price)
2. test_dedemo_variant: 寒い中並んででもあそこのラーメンを食べてみたい (で+でも variant)
3. test_tedemo_must_finish: このレポートは徹夜してでも終わらせなければいけない (must finish task)

**Tokenization observations**:
- て/で tokenizes as 助詞/接続助詞 (conjunction particle)
- でも can be either:
  - 助詞/副助詞 (adverbial particle) - after て
  - 接続詞 (conjunction) - after で
- Pattern ranges include: verb stem + て/で + でも

## Session 389 - 2025-12-15

### Patterns Implemented
1. **上** (ue) - "From the standpoint of" ✅
   - N2 pattern, 1 structure variant
   - 3 tests added (all passing)
   - Structure: Noun + 上(じょう)
   - Range: Full construction (Noun + suffix)

2. **上に** (ueni) - "In addition to / as well as" ✅
   - N2 pattern, 5 structure variants (4 standard + である variations)
   - 6 tests added (all passing)
   - Structures: 
     * Verb + 上(うえ)に
     * い-Adj + 上に
     * な-Adj + な + 上に
     * Noun + の + 上に
     * X + である + 上に (na-adj/noun + である)
   - Implementation uses custom matcher to validate preceding tokens
   - Range: Varies by structure (verb/adj/particle + うえ + に)

### Implementation Details

**Pattern: 上**
- Matcher checks for 上 with pos=名詞/接尾/副詞可能
- Follows noun (any type)
- Simple two-token pattern

**Pattern: 上に**  
- More complex with multiple structure variants
- Custom matcher `UePreMatcher` validates preceding token can be:
  * 動詞 (verb in basic form)
  * 形容詞 (い-adjective in basic form)
  * 助動詞 "な" or "ある" (for な-adj or である constructions)
  * 助詞 "の" with pos 連体化 (for noun constructions)
- Core pattern: [valid_pre_token] + うえ + (に)
- The に particle is optional
- Range includes the grammatical connector (verb/adj/particle) + うえ + に

**Patterns completed**: 2 patterns (上, 上に)
**Total tests added**: 9 tests (3 for 上, 6 for 上に)

✅ No regressions (all 2526 tests passing)

### Technical Notes

**上 vs 上に distinction**:
- 上(じょう): Formal suffix for "standpoint/perspective", follows nouns directly
- 上(うえ)に: Conjunction meaning "in addition to", has multiple attachment patterns

**Tokenization observations**:
- 上 as じょう: pos=名詞/接尾/副詞可能
- 上 as うえ: pos=名詞/非自立/副詞可能
- Different readings and POS tags distinguish these homographs

**Pattern matching approach for 上に**:
- Initially tried `TokenMatcher::Any` but was too broad
- Created specific `UePreMatcher` to validate only appropriate preceding tokens
- This ensures fewer false positives while covering all legitimate structures
- である variations work because "ある" is matched as auxiliary

## Session 418 - にかかわらず Pattern (N2) Implementation

**Date**: 2025-12-15
**Status**: COMPLETE

### Completed Work

Successfully implemented the **にかかわらず** (regardless of) pattern for N2 level.

**Tests**: 2733 tests passing (up from 2727, added 6 new tests)
**Files Modified**: 3 files (matchers/n2.rs, tests/n2_patterns.rs, feature_list.json)

### Implementation Summary

Created matcher function nikakawarazu() to handle the pattern:
- Core structure: [Content] + に + かかわら(未然形) + ず
- Used Wildcard matcher (min=1, max=10) with stop conditions
- Stop conditions: は particle (係助詞) and が particle (格助詞)
- Prevents wildcard from expanding too far back in the sentence

### Tests Created (6 tests in nikakawarazu_tests module)

All tests include assert_has_pattern and assert_pattern_range with correct character positions.

1. test_nikakawarazu_noun_with_comma - 性別や国籍にかかわらず
2. test_nikakawarazu_ka_ka - 降るか降らないかにかかわらず
3. test_nikakawarazu_adjective_antonym - 上手い下手にかかわらず
4. test_nikakawarazu_kadouka - いいかどうかにかかわらず
5. test_nikakawarazu_affirmative_negative - できるできないにかかわらず
6. test_nikakawarazu_simple_noun - 年齢にかかわらず

### Key Implementation Points

Wildcard with Stop Conditions:
- Initially used simple Wildcard which created overlapping matches
- Added stop conditions to prevent wildcard from going back past は/が particles
- This ensures the pattern starts at a meaningful grammatical boundary

Token Structure:
- に: 助詞/格助詞/一般
- かかわら: 動詞/自立, base=かかわる, form=未然形
- ず: 助動詞, base=ぬ

### Structure Variants Covered

All variants from grammar_points_data.json tested:
- standard[0]: Noun (A) + 、 + Noun (B) + にかかわらず
- standard[1]: A + か + B + か + にかかわらず
- standard[2]: Adj/Verb + (Antonym) Adj/Verb + にかかわらず
- standard[3]: A + かどうか + にかかわらず
- standard[4]: A + Aない + にかかわらず

### Technical Challenge

Challenge: Wildcard matcher created multiple overlapping matches with different starting points, and the pattern matcher selected the one with the most tokens (starting from position 0 instead of the grammatically correct position).

Solution: Added stop_conditions to the Wildcard to prevent it from expanding past は and が particles. This ensures the pattern starts at a meaningful boundary rather than including irrelevant preceding content.

### Next Steps

Continue with remaining N2 priority 4 patterns: 限り, を問わず, に越したことはない, ないではいられない, ねばならない

**Session outcome**: Successfully completed 1 new N2 pattern (にかかわらず) with full test coverage across 6 structure variants. Total of 6 new tests added, all passing, no regressions. All 2733 tests passing.

4. test_kagiri_noun_dearu_form - であるかぎり (Noun + である + かぎり)
5. test_kagiri_verb_ta_form - 聞いたかぎり (Verb[た] + かぎり)
6. test_kagiri_living_in_japan - 住むかぎり (Verb[ru] + かぎり, different context)

### Key Implementation Points

**Tokenization Pattern:**
- かぎり/限り: 名詞/非自立/副詞可能/*
- base_form: かぎり
- Appears after various verb forms and noun + である constructions

**Matcher Strategy:**
- Used TokenMatcher::Any to match any preceding token
- This allows the pattern to match after verbs in any form (る/ない/た/ている)
- Also matches after である (noun + である + かぎり)
- Checks both surface forms (かぎり and 限り) for completeness

### Structure Variants Covered

All variants from grammar_points_data.json tested:
- standard[0]: Verb[る] + 限り + Phrase
- standard[1]: Verb[ない] + 限り + Phrase
- standard[2]: Verb[ている] + 限り + Phrase
- standard[3]: Noun + である + 限り
- standard[4]: Verb[た] + 限り (note in data)

### Pattern Meanings

The pattern has three main meanings depending on context:
1. Limited to scope of A: "limited to (A), (B)"
2. Within scope of knowledge/senses: "as far as (A), (B)", "as long as (A), (B)"
3. Assuming A is true: "while (A) is the case, (B)", "assuming (A), (B)"

All meanings are unified by the concept of 限り limiting/constraining the scope of (B).

## Session 419 Update - を問わず Pattern (N2) Implementation

**Date**: 2025-12-15

### Implementation Summary - を問わず Pattern

Created matcher function wotowazu() to handle the pattern:
- Core structure: [Content] + を/は + とわ/問わ(未然形) + ず
- Used Wildcard matcher (min=1, max=10) with stop conditions
- Stop conditions: は particle (係助詞) and が particle (格助詞/係助詞)
- Prevents wildcard from expanding too far back in the sentence

### Tests Created (6 tests in wotowazu_tests module)

All tests include assert_has_pattern and assert_pattern_range with correct character positions.

1. test_wotowazu_simple_noun - 宗教や国籍をとわず
2. test_wotowazu_ka_ka_pattern - パソコンの上級者か初心者かをとわず
3. test_wotowazu_kadouka_pattern - このマンションを買うかどうかをとわず
4. test_wotowazu_day_night - 昼夜をとわず
5. test_wotowazu_with_ha_particle - どのブランドかはとわず (は instead of を)
6. test_wotowazu_affirmative_negative - 経験がある経験がないをとわず

### Key Implementation Points

**Tokenization Pattern:**
- を/は: 助詞
- とわ/問わ: 動詞/自立, base=とう, form=未然形 (五段・ワ行促音便)
- ず: 助動詞, base=ぬ, 連用ニ接続

**Matcher Strategy:**
- Used Wildcard to capture preceding content (min=1, max=10)
- Added stop conditions to prevent matching across は/が particles
- Supports both を and は variants (は for emphasis)
- Matches both hiragana (とわ) and kanji (問わ) forms

### Structure Variants Covered

All major variants from grammar_points_data.json tested:
- standard[0]: Noun + を問わず
- standard[1]: Noun (A) + 、 + Noun (B) + を問わず
- standard[2]: A + か + B + か + を問わず
- standard[3]: A (Antonym) A + を問わず
- standard[4]: A + かどうか + を問わず
- standard[5]: A + A[ない] + を問わず

### Pattern Comparison

**を問わず vs にかかわらず:**
- を問わず: "regardless of, whether or not" - formal, emphasizes scope
- にかかわらず: "regardless of, irrespective of" - formal, emphasizes independence
- Both use similar Wildcard + stop conditions approach
- Slightly different nuances: を問わず = "in any case for (A)", にかかわらず = "independent of (A)"

**Goal:** Implement the のももっともだ pattern (N2 priority 4)

### Pattern Completed: のももっともだ

**Pattern:** のももっともだ (it's only natural that, it's reasonable that)
**JLPT Level:** N2
**Priority:** 4
**Category:** Construction

### Implementation Details

**Tokenization Analysis:**
- もっとも can tokenize as either:
  - 副詞/一般 (adverb)
  - 名詞/形容動詞語幹 (na-adjective)
- Both forms must be handled

**Pattern Structure:**
```
[Word] + [Optional な] + [Optional の] + も/は + もっとも + だ/です
```

**Matcher Strategy:**
- Used `TokenMatcher::Any` for the preceding word (verb/adjective/noun)
- Optional な matcher for な-adjectives (特殊・ダ, 体言接続)
- Optional の matcher for nominalization (名詞/非自立)
- も/は particle matcher (助詞/係助詞)
- もっとも matcher handling both POS types
- だ/です auxiliary matcher

**Key Learning:**
- Initial attempt with `TokenMatcher::Wildcard` failed because wildcards with complex stop conditions can be too restrictive
- Switched to `TokenMatcher::Any` which matches any single token, allowing the pattern to be detected correctly
- The pattern matcher naturally selects the best (highest confidence) match when multiple matches exist

### Tests Implemented

Created 8 comprehensive tests covering all structure variants:
1. `test_verb_standard` - 怒るのももっともだ
2. `test_i_adjective_standard` - 眠いのももっともだ

4. `test_noun_standard` - 反対ももっともだ
5. `test_verb_with_noha` - 考えるのはもっともだ (は variant)
6. `test_noun_with_ha` - 心配はもっともだ (は variant)
7. `test_verb_polite` - 疲れるのももっともです
8. `test_na_adjective_polite` - 高価なのももっともです

### Pattern Completed: ないわけにはいかない

**Pattern:** ないわけにはいかない (cannot afford not to, must do)
**JLPT Level:** N2
**Priority:** 4
**Category:** Construction

### Implementation Details

**Pattern Structure:**
- Double negative construction extending わけにはいかない
- Creates "can't not do" meaning, emphasizing unavoidable circumstances
- Standard: Verb[未然形] + ない + わけにはいかない
- Polite: Verb[未然形] + ない + わけにはいきません

**Key Challenge:**
The verb いく appears in different conjugation forms:
- 未然形 (いか) before ない in standard form
- 連用形 (いき) before ませ in polite form

Solution: Created flexible IkuVerb matcher accepting both forms.

**Matcher Components:**
- NaiAuxiliary: matches ない auxiliary
- WakeMatcher: matches わけ noun
- IkuVerb: matches いく in 未然形 or 連用形
- EndingMatcher: matches ない/ませ/ん
- Optional second EndingMatcher for ん after ませ

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ぶりに (for the first time in [time period])

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: When used as a suffix, the voiced form of 振（ふ）り 'style', 振（ぶ）り will be used to indicate either the way something appears, or some specific lapse of time since the last time something happened. This lesson focuses on the 'lapse of time' meaning. Can be translated as 'for the first time in (A)', or 'haven't done (B) for (A) time'.

**Structures tested**: 6 test variants covering all forms
1. Noun + ぶり + だ: 五年ぶりだ (chars 7-12) - "first time in 5 years"
2. Noun + ぶり + です: 三年ぶりです (chars 7-13) - "first time in 3 years (polite)"
3. Noun + ぶりに: 一年ぶりに (chars 0-5) - "for the first time in 1 year"
4. Noun + ぶりの + Noun: 四年ぶりの寿司 (chars 0-5) - "sushi for the first time in 4 years"
5. Noun + ぶりに + Verb: 三年ぶりに風呂に入る (chars 0-5) - "bathe for the first time in 3 years"
6. Noun + ぶりの + Noun: 十年ぶりの優勝 (chars 5-10) - "victory for the first time in 10 years"

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: TimeNoun + Optional(TimeNoun) + ぶり + Optional(に/の/だ/です)
- Custom matchers created:
  - BuriMatcher: matches ぶり as 名詞/接尾/一般
  - TimeNounMatcher: matches any noun (numbers, counters, time words)
  - BuriFollowMatcher: matches に, の, だ, or です after ぶり
- Flexible matcher handles time expressions like 一年, 三年, 五年, 十年
- Accepts optional endings to support all structure variants
- Pattern includes full time expression + ぶり + optional ending

**Test sentences used**:
- "彼女との再会は五年ぶりだ" - reunion after 5 years
- "実家に帰るのは三年ぶりです" - returning home after 3 years
- "一年ぶりに五キロも走ったから明日は絶対に筋肉痛だ" - running after 1 year
- "四年ぶりの寿司だ！やっぱり日本の寿司はうまいな" - sushi after 4 years
- "三年ぶりに風呂に入る" - bathing after 3 years
- "高橋選手が十年ぶりの優勝！" - victory after 10 years

**Tokenization insights**:
- Time expressions tokenize as: Number (名詞/数) + Counter (名詞/接尾/助数詞)
- ぶり tokenizes as: 名詞/接尾/一般 (suffix noun)
- Followed by: に (格助詞), の (連体化), だ (助動詞), or です (助動詞)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: ては (if/when - conditional with negative expectation)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: When the particles て and は are combined, they form a regular composite structure that is used primarily to highlight repeating actions, or to make assessments or judgments about potential situations. This pattern expresses "if (A), (B)", "when (A), (B)", or "because of (A), (B)" with a negative expectation or comment.

**Structures tested**: 6 test variants covering all standard forms
1. Verb[て] + は: 回しては (chars 10-14) - "if you turn (with force)"
2. い-Adjective[て] + は: 長くては (chars 4-8) - "if it's long"
3. な-Adjective + では: 几帳面では (chars 14-19) - "if (you are) meticulous"
4. Noun + では: 格好では (chars 8-12) - "with that outfit"
5. Verb[て] + ちゃ: なくちゃ (chars 7-11) - "if you don't" (casual contraction)
6. Noun + じゃ: 人じゃ (chars 7-10) - "if that person" (casual contraction)

**Implementation details**:
- Matcher structure: PrecedingElement + て/で/ちゃ/じゃ + Optional(は)
- Custom matchers created:
  - PrecedingElementMatcher: matches verb, adjective, noun, or auxiliary
  - TeDeJaChaMatcher: matches て/で (particles or copula), ちゃ, or じゃ
  - HaParticleMatcher: matches は (topic/contrast particle)
- Handles both formal (て+は, で+は) and casual (ちゃ, じゃ) contractions
- Distinguishes between で as particle vs copula based on POS tags
- は is optional to allow for casual contractions ちゃ/じゃ

**Test sentences used**:
- "そのように力を入れて回してはネジがだめになってしまいますよ" - verb form
- "そんなに長くては鞄に入らないので、半分に折ってください" - i-adjective form
- "私は掃除が苦手なので、そんな几帳面では私と住むことはできないでしょう" - na-adjective form
- "そんな貧乏くさい格好ではあのレストランには入れないよ" - noun form
- "ご飯を全部食べなくちゃ大きくなれないよ" - ちゃ contraction
- "リーダーがあの人じゃ嫌です" - じゃ contraction

**Tokenization insights**:
- Verb/i-adj use: て/で (助詞/接続助詞) + は (助詞/係助詞)
- Na-adj/Noun use: で (助動詞 base=だ OR 助詞/格助詞) + は (助詞/係助詞)
- ちゃ: standalone particle (助詞/接続助詞)
- じゃ: standalone particle (助詞/副助詞)

### ✅ Pattern Implemented: 結果・の結果 (as a result of)

**JLPT Level**: N2
**Category**: Construction
**Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: 結果（けっか） 'result' is a noun in Japanese that is often seen paired with the past form of verbs, or with nouns followed by の. This grammar structure can be translated as 'as a result of (A), (B)', and simply indicates (A) as being the cause for how something ended up. Unlike あげく (which tends to be used only for negative results), 結果 may highlight both positive and negative results.

**Structures tested**: All 2 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json
1. Verb[た] + 結果: 運転した結果 (chars 6-12) - driving result
2. Verb[た] + 結果 (positive): 努力した結果 (chars 4-10) - effort result
3. Noun + の + 結果: 話し合いの結果 (chars 0-7) - discussion result
4. Noun + の + 結果 (investigation): 調査の結果 (chars 0-5) - investigation result

**Implementation details**:
- Created TWO separate matcher functions to handle both structures:
  - `kekka_u30fb_nokekka()`: Verb[た] + 結果 pattern
  - `kekka_u30fb_nokekka_noun()`: Noun + の + 結果 pattern
- Pattern declarations: Both use the same name "結果・の結果" but different enum variants
- Helper matchers created:
  - `kekka_noun_matcher()`: Matches 結果 (名詞/副詞可能)
  - `no_particle_rentaika_matcher()`: Matches の (助詞/連体化)
- Verb pattern uses: flexible_verb_form() + past_auxiliary() + kekka_noun_matcher()
- Noun pattern uses: noun_matcher() + no_particle_rentaika_matcher() + kekka_noun_matcher()

**Test count**: 4 tests (all passing)
- test_verb_ta_kekka: "運転した結果" - negative outcome
- test_verb_ta_kekka_positive: "努力した結果" - positive outcome
- test_noun_no_kekka: "話し合いの結果" - discussion result
- test_noun_no_kekka_investigation: "調査の結果" - investigation result

**Tokenization observations**:
- Verb pattern: Verb[連用形/連用タ接続] + た (助動詞) + 結果 (名詞/副詞可能)
- Noun pattern: Noun + の (助詞/連体化) + 結果 (名詞/副詞可能)
- 結果 always tokenizes as 名詞/副詞可能 (noun that can function as adverb)
- た tokenizes as 助動詞, base=た
- の tokenizes as 助詞/連体化 (nominalizing particle)

### Pattern Implemented: とも (even if, no matter if)

**JLPT Level**: N2, **Category**: Construction, **Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: とも is a conjunction particle that expresses concession, meaning even if (A), no matter if (A), or in spite of (A), still (B). It attaches to volitional forms of verbs, conjunctive forms of い-adjectives, and な-adjectives with であろう.

## Session 439 - 2025-12-15

### Pattern Implemented: 陸に～ない (ろくに～ない - barely, hardly, not properly)

**JLPT Level**: N2, **Category**: Construction, **Priority**: 1

**Pattern**: ろくに is an adverb meaning "satisfactorily" that appears with negative phrases to indicate something is "not done properly", "barely", "hardly", or "not enough". It can be written as ろくに, 陸に, or 碌に (ateji).

**Structures tested**: 5 variants
1. ろくに + Verb[potential] + ない: 話せない (chars 6-13)
2. ろくに + Verb[potential] + なかった: 寝れなかった (chars 3-12)
3. ろくに + Noun + も + Verb + ない: 仕事も出来ない (chars 4-14)
4. ろくに + Verb + ない: 食べない (chars 4-11)
5. ろくに + Verb[て] + いない: 会えていない (chars 10-23)

**Implementation**:
- RokuNiMatcher: matches ろくに/陸に/碌に as adverb (副詞/一般)
- Wildcard(0-10): allows intermediate tokens between adverb and negative
- NaiMatcher: matches ない/なかった as negative auxiliary (助動詞)

## Session 450 - 2025-01-XX

### Session Goal
Complete N2 patterns with test-first workflow, focusing on highest priority incomplete patterns.

### Patterns Implemented

#### Pattern 1/2: 抜く (to do completely, to pull through)

**JLPT Level**: N2, **Category**: Construction, **Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: 抜く expresses doing something completely or thoroughly, pulling through to the end despite difficulties. Literally "to extract", conveying completion or seeing something through to the bitter end.

**Structures tested**: 6 variants
1. Verb[stem] + ぬく (standard): 守りぬく (chars 7-11)
2. Verb[stem] + ぬきます (polite): 乗り越えぬきます (chars 5-13)
3. Verb[stem] + ぬかない (negative): やりぬかない (chars 12-18)
4. Verb[stem] + ぬいた (past): 考えぬいた (chars 0-5)
5. Verb[stem] + ぬかれる (passive): 見ぬか (chars 7-10)
6. Compound verb + ぬく (知りぬく): 知りぬい (chars 11-15)

**Implementation**:
- nuku(): Matches Verb連用形 + ぬく (動詞/非自立)
- nuku_compound(): Matches compound verbs ending in ぬく (like 知りぬく tokenized as single word)
- Two separate patterns registered: "抜く" and "抜く_compound"
- Handles both separate tokenization and compound verb forms

**Files modified**: 3 (n2.rs matchers, patterns.rs, n2_patterns.rs tests, feature_list.json)

#### Pattern 2/2: 抜きで (without, leaving out)

**JLPT Level**: N2, **Category**: Construction, **Priority**: 4

**Pattern**: 抜きで indicates doing something "without (A)" or "with the removal of (A)", leaving out or setting aside something that is regularly included.

**Structures tested**: 5 variants (4 detectable, 1 undetectable)
1. Noun + 抜きで: 冗談抜きで (chars 4-9)
2. Noun + 抜きで (compound noun): ら抜きで (chars 14-18)
3. Noun + 抜きに: 彼女抜きに (chars 0-5)
4. Noun + 抜きに (within 抜きにして): 者抜きに (chars 4-8)
5. Noun + 抜きの: ワサビ抜きの (chars 0-6)

**Undetectable variant**: Noun + は + 抜きとして
- Structure: 前置き + は + 抜き + として
- Particle は appears between noun and 抜き, breaking the pattern
- Would require more complex matcher allowing optional particles before 抜き

**Implementation**:
- nukide(): Matches Noun + 抜き(名詞) + (で/に/の/として)
- NukiNounMatcher: Matches 抜き as 名詞/一般
- NukideParticleMatcher: Matches で, に, の, or として particles
- Handles multiple particle variations after 抜き

**Files modified**: 3 (n2.rs matchers, n2_patterns.rs tests, feature_list.json)

### Session Summary

**Patterns completed**: 2 (both N2, priority 4)
- 抜く - 6 tests (including compound form variant)
- 抜きで - 5 tests (4 detectable variants)

**Stats**:
- Tests: 2970 passing (was 2959, net +11 tests)
- N2 patterns remaining: ~86 (down from ~88)
- Total patterns remaining: ~268

**Key achievements**:
- Implemented compound verb handling for 抜く (separate pattern for single-token compounds)
- Handled multiple particle variations for 抜きで (で/に/の/として)
- Documented undetectable variant with clear TODO explaining structural limitation
- Both patterns implemented with comprehensive test coverage
- No regressions (all tests passing)

### Technical Notes

**Compound verb tokenization**:
- Some verbs ending in ぬく tokenize as Verb連用形 + ぬく(動詞/非自立)
  Example: 守り + ぬく, やり + ぬか + ない
- Others tokenize as single compound verb (動詞/自立) with base_form ending in ぬく
  Example: 知りぬく (single token)
- Solution: Two separate patterns registered in patterns.rs

**Particle between noun and pattern marker**:
- Pattern: Noun + は + 抜きとして cannot be detected with current matcher design
- Current matcher expects: Noun directly before 抜き
- Would need Optional(particle_matcher()) before 抜き to handle this case
- Left as TODO for future enhancement if needed

- Codebase clean, feature_list.json updated
- Two N2 patterns completed with proper test coverage

  - にこたえた (past form)
- Implementation: Matches Noun + に + こたえる (verb) with optional て/た following
- All 3 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json covered

### Implementation Details

**Pattern structure**:
- Core pattern: Noun + に + こたえる (verb with base_form="こたえる")
- Optional following particle (て or た) to handle different forms
- KotaeruMatcher: Checks base_form == "こたえる" and pos == "動詞"
- TeOrTaMatcher: Matches て (助詞) or た (助動詞) when they follow the verb

**Tokenization observations**:
- にこたえて: `要望` + `に` + `こたえ` (連用形) + `て`
- にこたえる: `要望` + `に` + `こたえる` (基本形) - used when modifying nouns
- にこたえた: `声` + `に` + `こたえ` (連用形) + `た`

**Range handling**:
- When followed by て/た: Includes the particle (e.g., 要望にこたえて)
- When in 基本形: Just the verb (e.g., ニーズにこたえる)
- Pattern correctly captures the full grammatical construction in all cases
